            blockers.append("expected_actual_lane_missing")
    if workload_ok and sla_ok:
        for lane in actual:
            lane_contract = next((item for item in workload["expected_lanes"]
                if str(item["category_key"]) == lane["category_key"] and str(item["workflow_key"]) == lane["workflow_key"]), None)
            arrivals = [r for r in grouped[(lane["category_key"], lane["workflow_key"], "actual_e2e")] if r.get("event_kind") == "arrival"]
            completions = [r for r in grouped[(lane["category_key"], lane["workflow_key"], "actual_e2e")] if r.get("event_kind") == "completion"]
            sink_records = [r for r in grouped[(lane["category_key"], lane["workflow_key"], "actual_e2e")] if r.get("event_kind") in {"readback", "outbox"}]
            arrival_ids = {str(r["event_id"]) for r in arrivals if r.get("event_id")}
            completion_ids = {str(r["event_id"]) for r in completions if r.get("event_id")}
            if not arrival_ids or arrival_ids != completion_ids or len(arrivals) != len(completions):
                blockers.append("event_identity_mismatch")
            if len(completions) < sla["minimum_completed_events"]:
                blockers.append("completion_sample_insufficient")
            if lane["invocations"] == 0:
                blockers.append("invocation_evidence_missing")
            arrival_span = _time(arrivals[-1]) - _time(arrivals[0]) if len(arrivals) > 1 else 0
            if arrival_span < workload["burst_duration_seconds"] or len(arrivals) / arrival_span < workload["peak_arrivals_per_second"]:
                blockers.append("peak_load_not_attained")
            required = (lane["latency_ms"], lane["backlog"], lane["readback"], lane["outbox"], lane["drain_seconds"], lane["cost_micros"], lane["fanout"])
            if (any(value is None for value in required)
                    or lane["backlog"].get("slope_per_second") is None
                    or lane["backlog"].get("oldest_age_seconds_max") is None
                    or lane["latency_ms"]["sample_count"] != len(completions)
                    or lane["fanout"]["sample_count"] != len(completions)
                    or lane["readback"]["attempts"] < len(completions)
                    or lane["outbox"]["attempts"] < len(completions)
                    or any(not isinstance(record.get("success"), bool) for record in sink_records)
                    or lane["cost_micros"]["unknown_records"] > 0):
                blockers.append("required_e2e_metrics_missing")
                continue
            if lane_contract and lane_contract["input_kind"] == "case":
                distribution = workload["case_image_distribution"]
                if (lane["fanout"]["p50"] < distribution["p50"]
                        or lane["fanout"]["p95"] < distribution["p95"]
                        or lane["fanout"]["max"] < distribution["max"]):
                    blockers.append("case_image_distribution_not_attained")
            elif lane_contract and (lane["fanout"]["p50"] != 1 or lane["fanout"]["max"] != 1):
                blockers.append("single_image_fanout_invalid")
            checks = (
                (lane["latency_ms"]["p95"] > sla["p95_ms"], "p95_sla_failed"),
                (lane["latency_ms"]["p99"] > sla["p99_ms"], "p99_sla_failed"),
                (lane["errors"]["rate_limited_429"] is None or lane["errors"]["rate_limited_429"]["rate"] > sla["max_429_rate"], "rate_limit_sla_failed"),
                (lane["errors"]["timeouts"] is None or lane["errors"]["timeouts"]["rate"] > sla["max_timeout_rate"], "timeout_sla_failed"),
                (lane["retries"] is None or lane["retries"]["amplification"] > sla["max_retry_amplification"], "retry_amplification_sla_failed"),
                (lane["outbox"]["failure_rate"] > sla["max_outbox_failure_rate"], "outbox_sla_failed"),
                (lane["readback"]["failure_rate"] > sla["max_readback_failure_rate"], "readback_sla_failed"),
                (lane["backlog"]["slope_per_second"] > sla["max_backlog_slope_per_second"], "backlog_slope_sla_failed"),
                (lane["drain_seconds"]["max"] > sla["recovery_window_seconds"], "recovery_window_sla_failed"),
            )
            blockers.extend(code for failed, code in checks if failed)
    blockers = list(dict.fromkeys(blockers))
    return {"schema_version": 2, "sources": sources or [], "workload": workload, "sla": sla, "evidence_summary": counts, "lanes": lanes, "capacity_accepted": not blockers, "acceptance_blockers": blockers}


class _NoRedirect(HTTPRedirectHandler):
    def redirect_request(self, *_args: Any, **_kwargs: Any) -> None:
        return None


def execute_actual_ingress(event_file: Path, endpoint: str, budget: IngressBudget, *, confirmation: str,
        timeout_seconds: float = 10.0, auth_token: str | None = None, max_concurrency: int = 1,
        requests_per_second: float | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if confirmation != ACTUAL_CONFIRMATION:
        raise ValueError(f"confirmation must equal {ACTUAL_CONFIRMATION}")
    parsed = urlsplit(endpoint)
    if parsed.scheme not in {"http", "https"} or parsed.hostname not in {"127.0.0.1", "localhost", "::1"} or parsed.username or parsed.password:
        raise ValueError("actual ingress harness permits loopback endpoints only")
    if auth_token is not None and (not auth_token or "\r" in auth_token or "\n" in auth_token):
        raise ValueError("auth_token must be non-empty and contain no newlines")
    if not isinstance(max_concurrency, int) or isinstance(max_concurrency, bool) or not 1 <= max_concurrency <= 32:
        raise ValueError("max_concurrency must be between 1 and 32")
    if requests_per_second is not None and not _positive_number(requests_per_second):
        raise ValueError("requests_per_second must be finite and positive")
    events = load_jsonl(event_file)
    for index, event in enumerate(events, 1):
        if "estimated_cost_micros" not in event:
            raise ValueError(f"event {index} is missing estimated_cost_micros")
        estimate = event["estimated_cost_micros"]
        if not isinstance(estimate, int) or isinstance(estimate, bool) or estimate <= 0:
            raise ValueError(f"event {index} estimated_cost_micros must be a positive integer")
    declared_cost = 0
    selected: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for event in events:
        if len(selected) >= budget.max_requests:
            break
        event_cost = event["estimated_cost_micros"]
        if declared_cost + event_cost > budget.max_cost_micros:
            break
        selected.append(event)
        declared_cost += event_cost

    headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
    if auth_token is not None:
        headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {auth_token}"
    def send(event: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        body = json.dumps(event, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8")
        try:
            with build_opener(_NoRedirect()).open(Request(endpoint, data=body, headers=headers, method="POST"), timeout=timeout_seconds) as response:
                status = response.status
                response.read()
            return {"event_id": event.get("event_id"), "http_status": status}
        except Exception as exc:  # the result is evidence; callers decide acceptance
            return {"event_id": event.get("event_id"), "error_type": type(exc).__name__}

    futures = []
    interval = 1 / requests_per_second if requests_per_second is not None else 0
    next_submit = time.monotonic()
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=max_concurrency) as pool:
        for event in selected:
            delay = next_submit - time.monotonic()
            if delay > 0:
                time.sleep(delay)
            futures.append(pool.submit(send, event))
            next_submit = max(next_submit + interval, time.monotonic())
        observations = [future.result() for future in futures]
    attempted = len(selected)
    succeeded = sum(200 <= item.get("http_status", 0) < 300 for item in observations)
    reason = "request_budget_reached" if attempted >= budget.max_requests and len(events) > attempted else "cost_budget_reached" if attempted < len(events) else "event_file_exhausted"
    return {"endpoint": endpoint, "event_file": str(event_file.resolve()), "request_limit": budget.max_requests,
        "max_concurrency": max_concurrency, "requests_per_second": requests_per_second,
        "authentication": "bearer_from_environment" if auth_token is not None else "none",
        "redirects_followed": False, "cost_limit_micros": budget.max_cost_micros,
        "cost_limit_kind": "declared_event_estimates", "spending_limit_enforced": False,
        "requests_attempted": attempted, "requests_succeeded": succeeded,
        "declared_cost_micros": declared_cost, "stopped_reason": reason, "observations": observations}

===== FILE backend/app/incremental_capacity_observer.py =====
"""Read-only, bounded export of platform capacity evidence."""
from __future__ import annotations
from .workflow_snapshot_store import read_run_snapshot_document

import json
from contextlib import contextmanager
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import func, select, text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .models import (ContentIngressEvent, DownstreamPublishLedger, IncrementalShadowResult,
    ProductionRun, RuntimeOutboxEvent, WorkflowDefinition, WorkflowForeachItem,
    WorkflowModelInvocation)

MAX_WINDOW = timedelta(days=31)
MAX_ROWS = 100_000


@contextmanager
def readonly_observation(db):
    """Fresh observation transactions only; rollback, never leave a pool pragma."""
    if db.in_transaction():
        raise ValueError('observation requires a fresh transaction')
    dialect = db.get_bind().dialect.name
    sqlite_connection = None
    previous = None
    try:
        if dialect == 'sqlite':
            sqlite_connection = db.connection().connection.dbapi_connection
            previous = sqlite_connection.execute('PRAGMA query_only').fetchone()[0]
            sqlite_connection.execute('PRAGMA query_only=ON')
        elif dialect in {'mysql', 'postgresql'}:
            db.execute(text('SET TRANSACTION READ ONLY'))
        else:
            raise ValueError('unsupported read-only observation database')
        with db.no_autoflush:
            yield
    finally:
        try:
            if sqlite_connection is not None:
                sqlite_connection.execute(f'PRAGMA query_only={int(previous)}')
        finally:
            db.rollback()


def _utc(value: datetime) -> datetime:
    if value.tzinfo is None:
        return value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return value.astimezone(timezone.utc)


def _iso(value: datetime) -> str:
    return _utc(value).isoformat()


def _route(run: ProductionRun) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    try:
        return read_run_snapshot_document(run).get("runtime_context", {}).get("incremental_route")
    except (TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError):
        return None


def _has_frozen_single_asset(run: ProductionRun) -> bool:
    try:
        frozen_input = read_run_snapshot_document(run).get("runtime_context", {}).get("input_manifest", {})
    except (TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError):
        return False
    asset_id = frozen_input.get("asset_id") if isinstance(frozen_input, dict) else None
    return isinstance(asset_id, int) and not isinstance(asset_id, bool) and asset_id > 0


def _settlement_basis(invocation: WorkflowModelInvocation) -> str | None:
    try:
        evidence = json.loads(invocation.evidence_json or "{}")
    except (TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError):
        return None
    settlement = evidence.get("pricing_settlement") if isinstance(evidence, dict) else None
    return settlement.get("settlement_basis") if isinstance(settlement, dict) else None


def _e2e_completion_timing(ingress_at: datetime | None, run_finished_at: datetime | None,
        sink_verified_at: datetime | None) -> tuple[datetime, float] | None:
    if ingress_at is None or run_finished_at is None or sink_verified_at is None:
        return None
    start = _utc(ingress_at)
    if _utc(sink_verified_at) < start or _utc(run_finished_at) < start:
        return None
    end = max(_utc(run_finished_at), _utc(sink_verified_at))
    latency_ms = (end - start).total_seconds() * 1000
    return (end, latency_ms) if latency_ms >= 0 else None


def _paid_call_retry_flags(invocations: list[WorkflowModelInvocation]) -> list[bool]:
    seen: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()
    flags: list[bool] = []
    for invocation in invocations:
        identity = (invocation.node_instance_id, invocation.invocation_key)
        flags.append(identity in seen)
        seen.add(identity)
    return flags


def observe_capacity_records(db: Session, *, category_key: str, workflow_definition_id: int,
        since: datetime, until: datetime, max_rows: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if since.tzinfo is None or since.utcoffset() is None or until.tzinfo is None or until.utcoffset() is None:
        raise ValueError("since and until must include a timezone")
    since, until = _utc(since), _utc(until)
    if until <= since:
        raise ValueError("until must be after since")
    if until - since > MAX_WINDOW:
        raise ValueError("observation window must not exceed 31 days")
    if not isinstance(max_rows, int) or isinstance(max_rows, bool) or not 1 <= max_rows <= MAX_ROWS:
        raise ValueError(f"max_rows must be between 1 and {MAX_ROWS}")
    if not category_key or workflow_definition_id <= 0:
        raise ValueError("category_key and workflow_definition_id are required")

    definition = db.get(WorkflowDefinition, workflow_definition_id)
    workflow_key = definition.workflow_key if definition else f"definition-{workflow_definition_id}"
    runs = list(db.scalars(select(ProductionRun).where(
        ProductionRun.category_key == category_key,
        ProductionRun.workflow_definition_id == workflow_definition_id,
        ProductionRun.source_type == "content_ingress_event",
        ProductionRun.created_at >= since - MAX_WINDOW,
        ProductionRun.created_at < until,
    ).order_by(ProductionRun.created_at, ProductionRun.id).limit(max_rows + 1)))
    query_truncated = len(runs) > max_rows
    runs = runs[:max_rows]
    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    emitted_arrivals: set[tuple[int, str]] = set()

    for run in runs:
        event = db.get(ContentIngressEvent, run.source_id) if run.source_id else None
        if event is None:
            continue
        evidence_kind = "actual_e2e" if run.model_execution_mode == "real" else "synthetic"
        common = {"evidence_kind": evidence_kind, "category_key": category_key,
            "workflow_key": workflow_key, "event_id": event.event_id,
            "observation_source": "platform_database", "source_run_key": run.run_key,
            "model_execution_mode": run.model_execution_mode}
        arrival_key = (event.id, evidence_kind)
        if arrival_key not in emitted_arrivals:
            records.append({**common, "observed_at": _iso(event.received_at), "event_kind": "arrival"})
            emitted_arrivals.add(arrival_key)

        invocations = list(db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation).where(
            WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id == run.id).order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.created_at, WorkflowModelInvocation.id)))
        for invocation, is_retry in zip(invocations, _paid_call_retry_flags(invocations), strict=True):
            records.append({**common, "observed_at": _iso(invocation.created_at), "event_kind": "invocation",
                "invocation_id": invocation.id, "invocation_status": invocation.status})
            if is_retry:
                records.append({**common, "observed_at": _iso(invocation.created_at), "event_kind": "retry"})
            error_kind = "429" if invocation.upstream_status_code == 429 else "timeout" if "timeout" in (invocation.error_code or "").lower() else None
            if error_kind:
                records.append({**common, "observed_at": _iso(invocation.finished_at or invocation.created_at), "event_kind": "error", "error_kind": error_kind})

        foreach_count = db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowForeachItem.id)).where(
            WorkflowForeachItem.production_run_id == run.id)) or 0
        route = _route(run)
        destination = str(route.get("destination")) if route else "legacy"
        shadow_result = db.scalar(select(IncrementalShadowResult).where(
            IncrementalShadowResult.run_key == run.run_key).order_by(IncrementalShadowResult.created_at.desc()).limit(1))
        shadow_verified = shadow_result is not None
        ledgers = list(db.scalars(select(DownstreamPublishLedger).where(
            DownstreamPublishLedger.origin_run_key == run.run_key).order_by(DownstreamPublishLedger.id)))
        formal_verified = False
        formal_verified_at: datetime | None = None
        for ledger in ledgers:
            outbox = db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, ledger.outbox_event_id)
            if outbox is None:
                continue
            receipt = json.loads(outbox.delivery_receipt_json or "{}")
            outbox_success = outbox.status == "delivered"
            readback_success = outbox_success and receipt.get("read_back_matched") is True and ledger.delivery_status == "delivered"
            observed = outbox.delivered_at or outbox.updated_at or outbox.created_at
            records.append({**common, "observed_at": _iso(observed), "event_kind": "outbox", "success": outbox_success})
            if receipt.get("read_back_matched") is not None:
                records.append({**common, "observed_at": _iso(observed), "event_kind": "readback", "success": readback_success})
            formal_verified = formal_verified or readback_success
            if readback_success:
                timestamps = [value for value in (outbox.delivered_at, ledger.delivered_at) if value is not None]
                if timestamps:
                    candidate = max(timestamps, key=_utc)
                    formal_verified_at = candidate if formal_verified_at is None or _utc(candidate) > _utc(formal_verified_at) else formal_verified_at

        sink_verified = shadow_verified if destination == "shadow" else formal_verified if destination == "formal" else False
        sink_verified_at = shadow_result.created_at if destination == "shadow" and shadow_result else formal_verified_at if destination == "formal" else None
        timing = _e2e_completion_timing(event.received_at, run.finished_at, sink_verified_at) if sink_verified else None
        frozen_sla = read_run_snapshot_document(run).get('runtime_context', {}).get('incremental_sla')
        if (isinstance(frozen_sla, dict) and frozen_sla.get('version') == 'incremental-sla-v1'
                and frozen_sla.get('source_event_id') == event.id
                and frozen_sla.get('service_class') in {'high', 'normal'}):
            if until > datetime.now(timezone.utc):
                raise ValueError('SLA snapshots cannot observe a future until')
            usable = (timing[0] if run.status == 'succeeded' and timing is not None
                      and timing[0] <= until else None)
            elapsed = ((usable or until) - _utc(event.received_at)).total_seconds()
            records.append({**common, 'event_kind': 'sla',
                'observed_at': _iso(until - timedelta(microseconds=1)), 'as_of': _iso(until),
                'service_class': frozen_sla['service_class'],
                'first_queued_at': _iso(event.received_at),
                'usable_result_at': _iso(usable) if usable else None,
                'sla_violated': frozen_sla['service_class'] == 'high' and elapsed > 18000,
                'elapsed_seconds': elapsed})
        if run.status == "succeeded" and timing is not None:
            completion_at, latency_ms = timing
            completion = {**common, "observed_at": _iso(completion_at), "event_kind": "completion",
                "latency_ms": latency_ms,
                "destination": destination, "local_shadow_verified": destination == "shadow" and shadow_verified}
            if foreach_count:
                completion["fanout"] = foreach_count
            elif _has_frozen_single_asset(run):
                completion["fanout"] = 1
            if invocations:
                verified = sum(item.actual_cost_micros for item in invocations
                    if item.status != "reserved" and _settlement_basis(item) != "conservative_full_input")
                conservative = sum((item.actual_cost_micros if item.status != "reserved" else item.estimated_cost_micros)
                    for item in invocations if item.status == "reserved" or _settlement_basis(item) == "conservative_full_input")
                if verified:
                    completion["cost_micros"] = verified
                if conservative:
                    completion["estimated_cost_micros"] = conservative
            records.append(completion)

    records = [row for row in records if since <= datetime.fromisoformat(row["observed_at"]) < until]
    records.sort(key=lambda row: (row["observed_at"], row["event_kind"], str(row.get("source_run_key", ""))))
    truncated = query_truncated or len(records) > max_rows
    records = records[:max_rows]
    if truncated:
        for record in records:
            record["observer_truncated"] = True
    return {"schema_version": 1, "source": {"kind": "platform_database", "read_only": True,
        "category_key": category_key, "workflow_definition_id": workflow_definition_id,
        "since": _iso(since), "until": _iso(until)}, "records": records, "truncated": truncated,
        "acceptance_warnings": ["observer_rows_truncated"] if truncated else [],
        "inventory_complete": False,
        "snapshot_limitations": ["backlog_slope_requires_sequential_observations", "drain_time_requires_recovery_observation",
            "31_day_lookback_and_scope_inventory_require_reconciliation"]}

===== FILE backend/app/incremental_correction.py =====
"""增量纠偏队列：100% 进队、先发后审、人工只覆盖有问题的那些。

和「今日人审」的区别是本质上的。人审是抽样：每天抽 100 条，没抽到的记为「未抽查」，
而且严重风险超容量时会暂停链路——它是阻断性的。这里不抽样也不阻断：

- 每一条增量结果都在列表里（100% 进队），运营自己挑有问题的改，没改的就保持 AI 结果；
- AI 结果在跑完时已经写了下游（review_state=auto），人工纠偏只是后面再发一版
  （revision+1、review_state=reviewed、trigger_kind=human_correction）原地覆盖同一行；
- 所以「没纠偏」不等于「人工通过」，只等于「运营没有提出异议」，这一点在列表里写明。

列表纠偏等级和进详情逐字段纠偏，最终效果一样：都会覆盖 AI 结果写下游。区别只是
详情页能改具体内容字段，列表只能改最终等级。
"""

from __future__ import annotations
from .workflow_snapshot_store import read_run_snapshot_text, prefetch_run_snapshot_blobs

import hashlib
import json
import os
from copy import deepcopy
from datetime import date, datetime, timezone
from collections.abc import Mapping
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import JSON, Select, case, cast, func, select, type_coerce, and_, or_, union_all
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, load_only

from .api_datetime_encoding import PLATFORM_TIMEZONE, platform_isoformat
from .audit import canonical_json
from .incremental_correction_models import IncrementalCorrectionCase
from .factory_classification import factory_class_name, unique_class_label
from .models import (
    Asset,
    DownstreamPublishLedger,
    EvaluationResult,
    ProductionRun,
    WorkflowVersion,
)

CORRECTION_STATES = ("uncorrected", "corrected")
#: 列表里能直接改的等级；具体某条素材可选哪些由它自己冻结的工作流决定。
KNOWN_LEVELS = ("L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5")
_DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE = 50
_MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 200
#: 一次批量改等级最多多少条：一页的量。再多就分页做，避免一次请求里上百次下游重发。
_MAX_BATCH_ITEMS = 200
#: 上游分类 id → 名字。上游（工厂）只给 class_id_1 数字，名字由荣栋那边给一份表，配在
#: Legacy display overrides are retained for unknown IDs only; authoritative
#: Factory mappings are keyed by resource type and class ID.
_UPSTREAM_CLASS_LABELS_ENV = "INCREMENTAL_UPSTREAM_CLASS_LABELS_JSON"


def upstream_class_labels() -> dict[str, str]:
    raw = os.environ.get(_UPSTREAM_CLASS_LABELS_ENV, "").strip()
    if not raw:
        return {}
    try:
        parsed = json.loads(raw)
    except ValueError:
        return {}
    if not isinstance(parsed, Mapping):
        return {}
    return {str(key): str(value) for key, value in parsed.items() if str(value).strip()}


def upstream_class_label(class_id: int | None, res_type: int | None = None) -> str:
    if class_id is None:
        return ""
    if res_type is not None:
        label = factory_class_name(res_type, class_id)
        return f"{'3D' if res_type == 1 else 'SU'} · {label}" if label else f"分类 {class_id}"
    return unique_class_label(class_id) or upstream_class_labels().get(str(class_id), f"分类 {class_id}")


def _platform_day_bounds(published_from: str | None, published_to: str | None) -> tuple[datetime | None, datetime | None]:
    """把运营输入的东八区日期（YYYY-MM-DD）换成 UTC 的 [start, end) 区间。

    两端都取当天 00:00（东八区）：选 9/20~9/21 覆盖的是 9/20 00:00 到 9/21 00:00。
    """

    def _parse(raw: str | None, *, field: str) -> date | None:
        if raw is None or not str(raw).strip():
            return None
        try:
            return date.fromisoformat(str(raw).strip())
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise IncrementalCorrectionError(
                "incremental_correction_date_invalid",
                f"{field} 必须是 YYYY-MM-DD",
                status_code=422,
            ) from exc

    start_day = _parse(published_from, field="published_from")
    end_day = _parse(published_to, field="published_to")
    if start_day and end_day and end_day < start_day:
        raise IncrementalCorrectionError(
            "incremental_correction_date_invalid", "结束日期不能早于开始日期", status_code=422,
        )
    start = (
        datetime.combine(start_day, datetime.min.time(), tzinfo=PLATFORM_TIMEZONE).astimezone(timezone.utc)
        if start_day else None
    )
    end = (
        # 2026-09-20 老大定的口径：选中的两个日期就是区间的两端，都取当天 00:00。
        # 选 9/20~9/21 就是 9/20 00:00 ~ 9/21 00:00，不再把结束日整天算进来。
        datetime.combine(end_day, datetime.min.time(), tzinfo=PLATFORM_TIMEZONE).astimezone(timezone.utc)
        if end_day else None
    )
    return start, end


class IncrementalCorrectionError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, *, status_code: int = 409) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


def _json_object(value: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        parsed = json.loads(value or "{}")
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return {}
    return parsed if isinstance(parsed, dict) else {}


def _json_list(value: Any) -> list[Any]:
    try:
        parsed = json.loads(value or "[]")
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return []
    return parsed if isinstance(parsed, list) else []


def _run_mode(run: ProductionRun) -> str:
    runtime = _json_object(read_run_snapshot_text(run)).get("runtime_context")
    return str(runtime.get("run_mode") or "") if isinstance(runtime, Mapping) else ""


def _manifest(run: ProductionRun) -> dict[str, Any]:
    workflow = _json_object(read_run_snapshot_text(run)).get("workflow")
    manifest = workflow.get("manifest") if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else None
    return dict(manifest) if isinstance(manifest, Mapping) else {}


def _downstream_node_id(manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str | None:
    for node in manifest.get("nodes") or []:
        if isinstance(node, Mapping) and node.get("kind") == "downstream_projection":
            node_id = node.get("node_instance_id")
            if isinstance(node_id, str) and node_id:
                return node_id
    return None


def selectable_levels(run: ProductionRun) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """这条素材的工作流允许哪些等级——不能让运营改出一个下游不认识的等级。"""

    from .workflow_short_circuit import enabled_levels_from_manifest

    levels = tuple(
        level for level in enabled_levels_from_manifest(_manifest(run))
        if level in KNOWN_LEVELS
    )
    return levels


# 灵感图的 L5 不是等级而是过滤（提交 reject，不发布）。增量是先发后审：纠到 L5 要下游下架，
# 从 L5 纠回 L1–L4 要下游补发布，这两个能力工厂侧还没上线（2026-09-29 向荣栋提的需求）。
# 上线并适配前，灵感图等级纠偏里凡是跨过 L5 的都锁住；适配完成后把这个开关置 1 即可放开。
INSPIRATION_L5_CORRECTION_ENV = "LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_L5_CORRECTION_ENABLED"


def level_lock(run: ProductionRun, current_level: str | None) -> tuple[frozenset[str], str | None]:
    """这条素材现在不能纠到哪些等级，以及为什么。"""

    if (run.category_key or "") != "inspiration_image" or os.environ.get(INSPIRATION_L5_CORRECTION_ENV, "").strip() == "1":
        return frozenset(), None
    if current_level == "L5":
        return frozenset(KNOWN_LEVELS), "L5（过滤）素材纠回通过需要下游补发布，工厂侧支持前暂不开放"
    return frozenset({"L5"}), "纠偏为 L5（过滤）需要下游下架，工厂侧支持前暂不开放"


def _lock_payload(run: ProductionRun, current_level: str | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    locked, reason = level_lock(run, current_level)
    return {"locked_levels": [level for level in KNOWN_LEVELS if level in locked], "level_lock_reason": reason}


def _asset_id(run: ProductionRun, result: EvaluationResult) -> int | None:
    value = result.asset_id
    if isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool) and value > 0:
        return value
    return None


def correction_state(result: EvaluationResult) -> str:
    return "corrected" if int(result.review_revision or 0) > 0 else "uncorrected"


def platform_moment(value: Any) -> str:
    """把留痕里的时间戳统一成东八区再出口。

    纠偏历史是历年多个写入方留下的：有的写 UTC（``...+00:00``），有的已经写成东八区。
    运营在同一行里看到的 ``created_at`` / ``published_at`` 都是东八区，唯独纠偏时间
    按原样透出，于是 UTC 那批在页面上早了 8 小时（2026-09-20 老大指出）。存储保持原样
    不动——那是既有留痕；只在出口换算。认不出来的原样返回，不编时间。
    """

    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
        return ""
    try:
        moment = datetime.fromisoformat(value.strip())
    except ValueError:
        return value
    if moment.tzinfo is None:
        # 没有时区的历史值按 UTC 解释：写入方都在服务器上，服务器跑的是 UTC。
        moment = moment.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return platform_isoformat(moment)


def _last_human_correction(result: EvaluationResult) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    for entry in reversed(_json_list(result.correction_history_json)):
        if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get("corrector"):
            return entry
    return None


def _publish_states(db: Session, run_keys: list[str]) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    """每条素材下游现在是哪一版：自动那一版，还是人工覆盖过的那一版。

    下游只留最新一行，平台侧按 revision 逐版留痕，所以「下游现在是什么」就是这条
    素材 revision 最大的那一行。一次查完整页，不按行查。
    """

    if not run_keys:
        return {}
    rows = db.execute(
        select(
            DownstreamPublishLedger.origin_run_key,
            DownstreamPublishLedger.revision,
            DownstreamPublishLedger.review_state,
            DownstreamPublishLedger.delivery_status,
            DownstreamPublishLedger.created_at,
        )
        .where(DownstreamPublishLedger.origin_run_key.in_(run_keys))
        .order_by(
            DownstreamPublishLedger.revision.asc(),
            DownstreamPublishLedger.id.asc(),
        )
    ).all()
    latest: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    first_published: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for run_key, revision, review_state, delivery_status, created_at in rows:
        # 「发布时间」= 这条素材第一次发到下游的时间（自动那一版）；纠偏重发不改它。
        first_published.setdefault(str(run_key), platform_isoformat(created_at) if created_at else None)
        latest[str(run_key)] = {
            "published": True,
            "published_at": first_published[str(run_key)],
            "revision": int(revision or 1),
            "review_state": str(review_state or "auto"),
            "delivery_status": str(delivery_status or ""),
        }
    return latest


_UNPUBLISHED = {
    "published": False,
    "published_at": None,
    "revision": None,
    "review_state": None,
    "delivery_status": None,
}


def _levels_payload(run: ProductionRun) -> list[str]:
    return list(selectable_levels(run))


def _incremental_run_ids(db: Session) -> Any:
    """Materialize disjoint indexed branches before joining result rows.

    An OR containing snapshot JSON lets MySQL start with every result and read
    large stock snapshots via primary-key lookups. UNION ALL prevents merging
    this derived table; only NULL legacy rows need their frozen JSON parsed.
    The populated run_mode column remains authoritative, including conflicts.
    """
    document = (
        cast(ProductionRun.snapshot_json, JSON)
        if db.get_bind().dialect.name == "postgresql"
        else type_coerce(ProductionRun.snapshot_json, JSON)
    )
    return union_all(
        select(ProductionRun.id).where(ProductionRun.run_mode == "incremental"),
        select(ProductionRun.id).where(
            ProductionRun.run_mode.is_(None),
            document["runtime_context"]["run_mode"].as_string() == "incremental",
        ),
    ).subquery("incremental_run_ids")


def _incremental_join(db: Session, statement: Select[Any]) -> Select[Any]:
    ids = _incremental_run_ids(db)
    return (statement.select_from(EvaluationResult)
            .join(ids, ids.c.id == EvaluationResult.production_run_id)
            .join(ProductionRun, ProductionRun.id == ids.c.id))


def _base_query(db: Session) -> Select[Any]:
    return _incremental_join(db, select(EvaluationResult, ProductionRun))


def _result_window(statement: Select[Any], *, limit: int, offset: int = 0) -> Select[Any]:
    # Sort/page only identifiers. Do not materialize full model responses or
    # frozen run snapshots for every candidate before applying LIMIT.
    page = (statement.with_only_columns(EvaluationResult.id, maintain_column_froms=True)
            .order_by(EvaluationResult.created_at.desc(), EvaluationResult.id.desc())
            .offset(offset).limit(limit).subquery("correction_page"))
    return (select(EvaluationResult, ProductionRun)
            .join(page, page.c.id == EvaluationResult.id)
            .join(ProductionRun, ProductionRun.id == EvaluationResult.production_run_id)
            .order_by(EvaluationResult.created_at.desc(), EvaluationResult.id.desc()))


def _filtered(
    statement: Select[Any],
    *,
    category_key: str | None,
    workflow_version_id: int | None,
    state: str | None,
    level: str | None,
    upstream_class_id: int | None = None,
    corrected_by: str | None = None,
    published_from: str | None = None,
    published_to: str | None = None,
    search: str | None = None,
) -> Select[Any]:
    if category_key:
        statement = statement.where(ProductionRun.category_key == category_key)
    if workflow_version_id is not None:
        statement = statement.where(ProductionRun.workflow_version_id == workflow_version_id)
    if state == "corrected":
        statement = statement.where(EvaluationResult.review_revision > 0)
    elif state == "uncorrected":
        statement = statement.where(EvaluationResult.review_revision == 0)
    if level:
        statement = statement.where(EvaluationResult.level == level)
    if upstream_class_id is not None:
        # 上游分类：工厂任务的 class_id_1（迁移 119 抄成列），不是平台自己的类目。
        statement = statement.where(ProductionRun.upstream_class_id == int(upstream_class_id))
    if corrected_by and corrected_by.strip():
        # 谁改过：纠偏记录表按 corrector 有索引；一条素材被同一人改过多次也只算命中一次。
        statement = statement.where(
            select(IncrementalCorrectionCase.id)
            .where(IncrementalCorrectionCase.evaluation_id == EvaluationResult.id)
            .where(IncrementalCorrectionCase.corrector == corrected_by.strip())
            .exists()
        )
    start, end = _platform_day_bounds(published_from, published_to)
    if start is not None or end is not None:
        # 发布时间 = 第一次发到下游（revision 1）的时间；按东八区自然日筛。
        window = (
            select(DownstreamPublishLedger.id)
            .where(DownstreamPublishLedger.origin_run_key == ProductionRun.run_key)
            .where(DownstreamPublishLedger.revision == 1)
        )
        if start is not None:
            window = window.where(DownstreamPublishLedger.created_at >= start)
        if end is not None:
            window = window.where(DownstreamPublishLedger.created_at < end)
        statement = statement.where(window.exists())
    if search and search.strip():
        statement = statement.where(_search_predicate(search.strip()))
    return statement


def _search_predicate(term: str) -> Any:
    """ID 溯源：一串数字同时对评测 id、运行 id、素材 id 和上游 task/img/ll id；否则按 run_key 精确找。"""

    if term.isdigit():
        number = int(term)
        return or_(
            EvaluationResult.id == number,
            ProductionRun.id == number,
            EvaluationResult.asset_id == number,
            ProductionRun.upstream_task_id == number,
            ProductionRun.upstream_img_id == number,
            ProductionRun.upstream_ll_id == number,
        )
    return ProductionRun.run_key == term


def list_incremental_corrections(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str | None = None,
    workflow_version_id: int | None = None,
    state: str | None = None,
    level: str | None = None,
    upstream_class_id: int | None = None,
    corrected_by: str | None = None,
    published_from: str | None = None,
    published_to: str | None = None,
    search: str | None = None,
    limit: int = _DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE,
    offset: int = 0,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """100% 队列的一页。不抽样，所以 total 就是这组筛选下的全部增量素材数。"""

    if state is not None and state not in CORRECTION_STATES:
        raise IncrementalCorrectionError(
            "incremental_correction_state_invalid",
            "纠偏状态只能是 uncorrected 或 corrected",
            status_code=422,
        )
    page_size = min(max(int(limit), 1), _MAX_PAGE_SIZE)
    offset = max(int(offset), 0)
    statement = _filtered(
        _base_query(db),
        category_key=category_key,
        workflow_version_id=workflow_version_id,
        state=state,
        level=level,
        upstream_class_id=upstream_class_id,
        corrected_by=corrected_by,
        published_from=published_from,
        published_to=published_to,
        search=search,
    )
    total = db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(
        statement.with_only_columns(EvaluationResult.id, maintain_column_froms=True).subquery()
    )) or 0
    rows = db.execute(
        _result_window(statement, limit=page_size, offset=offset).options(
            load_only(EvaluationResult.asset_id, EvaluationResult.level, EvaluationResult.score,
                      EvaluationResult.confidence, EvaluationResult.needs_review,
                      EvaluationResult.review_revision, EvaluationResult.correction_history_json,
                      EvaluationResult.created_at, raiseload=True),
            load_only(ProductionRun.run_key, ProductionRun.snapshot_json,
                      ProductionRun.snapshot_blob_id, ProductionRun.snapshot_hash,
                      ProductionRun.category_key, ProductionRun.workflow_version_id,
                      ProductionRun.upstream_class_id, ProductionRun.upstream_task_id,
                      ProductionRun.upstream_img_id, ProductionRun.upstream_ll_id, raiseload=True),
        )
    ).all()
    prefetch_run_snapshot_blobs(db, [run for _, run in rows])
    assets = {
        asset.id: asset
        for asset in db.scalars(
            select(Asset).where(Asset.id.in_([result.asset_id for result, _ in rows]))
        ).all()
    } if rows else {}
    published = _publish_states(db, [run.run_key for _, run in rows])
    return {
        "items": [
            _row_payload(
                result, run, assets.get(result.asset_id),
                published=published.get(run.run_key, _UNPUBLISHED),
            )
            for result, run in rows
        ],
        "total": int(total),
        "offset": offset,
        "limit": page_size,
        "has_more": offset + len(rows) < int(total),
    }


def _row_payload(
    result: EvaluationResult,
    run: ProductionRun,
    asset: Asset | None,
    *,
    published: Mapping[str, Any] = _UNPUBLISHED,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    last = _last_human_correction(result)
    manifest = _json_object(read_run_snapshot_text(run)).get("runtime_context", {}).get("input_manifest", {})
    manifest = manifest if isinstance(manifest, dict) else {}
    task = manifest.get("factory_task", {})
    res_type = task.get("res_type") if isinstance(task, dict) else None
    # 灵感图的工厂任务号不写在 run.upstream_task_id 上，而是冻结在 input_manifest.inspiration_task 里
    inspiration_task = manifest.get("inspiration_task")
    upstream_task_id = run.upstream_task_id
    if upstream_task_id is None and isinstance(inspiration_task, dict):
        candidate = inspiration_task.get("task_id")
        upstream_task_id = candidate if type(candidate) is int else None
    return {
        "evaluation_id": int(result.id),
        "production_run_id": int(run.id),
        "run_key": run.run_key,
        "asset_id": _asset_id(run, result),
        "asset_name": asset.original_name if asset is not None else "",
        "category_key": run.category_key or "",
        "workflow_version_id": int(run.workflow_version_id),
        "level": result.level,
        "score": result.score,
        "confidence": result.confidence,
        "needs_review": bool(result.needs_review),
        "selectable_levels": _levels_payload(run),
        **_lock_payload(run, result.level),
        "correction_state": correction_state(result),
        "review_revision": int(result.review_revision or 0),
        "corrected_by": str(last.get("corrector") or "") if last else "",
        "corrected_at": platform_moment(last.get("corrected_at")) if last else "",
        "correction_reason": str(last.get("reason") or "") if last else "",
        "downstream": dict(published),
        "published_at": published.get("published_at"),
        "upstream": {
            "class_id": run.upstream_class_id,
            "class_label": upstream_class_label(run.upstream_class_id, res_type),
            "res_type": res_type,
            "task_id": upstream_task_id,
            "img_id": run.upstream_img_id,
            "ll_id": run.upstream_ll_id,
        },
        "created_at": platform_isoformat(result.created_at) if result.created_at else None,
    }


def correction_filter_options(db: Session) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """筛选项和各自的条数——多人同时纠偏时要能先各自圈出一块再开工。"""

    rows = db.execute(
        _incremental_join(db, select(
            ProductionRun.category_key,
            ProductionRun.workflow_version_id,
            func.count(EvaluationResult.id),
            # case() 而不是 IIF/IF：同一句要同时跑在 MySQL 和测试用的 SQLite 上。
            func.sum(case((EvaluationResult.review_revision > 0, 1), else_=0)),
        ))
        .group_by(ProductionRun.category_key, ProductionRun.workflow_version_id)
    ).all()
    versions = {
        version.id: version
        for version in db.scalars(
            select(WorkflowVersion).where(
                WorkflowVersion.id.in_([row[1] for row in rows])
            )
        ).all()
    } if rows else {}
    categories: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    workflows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for category_key, version_id, total, corrected in rows:
        key = str(category_key or "")
        bucket = categories.setdefault(key, {"category_key": key, "total": 0, "corrected": 0})
        bucket["total"] += int(total or 0)
        bucket["corrected"] += int(corrected or 0)
        version = versions.get(version_id)
        workflows.append(
            {
                "workflow_version_id": int(version_id),
                "category_key": key,
                "version": getattr(version, "version", None),
                "total": int(total or 0),
                "corrected": int(corrected or 0),
            }
        )
    class_rows = db.execute(
        _incremental_join(db, select(
            ProductionRun.upstream_class_id,
            func.count(EvaluationResult.id),
            func.sum(case((EvaluationResult.review_revision > 0, 1), else_=0)),
        ))
        .where(ProductionRun.upstream_class_id.is_not(None))
        .group_by(ProductionRun.upstream_class_id)
    ).all()
    upstream_classes = sorted(
        (
            {
                "class_id": int(class_id),
                "label": upstream_class_label(int(class_id)),
                "total": int(total or 0),
                "corrected": int(corrected or 0),
            }
            for class_id, total, corrected in class_rows
        ),
        key=lambda item: (-item["total"], item["class_id"]),
    )
    corrector_rows = db.execute(
        select(
            IncrementalCorrectionCase.corrector,
            func.count(func.distinct(IncrementalCorrectionCase.evaluation_id)),
        )
        .where(IncrementalCorrectionCase.corrector != "")
        .group_by(IncrementalCorrectionCase.corrector)
    ).all()
    correctors = sorted(
        ({"corrector": str(name), "total": int(total or 0)} for name, total in corrector_rows),
        key=lambda item: (-item["total"], item["corrector"]),
    )
    return {
        "categories": sorted(categories.values(), key=lambda item: item["category_key"]),
        "workflows": sorted(
            workflows, key=lambda item: (item["category_key"], -item["total"])
        ),
        "states": [
            {"key": "uncorrected", "label": "未纠偏"},
            {"key": "corrected", "label": "已纠偏"},
        ],
        "upstream_classes": upstream_classes,
        "correctors": correctors,
    }


def batch_correct_incremental_level(
    db: Session,
    *,
    items: list[Mapping[str, Any]],
    level: str,
    reason: str,
    actor: str,
    idempotency_key: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """一页里勾选的素材一起改成同一个等级：逐条走 correct_incremental_level，逐条提交。

    每条各自成败：已经是这个等级的记为 unchanged，别人刚改过（expected_level 对不上）
    或没发过下游的记为 skipped 并带错误码，其余照常重发下游。不做全成全败——运营勾了
    50 条，不能因为第 37 条被人先改了就把前 36 条也回滚。
    """

    if not items or len(items) > _MAX_BATCH_ITEMS:
        raise IncrementalCorrectionError(
            "incremental_correction_batch_size_invalid",
            f"一次批量最多 {_MAX_BATCH_ITEMS} 条",
            status_code=422,
        )
    seen: set[int] = set()
    corrected: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    unchanged: list[int] = []
    skipped: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for item in items:
        evaluation_id = int(item["evaluation_id"])
        if evaluation_id in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(evaluation_id)
        try:
            row = correct_incremental_level(
                db,
                evaluation_id=evaluation_id,
                level=level,
                expected_level=item.get("expected_level"),
                reason=reason,
                actor=actor,
                idempotency_key=f"{idempotency_key}:{evaluation_id}",
            )
        except IncrementalCorrectionError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            if exc.code == "incremental_correction_level_unchanged":
                unchanged.append(evaluation_id)
            else:
                skipped.append({"evaluation_id": evaluation_id, "code": exc.code, "message": str(exc)})
            continue
        db.commit()
        corrected.append(row)
    return {
        "requested": len(seen),
        "corrected": corrected,
        "unchanged": unchanged,
        "skipped": skipped,
    }


def _load(db: Session, evaluation_id: int) -> tuple[EvaluationResult, ProductionRun]:
    result = db.scalar(
        select(EvaluationResult)
        .where(EvaluationResult.id == evaluation_id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if result is None:
        raise IncrementalCorrectionError(
            "incremental_correction_result_not_found", "评测结果不存在", status_code=404
        )
    if result.production_run_id is None:
        raise IncrementalCorrectionError(
            "incremental_correction_not_native",
            "该结果不是原生工作流产出，不能在增量纠偏页处理",
            status_code=409,
        )
    run = db.get(ProductionRun, result.production_run_id)
    if run is None:
        raise IncrementalCorrectionError(
            "incremental_correction_run_not_found", "运行记录不存在", status_code=404
        )
    if _run_mode(run) != "incremental":
        raise IncrementalCorrectionError(
            "incremental_correction_not_incremental",
            "只有增量运行的结果会写下游，存量回归请到存量回归页纠偏",
            status_code=409,
        )
    return result, run


def correct_incremental_level(
    db: Session,
    *,
    evaluation_id: int,
    level: str,
    expected_level: str | None,
    reason: str,
    actor: str,
    idempotency_key: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """列表直接改等级：记一条可审计的人工纠偏，并按这个等级重发一版下游。

    这不改 AI 自己算出来的那一版事实（``normalized_result`` 里的推导过程原样保留），
    而是在它之上记一个明确的人工覆盖，然后把覆盖后的结果重新投递。所以任何时候都能
    分清「模型算的是什么」和「人工改成了什么」。
    """

    reason = (reason or "").strip()
    if not reason:
        raise IncrementalCorrectionError(
            "incremental_correction_reason_required", "纠偏必须填写理由", status_code=422
        )
    if not (idempotency_key or "").strip():
        raise IncrementalCorrectionError(
            "incremental_correction_idempotency_required", "缺少幂等键", status_code=422
        )
    result, run = _load(db, evaluation_id)
    correction_key = "incremental-level:" + hashlib.sha256(
        canonical_json(
            {"evaluation_id": int(result.id), "idempotency_key": idempotency_key}
        ).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()[:40]
    # 幂等先答，早于一切冲突判断。一次网络重试送来的是一模一样的请求体——包括当时看到的
    # expected_level 和当时的目标等级。这条已经生效了，所以「等级被别人改过」和「等级没变化」
    # 两个判断都会命中，然后把一次成功的重试变成 409 或 422。先按幂等键认出它，原样把结果还回去。
    for entry in _json_list(result.correction_history_json):
        if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get("correction_key") == correction_key:
            if entry.get("new_value") != level:
                raise IncrementalCorrectionError(
                    "incremental_correction_idempotency_conflict",
                    "同一幂等键已用于其他等级",
                )
            return _correction_response(db, result, run, replayed=False)
    allowed = selectable_levels(run)
    if not allowed:
        raise IncrementalCorrectionError(
            "incremental_correction_levels_unavailable",
            "该工作流没有冻结可用等级，不能在列表改等级",
        )
    if level not in allowed:
        raise IncrementalCorrectionError(
            "incremental_correction_level_invalid",
            "新等级必须是该工作流启用的等级：" + "、".join(allowed),
            status_code=422,
        )
    current = result.level
    locked, lock_reason = level_lock(run, current)
    if level in locked:
        raise IncrementalCorrectionError("incremental_correction_level_locked", lock_reason or "该等级暂不开放纠偏")
    if expected_level is not None and expected_level != current:
        raise IncrementalCorrectionError(
            "incremental_correction_level_conflict",
            "这条素材的等级已经被其他人改过，请刷新后再看",
        )
    if level == current:
        raise IncrementalCorrectionError(
            "incremental_correction_level_unchanged",
            "新等级和当前等级相同，不需要纠偏",
            status_code=422,
        )

    _append_level_correction(
        result,
        correction_key=correction_key,
        previous_level=current,
        level=level,
        reason=reason,
        actor=actor,
    )
    record_correction_case(
        db, result=result, run=run, correction_key=correction_key, kind="level",
        ai_value=current, human_value=level, reason=reason, actor=actor,
    )
    republish_corrected_projection(
        db,
        run=run,
        result=result,
        level=level,
        previous_level=current,
        actor=actor,
        reason=reason,
        correction_event_id=correction_key,
    )
    return _correction_response(db, result, run, replayed=True)


def _append_level_correction(
    result: EvaluationResult,
    *,
    correction_key: str,
    previous_level: str | None,
    level: str,
    reason: str,
    actor: str,
) -> None:
    """记一条人工等级覆盖。

    不走 ``apply_node_correction``：那条路要求结果里带着 v3 冻结上下文
    (``scoring.v3_context``) 才能重算，而原生工作流的结果一条都没有——它的等级是整条
    DAG 归并出来的，不是 v3 规则图算的。所以这里直接记覆盖事实，不假装能重放。

    模型自己那一版留在 ``model_level``，人工值写进 ``level``，两者都能追溯。
    """

    history = _json_list(result.correction_history_json)
    history.append(
        {
            "schema_version": "incremental-level-correction-v1",
            "correction_key": correction_key,
            "node_type": "final_level",
            "node_path": "final_level",
            "old_value": previous_level,
            "new_value": level,
            "evidence": [],
            "reason": reason,
            "reason_codes": [],
            "corrector": actor,
            "corrected_at": platform_isoformat(datetime.now(timezone.utc)),
            # 等级是人工直接定的，不是重算出来的；这个标记让事后分得清。
            "downstream_recomputed": False,
            "source": "incremental_correction_list",
        }
    )
    result.correction_history_json = json.dumps(history, ensure_ascii=False)
    scoring = _json_object(result.scoring_json)
    scoring["level"] = level
    scoring["manual_final_level"] = level
    scoring["model_level"] = scoring.get("model_level", previous_level)
    result.scoring_json = json.dumps(scoring, ensure_ascii=False)
    normalized = _json_object(result.normalized_result_json)
    if normalized:
        normalized["model_level"] = normalized.get("model_level", normalized.get("level"))
        normalized["level"] = level
        result.normalized_result_json = json.dumps(normalized, ensure_ascii=False)
    result.level = level
    # 人工已经看过并给了结论，这条就不必再挂「待复核」。
    result.needs_review = False
    result.review_revision = int(result.review_revision or 0) + 1
    # 素材大宽表（ADR-0111）：等级覆盖后同事务刷新；失败只记日志。
    session = Session.object_session(result)
    if session is not None:
        from .wide_material import refresh_material

        refresh_material(session, result.asset_id, event_kind="corrected")


LEVEL_OVERRIDE_SCHEMA = "incremental-level-override-v1"


def apply_level_override_to_output(
    output: Mapping[str, Any], human_override: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """把人工等级覆盖套到下游节点的一份 output 上（列表改等级与字段纠偏重放共用）。"""

    level = human_override["level"]
    corrected = deepcopy(dict(output))
    corrected["final_level"] = level
    normalized = corrected.get("normalized_result")
    if isinstance(normalized, Mapping):
        # 模型自己的推导原样保留在 model_level，人工值放进 level，两者都能看到。
        corrected["normalized_result"] = dict(
            normalized, level=level, model_level=normalized.get("level")
        )
    corrected["human_override"] = deepcopy(dict(human_override))
    return corrected


def active_level_override(
    history: list[Any], outputs: Mapping[str, Any], node_ids: list[str]
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """这条评测当前是否有仍然生效的人工等级覆盖；有就返回投下游用的 human_override。

    以纠偏历史为准：最后一条列表改等级（node_type=final_level）之后，若没有一次
    本身就改等级的字段纠偏把它取代，它就仍然生效。投出去的那份 human_override
    若还在下游节点输出里，原样沿用，保证与已投递的一致。
    """

    latest: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None
    for entry in history:
        if not isinstance(entry, Mapping):
            continue
        if entry.get("node_type") == "final_level" and isinstance(entry.get("new_value"), str) and entry.get("new_value"):
            latest = entry
        elif entry.get("level_override_superseded"):
            latest = None
    if latest is None:
        return None
    for node_id in node_ids:
        node_result = outputs.get(node_id)
        output = node_result.get("output") if isinstance(node_result, Mapping) else None
        frozen = output.get("human_override") if isinstance(output, Mapping) else None
        if (
            isinstance(frozen, Mapping)
            and frozen.get("schema_version") == LEVEL_OVERRIDE_SCHEMA
            and frozen.get("level") == latest.get("new_value")
        ):
            return deepcopy(dict(frozen))
    return {
        "schema_version": LEVEL_OVERRIDE_SCHEMA,
        "field": "final_level",
        "previous_level": latest.get("old_value"),
        "level": latest["new_value"],
        "actor": latest.get("corrector"),
        "reason": latest.get("reason"),
        "correction_event_id": latest.get("correction_key"),
    }


def republish_corrected_projection(
    db: Session,
    *,
    run: ProductionRun,
    result: EvaluationResult,
    level: str,
    previous_level: str | None,
    actor: str,
    reason: str,
    correction_event_id: str,
) -> list[int]:
    """把人工改过的等级按 ADR-0064 重发一版（revision+1、reviewed）覆盖同一行。

    下游节点的输出是整条 DAG 归并出来的，等级不是某一个节点的输出，所以这里不重放
    DAG——重放只会把模型自己的等级算回来。改的是投递用的那一份：``final_level`` 换成
    人工等级，并附上 ``human_override`` 说明改自哪个等级、谁改的、为什么。模型推导过程
    留在 ``normalized_result`` 里原样不动。
    """

    from .downstream_publish_ledger import record_downstream_publish
    from .runtime_outbox import enqueue_runtime_outbox
    from .workflow_runtime import build_incremental_projection_outbox_request

    manifest = _manifest(run)
    node_id = _downstream_node_id(manifest)
    if node_id is None:
        raise IncrementalCorrectionError(
            "incremental_correction_no_downstream",
            "该工作流没有写入下游节点，纠偏无法覆盖下游",
        )
    outputs = _json_object(result.workflow_node_outputs_json)
    node_result = outputs.get(node_id)
    if not isinstance(node_result, Mapping) or node_result.get("status") != "succeeded":
        raise IncrementalCorrectionError(
            "incremental_correction_no_published_output",
            "这条素材还没有成功写过下游，纠偏没有可覆盖的目标",
        )
    output = node_result.get("output")
    if not isinstance(output, Mapping):
        raise IncrementalCorrectionError(
            "incremental_correction_no_published_output",
            "下游节点没有结构化输出，无法覆盖",
        )
    corrected = apply_level_override_to_output(
        output,
        {
            "schema_version": LEVEL_OVERRIDE_SCHEMA,
            "field": "final_level",
            "previous_level": previous_level,
            "level": level,
            "actor": actor,
            "reason": reason,
            "correction_event_id": correction_event_id,
        },
    )
    # 投出去的那一份必须和自动跑出来的那一份同层级：自动路径发的是整条
    # workflow-node-result-v1（投递器再从里面取 output），纠偏这里只换掉它的
    # output。早先这里直接发了剥掉外壳的 output，投递器取不到 output 就按契约
    # 错误死信，人工纠偏永远覆盖不到下游。
    corrected_node_result = dict(node_result, output=corrected)
    request = build_incremental_projection_outbox_request(
        db,
        run,
        step_key=node_id,
        output_manifest=corrected_node_result,
        review_state="reviewed",
        change={
            "trigger_kind": "human_correction",
            "actor": actor,
            "reason": reason,
            "correction_event_id": correction_event_id,
        },
    )
    if request is None:
        return []
    event = enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, request)
    record_downstream_publish(db, event=event, payload=request.payload)
    # 读模型跟着改，否则列表和真正投出去的那一版会对不上。
    outputs[node_id] = corrected_node_result
    result.workflow_node_outputs_json = json.dumps(outputs, ensure_ascii=False)
    db.flush()
    from .wide_material import refresh_material

    refresh_material(db, result.asset_id, event_kind="corrected")
    return [int(event.id)]


def _correction_response(
    db: Session,
    result: EvaluationResult,
    run: ProductionRun,
    *,
    replayed: bool,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    payload = _row_payload(
        result,
        run,
        db.get(Asset, result.asset_id),
        published=_publish_states(db, [run.run_key]).get(run.run_key, _UNPUBLISHED),
    )
    payload["republished"] = replayed
    return payload


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 详情纠偏：改具体内容字段。
#
# 列表只能改最终等级；要改「分类写错了」「标题不对」这类具体内容，得进详情按节点改。
# 这条路直接用既有的重放引擎 append_node_correction_and_replay——它改完节点会重跑
# 下游节点、重新归并结果，并按 revision+1/reviewed 重发下游。和列表改等级一样，
# 最终效果都是覆盖 AI 结果写下游。
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #

_CORRECTABLE_KINDS = frozenset({"prompt", "rule_module", "foreach_image"})
_MAX_LEAF_FIELDS = 200


def _leaf_fields(value: Any, prefix: str = "") -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """把节点输出摊平成可编辑的叶子字段。

    列表和数组整体给出，不再往里拆——半路改数组里的一项没有稳定身份，事后对不上。
    """

    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        fields: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for key in value:
            child = f"{prefix}.{key}" if prefix else str(key)
            fields.extend(_leaf_fields(value[key], child))
            if len(fields) >= _MAX_LEAF_FIELDS:
                return fields[:_MAX_LEAF_FIELDS]
        return fields
    return [{
        "field_path": prefix or "output",
        "value": deepcopy(value),
        "value_type": (
            "list" if isinstance(value, list)
            else "bool" if isinstance(value, bool)
            else "number" if isinstance(value, (int, float))
            else "text" if isinstance(value, str)
            else "null" if value is None
            else "other"
        ),
    }]


def incremental_correction_detail(db: Session, *, evaluation_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """一条素材的可纠偏节点和它本轮的真实输出。"""

    result, run = _load(db, evaluation_id)
    manifest = _manifest(run)
    outputs = _json_object(result.workflow_node_outputs_json)
    nodes: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for node in manifest.get("nodes") or []:
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping):
            continue
        node_id = str(node.get("node_instance_id") or "")
        kind = str(node.get("kind") or "")
        node_result = outputs.get(node_id)
        status = (
            str(node_result.get("status") or "")
            if isinstance(node_result, Mapping) else ""
        )
        output = node_result.get("output") if isinstance(node_result, Mapping) else None
        correctable = (
            kind in _CORRECTABLE_KINDS
            and status == "succeeded"
            and isinstance(output, Mapping)
        )
        nodes.append({
            "node_instance_id": node_id,
            "display_name": str(node.get("display_name") or node_id),
            "kind": kind,
            "status": status or "not_run",
            "correctable": correctable,
            # 说清楚为什么不能改，免得运营对着一个灰掉的字段猜。
            "read_only_reason": (
                "" if correctable
                else "本次没有执行这一步" if status in {"skipped", "", "not_run"}
                else "接口和流程控制节点只留运行证据，不能纠偏"
                if kind not in _CORRECTABLE_KINDS else "这一步没有结构化输出"
            ),
            "fields": _leaf_fields(output) if correctable else [],
        })
    history = [
        entry for entry in _json_list(result.correction_history_json)
        if isinstance(entry, dict)
    ]
    return {
        "evaluation_id": int(result.id),
        "production_run_id": int(run.id),
        "run_key": run.run_key,
        "asset_id": _asset_id(run, result),
        "category_key": run.category_key or "",
        "level": result.level,
        "score": result.score,
        "selectable_levels": _levels_payload(run),
        **_lock_payload(run, result.level),
        # 真实模型跑出来的结果，改完上游要重跑提示词是要花钱的；前端据此先问一句。
        "model_execution_mode": run.model_execution_mode,
        "nodes": nodes,
        "correction_history": history[-20:],
    }


def correct_incremental_field(
    db: Session,
    *,
    evaluation_id: int,
    node_instance_id: str,
    field_path: str,
    value: Any,
    reason: str,
    actor: str,
    idempotency_key: str,
    real_execution_confirmed: bool = False,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """改一个具体内容字段，然后重放下游并覆盖下游那一行。"""

    reason = (reason or "").strip()
    if not reason:
        raise IncrementalCorrectionError(
            "incremental_correction_reason_required", "纠偏必须填写理由", status_code=422
        )
    if not (idempotency_key or "").strip():
        raise IncrementalCorrectionError(
            "incremental_correction_idempotency_required", "缺少幂等键", status_code=422
        )
    result, run = _load(db, evaluation_id)
    from .workflow_correction import (
        WorkflowCorrectionError,
        append_node_correction_and_replay,
    )

    correction_key = "incremental-field:" + hashlib.sha256(
        canonical_json(
            {"evaluation_id": int(result.id), "idempotency_key": idempotency_key}
        ).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()[:40]
    try:
        replay = append_node_correction_and_replay(
            db,
            run_id=int(run.id),
            node_instance_id=node_instance_id,
            field_path=field_path,
            value=value,
            reason=reason,
            actor=actor,
            correction_key=correction_key,
            real_execution_confirmed=real_execution_confirmed,
        )
    except WorkflowCorrectionError as exc:
        raise IncrementalCorrectionError(
            exc.code, str(exc), status_code=getattr(exc, "status_code", 409)
        ) from exc
    record_correction_case(
        db, result=result, run=run, correction_key=correction_key, kind="field",
        ai_value=_field_value_before(result, node_instance_id, field_path),
        human_value=value, reason=reason, actor=actor,
        node_instance_id=node_instance_id, field_path=field_path,
    )
    # 重放会重算最终等级；把它同步回列表读的那几个字段，否则列表和详情会各说各话。
    level = replay.normalized_result.get("level")
    if isinstance(level, str) and level:
        result.level = level
    score = replay.normalized_result.get("score")
    if isinstance(score, (int, float)) and not isinstance(score, bool):
        result.score = float(score)
    result.review_revision = int(result.review_revision or 0) + 1
    db.flush()
    payload = _correction_response(db, result, run, replayed=True)
    payload["affected_nodes"] = list(replay.affected_nodes)
    payload["downstream_publish_status"] = replay.downstream_publish_status
    return payload


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 看板：最近 N 条增量素材里，人工把 AI 的等级改成了什么。
#
# 和存量回归那块看板的区别必须说在前面：存量比的是「AI 判的」对「基准集真值」，
# 每一条都有真值。这里没有真值——只有「运营改过的」和「运营没动的」。没动不等于
# 人工确认正确，只等于没人提出异议，所以下面的口径把两者彻底分开：
#   * 纠偏率按全窗口算（分母是 N）；
#   * 等级流向矩阵只统计被改过的那些（改动本身才是信息）；
#   * 每个 AI 等级的「被改率」= 这个等级被改走的条数 / 这个等级出现的条数，
#     这是唯一能直接指向「哪一级判得最不准」的数字。
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #

DASHBOARD_WINDOW = 1000
_MAX_DASHBOARD_WINDOW = 5000


def _ai_level(result_level: str | None, history: list[Any]) -> str | None:
    """这条素材 AI 原本判的等级。

    纠偏会把 ``EvaluationResult.level`` 覆盖成人工值，所以被改过的行要回到纠偏
    历史里取第一次改动的 ``old_value``——中间可能被改过多次，最早那次的旧值才是
    模型自己的结论。
    """

    for entry in history:
        if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get("node_type") == "final_level":
            old = entry.get("old_value")
            return old if isinstance(old, str) and old else None
    return result_level


def correction_dashboard(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str | None = None,
    workflow_version_id: int | None = None,
    window: int = DASHBOARD_WINDOW,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    size = min(max(int(window), 1), _MAX_DASHBOARD_WINDOW)
    statement = _filtered(
        _base_query(db),
        category_key=category_key,
        workflow_version_id=workflow_version_id,
        state=None,
        level=None,
    )
    snapshots = db.execute(_result_window(statement, limit=size).options(
        load_only(EvaluationResult.level, EvaluationResult.review_revision,
                  EvaluationResult.correction_history_json, raiseload=True),
        load_only(ProductionRun.snapshot_json, ProductionRun.snapshot_blob_id, ProductionRun.snapshot_hash,
                  raiseload=True),
    ).execution_options(yield_per=50))
    # Keep only small correction facts, not a thousand full frozen snapshots.
    # No database calls while the MySQL streaming cursor is open.
    rows: list[EvaluationResult] = []
    levels: list[str] = []
    compressed_runs: list[ProductionRun] = []
    try:
        for result, run in snapshots:
            rows.append(result)
            if not run.snapshot_json:
                compressed_runs.append(run)
                continue
            for level in selectable_levels(run):
                if level not in levels:
                    levels.append(level)
    finally:
        snapshots.close()
    if compressed_runs:
        prefetch_run_snapshot_blobs(db, compressed_runs)
        for run in compressed_runs:
            for level in selectable_levels(run):
                if level not in levels:
                    levels.append(level)
    levels = [level for level in KNOWN_LEVELS if level in levels] or list(KNOWN_LEVELS[:4])

    matrix = {row: {column: 0 for column in levels} for row in levels}
    ai_counts = {level: 0 for level in levels}
    human_counts = {level: 0 for level in levels}
    corrected_away = {level: 0 for level in levels}
    corrected_into = {level: 0 for level in levels}
    corrected = unlevelled = 0
    correctors: dict[str, int] = {}

    for result in rows:
        history = _json_list(result.correction_history_json)
        was_corrected = int(result.review_revision or 0) > 0
        human = result.level
        ai = _ai_level(human, history)
        if was_corrected:
            corrected += 1
            last = _last_human_correction(result)
            if last is not None and last.get("corrector"):
                key = str(last["corrector"])
                correctors[key] = correctors.get(key, 0) + 1
        if ai in ai_counts:
            ai_counts[ai] += 1
        if human in human_counts:
            human_counts[human] += 1
        if ai not in matrix or human not in matrix.get(ai, {}):
            # 知识图谱这类没有等级的类目，或历史上用过现已停用的等级。
            unlevelled += 1
            continue
        if was_corrected and ai != human:
            matrix[ai][human] += 1
            corrected_away[ai] += 1
            corrected_into[human] += 1

    total = len(rows)
    by_level = [
        {
            "level": level,
            "ai_count": ai_counts[level],
            "human_count": human_counts[level],
            "corrected_away": corrected_away[level],
            "corrected_into": corrected_into[level],
            "net": corrected_into[level] - corrected_away[level],
            # 这个等级 AI 判出来之后，有多少被人改走了。没出现过就是 None，不是 0。
            "changed_rate": (corrected_away[level] / ai_counts[level]) if ai_counts[level] else None,
        }
        for level in levels
    ]
    changed = sum(corrected_away.values())
    return {
        "schema_version": "incremental-correction-dashboard-v1",
        "window_requested": size,
        "window_actual": total,
        "category_key": category_key,
        "workflow_version_id": workflow_version_id,
        "levels": levels,
        "corrected": corrected,
        "uncorrected": total - corrected,
        # 分母是全窗口：没被纠偏的也在里面，它只是没被提出异议。
        "correction_rate": (corrected / total) if total else None,
        # 被纠偏且等级真的变了的条数。纠偏也可能只改内容不改等级。
        "level_changed": changed,
        "level_changed_rate": (changed / total) if total else None,
        "unlevelled": unlevelled,
        "confusion_matrix": matrix,
        "by_level": by_level,
        "correctors": sorted(
            ({"actor": actor, "count": count} for actor, count in correctors.items()),
            key=lambda item: (-item["count"], item["actor"]),
        )[:10],
    }


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 纠偏案例：每一次人工改动落一行，供「增量纠偏分析」挑选。
#
# 案例是「一次改动」而不是「一条素材」：同一条素材既改了等级又改了字段，就是两个
# 案例——它们指向的问题不同，应该能分别被选进不同的分析。
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


def _field_value_before(result: EvaluationResult, node_instance_id: str, field_path: str) -> Any:
    """字段纠偏前，这个节点该字段的值。取不到就是 None，不编造。"""

    node = _json_object(result.workflow_node_outputs_json).get(node_instance_id)
    output = node.get("output") if isinstance(node, Mapping) else None
    cursor: Any = output
    for part in field_path.split("."):
        if not isinstance(cursor, Mapping) or part not in cursor:
            return None
        cursor = cursor[part]
    return deepcopy(cursor)


def record_correction_case(
    db: Session,
    *,
    result: EvaluationResult,
    run: ProductionRun,
    correction_key: str,
    kind: str,
    ai_value: Any,
    human_value: Any,
    reason: str,
    actor: str,
    node_instance_id: str = "",
    field_path: str = "",
):
    """登记一个纠偏案例；同一个 correction_key 重复调用只返回已有那行。"""

    from .incremental_correction_models import IncrementalCorrectionCase

    existing = db.scalar(
        select(IncrementalCorrectionCase)
        .where(IncrementalCorrectionCase.correction_key == correction_key)
    )
    if existing is not None:
        return existing
    case = IncrementalCorrectionCase(
        correction_key=correction_key,
        evaluation_id=int(result.id),
        production_run_id=int(run.id),
        asset_id=_asset_id(run, result),
        category_key=run.category_key or "",
        workflow_version_id=int(run.workflow_version_id),
        kind=kind,
        node_instance_id=node_instance_id[:160],
        field_path=field_path[:300],
        ai_value_json=json.dumps(ai_value, ensure_ascii=False, default=str),
        human_value_json=json.dumps(human_value, ensure_ascii=False, default=str),
        reason=reason,
        corrector=actor[:80],
        corrected_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
    )
    db.add(case)
    db.flush()
    from .wide_material import refresh_material

    refresh_material(db, _asset_id(run, result), event_kind="corrected")
    return case

===== FILE backend/app/incremental_correction_analysis.py =====
"""增量纠偏分析：从一批人工纠偏案例出发，产出结论、候选新版本和人工确认面板。

分成两半，来源不同：

* **结论**复用存量那边的 ``deterministic_correction_report``。它是纯函数，只吃一个
  冻结快照，不碰数据库也不看 v3 配置，所以两条链路能说同一套话（方向分布、跨级距离、
  等级对、薄弱维度、受影响层）。
* **候选**必须自己做。存量的候选生成器产出的是 ``CategoryEvaluationV3Revision`` ＋
  A/B 两段提示词，绑死在 v3 规则图上；增量走的是原生工作流，结果里连 ``v3_context``
  都没有。这里产出的是一个新的 ``WorkflowVersion``。

模型能改什么，是这个模块最重要的约束：**只能改提示词正文和规则模块内容**。节点的
增删、连线、种类、连接器、模型快照、节点 id 一律从冻结 manifest 原样继承，模型提的
任何越界修改直接拒绝。最后再由 ``create_workflow_version`` 用和人工建版完全相同的
``validate_workflow_manifest`` 过一遍——模型写出来的东西和人写的受同一套检验。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
from copy import deepcopy
from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Protocol

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .incremental_correction_models import (
    IncrementalCorrectionAnalysis,
    IncrementalCorrectionCase,
)
from .models import (
    EvaluationResult,
    ModelConfig,
    ModelRegistryEntry,
    OptimizerConfig,

    PromptVersion,
    WorkflowDefinition,
    WorkflowVersion,
)

#: 模型唯一可以改的两处。键是节点 kind，值是该 kind 下允许出现的补丁字段。
EDITABLE_BY_KIND: dict[str, frozenset[str]] = {
    "prompt": frozenset({"system_prompt", "user_prompt"}),
    "rule_module": frozenset({"content"}),
}
MAX_CASES_PER_ANALYSIS = 200


class AnalysisError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, *, status_code: int = 409) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class NodeEdit:
    node_instance_id: str
    kind: str
    fields: dict[str, Any]


@dataclass
class GeneratedCandidate:
    edits: tuple[NodeEdit, ...]
    summary: str = ""
    rationale: str = ""
    trace: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)


class CandidateGenerator(Protocol):
    def generate(
        self,
        *,
        db: Session,
        analysis: IncrementalCorrectionAnalysis,
        manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
        report: Mapping[str, Any],
    ) -> GeneratedCandidate:
        ...


def _json(value: Any, fallback: Any) -> Any:
    try:
        parsed = json.loads(value if value is not None else "null")
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return fallback
    return parsed if parsed is not None else fallback


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 冻结输入：把案例翻译成 deterministic_correction_report 认识的形状
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


def build_input_snapshot(db: Session, cases: list[IncrementalCorrectionCase]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """把一批案例冻结成分析输入。

    报告只认「期望等级 / 模型等级」这一对，所以字段纠偏也要落到它影响的那条素材的
    等级上去看——一条素材可能既被改了字段又被改了等级，这里按素材归并，等级取该素材
    最终的人工值，字段改动进 ``node_corrections`` 作为证据。
    """

    by_evaluation: dict[int, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for case in cases:
        item = by_evaluation.setdefault(int(case.evaluation_id), {
            # 报告用 item_id 做样本身份。增量没有「基准集条目」这个东西，
            # 用评测结果 id 当身份是唯一稳定且能反查回素材的选择。
            "item_id": int(case.evaluation_id),
            "evaluation_id": int(case.evaluation_id),
            "asset_id": case.asset_id,
            "expected_level": None,
            "predicted_level": None,
            "correction_context": {
                "evaluation_id": int(case.evaluation_id),
                "node_corrections": [],
                "human_reviews": [],
                "affected_layers": [],
                "source": "incremental_correction",
                "severity": "normal",
                "reason": "",
            },
        })
        context = item["correction_context"]
        if case.kind == "level":
            item["predicted_level"] = _json(case.ai_value_json, None)
            item["expected_level"] = _json(case.human_value_json, None)
        else:
            context["node_corrections"].append({
                "key": case.field_path or case.node_instance_id,
                "node_instance_id": case.node_instance_id,
                "field_path": case.field_path,
                "old_value": _json(case.ai_value_json, None),
                "new_value": _json(case.human_value_json, None),
                "reason": case.reason,
                "source": "human",
            })
            if case.node_instance_id and case.node_instance_id not in context["affected_layers"]:
                context["affected_layers"].append(case.node_instance_id)
        if case.reason and not context["reason"]:
            context["reason"] = case.reason
        context["human_reviews"].append({
            "corrector": case.corrector,
            "reason": case.reason,
            "source": "human",
        })

    # 只改了字段、没改等级的素材：等级两端都取它当前的等级，报告会把它当成「等级没变
    # 但有节点纠偏」来处理，而不是凭空编一个跨级。
    for evaluation_id, item in by_evaluation.items():
        if item["expected_level"] is None or item["predicted_level"] is None:
            result = db.get(EvaluationResult, evaluation_id)
            level = getattr(result, "level", None)
            item["expected_level"] = item["expected_level"] or level
            item["predicted_level"] = item["predicted_level"] or level
        normalized = _json(getattr(db.get(EvaluationResult, evaluation_id), "normalized_result_json", None), {})
        if isinstance(normalized, Mapping):
            item["level_explanation"] = {
                "steps": normalized.get("steps", []),
                "track_key": normalized.get("track_key"),
                "hit_rules": normalized.get("hit_rules", []),
                "caps": normalized.get("caps", []),
            }
            item["correction_context"]["caps"] = normalized.get("caps", [])

    return {
        "schema_version": "incremental-correction-analysis-input-v1",
        "items": list(by_evaluation.values()),
    }


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 候选：把模型提的编辑套到冻结 manifest 上，越界一律拒绝
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


def _nodes_by_id(manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    return {
        str(node.get("node_instance_id")): dict(node)
        for node in manifest.get("nodes", [])
        if isinstance(node, Mapping) and node.get("node_instance_id")
    }


def normalize_edits(raw: Any, manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> tuple[NodeEdit, ...]:
    """把模型返回的编辑规整成白名单内的形状；任何越界都抛错而不是静默丢弃。

    静默丢弃是最坏的选择：候选看起来生成成功了，实际上模型想改的东西一个都没落地，
    运营还会拿它去跑回归。
    """

    if not isinstance(raw, Mapping) or not raw:
        raise AnalysisError("incremental_analysis_candidate_empty", "模型没有给出任何可应用的修改")
    nodes = _nodes_by_id(manifest)
    edits: list[NodeEdit] = []
    for node_id, payload in raw.items():
        node = nodes.get(str(node_id))
        if node is None:
            raise AnalysisError(
