                "incremental_analysis_candidate_unknown_node",
                f"候选修改指向了冻结工作流里不存在的节点：{node_id}",
            )
        kind = str(node.get("kind") or "")
        allowed = EDITABLE_BY_KIND.get(kind)
        if allowed is None:
            raise AnalysisError(
                "incremental_analysis_candidate_node_read_only",
                f"节点 {node_id} 的种类 {kind} 不允许调优修改",
            )
        if not isinstance(payload, Mapping) or not payload:
            raise AnalysisError(
                "incremental_analysis_candidate_invalid",
                f"节点 {node_id} 的修改不是一个非空对象",
            )
        unknown = set(payload) - allowed
        if unknown:
            raise AnalysisError(
                "incremental_analysis_candidate_field_forbidden",
                f"节点 {node_id} 只允许修改 {sorted(allowed)}，收到 {sorted(unknown)}",
            )
        for key, value in payload.items():
            if key in {"system_prompt", "user_prompt"} and (not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip()):
                raise AnalysisError(
                    "incremental_analysis_candidate_invalid",
                    f"节点 {node_id} 的 {key} 必须是非空文本",
                )
            if key == "content" and not isinstance(value, Mapping):
                raise AnalysisError(
                    "incremental_analysis_candidate_invalid",
                    f"节点 {node_id} 的规则内容必须是一个对象",
                )
        edits.append(NodeEdit(node_instance_id=str(node_id), kind=kind, fields=dict(payload)))
    return tuple(edits)


def apply_edits(
    db: Session,
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    edits: tuple[NodeEdit, ...],
    *,
    analysis: IncrementalCorrectionAnalysis,
    actor: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """把编辑套到冻结 manifest 的副本上，返回候选 manifest。

    结构一律继承：节点集合、连线、种类、连接器、模型快照、节点 id 都来自冻结版本，
    这里只替换提示词引用和规则内容。
    """

    candidate = deepcopy(dict(manifest))
    nodes = candidate.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list):
        raise AnalysisError("incremental_analysis_manifest_invalid", "冻结工作流缺少节点定义")
    index = {str(node.get("node_instance_id")): node for node in nodes if isinstance(node, Mapping)}
    for edit in edits:
        node = index[edit.node_instance_id]
        config = node.setdefault("config", {})
        if edit.kind == "prompt":
            prompt = _candidate_prompt(db, analysis=analysis, node=node, edit=edit, actor=actor)
            config["prompt_version_id"] = prompt.id
            snapshot = config.get("component_snapshot")
            if isinstance(snapshot, Mapping):
                config["component_snapshot"] = dict(
                    snapshot, version=prompt.version, version_id=prompt.id)
        else:
            snapshot = config.get("component_snapshot")
            if not isinstance(snapshot, Mapping):
                raise AnalysisError(
                    "incremental_analysis_manifest_invalid",
                    f"规则模块 {edit.node_instance_id} 缺少冻结内容，无法生成候选",
                )
            merged = dict(snapshot)
            merged["content_json"] = canonical_json(edit.fields["content"])
            config["component_snapshot"] = merged
    return candidate


def _candidate_prompt(
    db: Session,
    *,
    analysis: IncrementalCorrectionAnalysis,
    node: Mapping[str, Any],
    edit: NodeEdit,
    actor: str,
) -> PromptVersion:
    config = node.get("config")
    config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
    base_id = config.get("prompt_version_id")
    base = db.get(PromptVersion, base_id) if isinstance(base_id, int) else None
    if base is None:
        raise AnalysisError(
            "incremental_analysis_prompt_missing",
            f"节点 {edit.node_instance_id} 的冻结提示词不存在，无法派生候选",
        )
    version = f"{base.version}+corr{analysis.id}-{edit.node_instance_id}"[:120]
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(PromptVersion).where(
            PromptVersion.category_key == base.category_key,
            PromptVersion.stage == base.stage,
            PromptVersion.version == version,
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        return existing
    candidate = PromptVersion(
        category_key=base.category_key,
        pipeline_scope=base.pipeline_scope,
        stage=base.stage,
        name=base.name,
        version=version,
        system_prompt=edit.fields.get("system_prompt", base.system_prompt),
        user_prompt=edit.fields.get("user_prompt", base.user_prompt),
        rubric_version=base.rubric_version,
        status="draft",
        source="correction_analysis",
        created_by=actor,
    )
    db.add(candidate)
    db.flush()
    return candidate


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 编排：选案例 → 出结论 → 生成候选 → 等人工确认
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


def _load_cases(db: Session, case_ids: list[int]) -> list[IncrementalCorrectionCase]:
    if not case_ids:
        raise AnalysisError("incremental_analysis_cases_required", "至少要选一个纠偏案例", status_code=422)
    if len(case_ids) > MAX_CASES_PER_ANALYSIS:
        raise AnalysisError(
            "incremental_analysis_cases_too_many",
            f"一次最多分析 {MAX_CASES_PER_ANALYSIS} 个案例",
            status_code=422,
        )
    cases = db.scalars(
        select(IncrementalCorrectionCase)
        .where(IncrementalCorrectionCase.id.in_(case_ids))
        .with_for_update()
    ).all()
    missing = set(case_ids) - {case.id for case in cases}
    if missing:
        raise AnalysisError(
            "incremental_analysis_case_not_found",
            "选中的纠偏案例不存在：" + "、".join(str(item) for item in sorted(missing)),
            status_code=404,
        )
    busy = [case.id for case in cases if case.status not in {"open", "analyzed"}]
    if busy:
        raise AnalysisError(
            "incremental_analysis_case_busy",
            "这些案例正在别的分析里：" + "、".join(str(item) for item in sorted(busy)),
        )
    categories = {case.category_key for case in cases}
    versions = {case.workflow_version_id for case in cases}
    if len(categories) != 1 or len(versions) != 1:
        # 跨机制版本混在一起，报告里的「哪一级判得不准」就没有共同的基准可言，
        # 生成出来的候选也不知道该改哪个版本。
        raise AnalysisError(
            "incremental_analysis_cases_mixed",
            "一次分析只能包含同一类目、同一机制版本的案例",
            status_code=422,
        )
    return list(cases)


def start_analysis(
    db: Session,
    *,
    case_ids: list[int],
    actor: str,
    idempotency_key: str,
    generator: CandidateGenerator | None = None,
) -> IncrementalCorrectionAnalysis:
    from .baseline_regression import deterministic_correction_report
    from .workflow_registry import WorkflowRegistryError, create_workflow_version

    if not (idempotency_key or "").strip():
        raise AnalysisError("incremental_analysis_idempotency_required", "缺少幂等键", status_code=422)
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(IncrementalCorrectionAnalysis)
        .where(IncrementalCorrectionAnalysis.idempotency_key == idempotency_key)
    )
    if existing is not None:
        return existing

    cases = _load_cases(db, case_ids)
    first = cases[0]
    analysis = IncrementalCorrectionAnalysis(
        idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
        category_key=first.category_key,
        workflow_version_id=first.workflow_version_id,
        case_ids_json=canonical_json(sorted(case.id for case in cases)),
        created_by=actor,
        status="processing",
        stage="analysis",
        progress=10,
    )
    db.add(analysis)
    db.flush()
    for case in cases:
        case.status = "analyzing"
        case.analysis_id = analysis.id

    snapshot = build_input_snapshot(db, cases)
    analysis.input_snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
    try:
        report = deterministic_correction_report(snapshot)
    except ValueError as exc:
        return _fail(db, analysis, cases, "incremental_analysis_report_failed", str(exc))
    analysis.report_json = canonical_json(report)
    analysis.stage = "candidate_generation"
    analysis.progress = 45
    db.flush()

    version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, analysis.workflow_version_id)
    if version is None:
        return _fail(db, analysis, cases, "incremental_analysis_mechanism_missing", "冻结机制版本不存在")
    from .workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest

    manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(version)
    generator = generator or configured_native_generator(db)
    try:
        candidate = generator.generate(db=db, analysis=analysis, manifest=manifest, report=report)
        edits = normalize_edits({edit.node_instance_id: edit.fields for edit in candidate.edits}, manifest)
        candidate_manifest = apply_edits(db, manifest, edits, analysis=analysis, actor=actor)
    except AnalysisError as exc:
        return _fail(db, analysis, cases, exc.code, str(exc))
    analysis.generator_json = canonical_json({
        "summary": candidate.summary,
        "rationale": candidate.rationale,
        "edited_nodes": [edit.node_instance_id for edit in candidate.edits],
        "model": getattr(generator, "identity", lambda: None)(),
        "trace": candidate.trace,
    })

    definition = db.get(WorkflowDefinition, version.workflow_definition_id)
    if definition is None:
        return _fail(db, analysis, cases, "incremental_analysis_mechanism_missing", "工作流定义不存在")
    try:
        # 和人工建版走完全相同的入口：validate_workflow_manifest 会把模型写出来的
        # manifest 按同一套规则检一遍，写不出合法版本就不会有候选。
        created = create_workflow_version(
            db,
            definition=definition,
            version=f"{version.version}+corr{analysis.id}",
            manifest=candidate_manifest,
            created_by=actor,
        )
    except WorkflowRegistryError as exc:
        return _fail(db, analysis, cases, exc.code, str(exc))
    analysis.candidate_workflow_version_id = created.id
    analysis.stage = "ready"
    analysis.status = "awaiting_decision"
    analysis.progress = 100
    analysis.finished_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    for case in cases:
        case.status = "analyzed"
    db.flush()
    return analysis


def _fail(db, analysis, cases, code: str, message: str):
    analysis.status = "failed"
    analysis.error_code = code[:80]
    analysis.error_message = message
    analysis.finished_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    for case in cases:
        # 失败不该把案例锁死：它们要能再次被选进下一次分析。
        case.status = "open"
        case.analysis_id = None
    db.flush()
    return analysis


def decide_analysis(
    db: Session,
    *,
    analysis_id: int,
    decision: str,
    actor: str,
    note: str,
    expected_lock_version: int | None = None,
) -> IncrementalCorrectionAnalysis:
    """人工确认。两种落地结果，外加一个明确的否决。

    ``adopt``  —— 候选版本转 active，并把该类目的正式增量机制指过去，立即生效。
    ``regression`` —— 不直接生效，冻结一份入口参数，运营去存量回归自己发起验证。
    ``reject`` —— 明确否决，案例放回池子里。
    """

    from .incremental_mechanisms import IncrementalMechanismError, set_incremental_mechanism
    from .workflow_registry import WorkflowRegistryError, transition_workflow_version

    if decision not in {"adopt", "regression", "reject"}:
        raise AnalysisError("incremental_analysis_decision_invalid", "确认结果只能是采纳、转存量回归或否决", status_code=422)
    note = (note or "").strip()
    if not note:
        raise AnalysisError("incremental_analysis_note_required", "人工确认必须写明理由", status_code=422)
    analysis = db.scalar(
        select(IncrementalCorrectionAnalysis)
        .where(IncrementalCorrectionAnalysis.id == analysis_id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if analysis is None:
        raise AnalysisError("incremental_analysis_not_found", "纠偏分析不存在", status_code=404)
    if analysis.status != "awaiting_decision":
        raise AnalysisError(
            "incremental_analysis_not_decidable",
            f"当前状态 {analysis.status} 不能做人工确认",
        )

    if decision == "adopt":
        if analysis.candidate_workflow_version_id is None:
            raise AnalysisError("incremental_analysis_candidate_missing", "这次分析没有候选版本可采纳")
        try:
            transition_workflow_version(db, analysis.candidate_workflow_version_id, "active", actor=actor)
            set_incremental_mechanism(
                db,
                category_key=analysis.category_key,
                workflow_version_id=analysis.candidate_workflow_version_id,
                expected_lock_version=expected_lock_version,
                reason=f"纠偏分析 #{analysis.id} 采纳：{note}",
                actor=actor,
            )
        except (WorkflowRegistryError, IncrementalMechanismError) as exc:
            raise AnalysisError(exc.code, str(exc), status_code=getattr(exc, "status_code", 409)) from exc
        analysis.status = "approved"
    elif decision == "regression":
        # 不直接生效：把发起一轮存量回归所需的东西冻下来，运营照着去手动发起。
        analysis.regression_handoff_json = canonical_json({
            "schema_version": "incremental-correction-regression-handoff-v1",
            "category_key": analysis.category_key,
            "candidate_workflow_version_id": analysis.candidate_workflow_version_id,
            "baseline_workflow_version_id": analysis.workflow_version_id,
            "analysis_id": analysis.id,
            "entry": "/workflow/optimization/baseline-regression",
        })
        analysis.status = "handed_to_regression"
    else:
        analysis.status = "rejected"

    analysis.decision = decision
    analysis.decided_by = actor[:80]
    analysis.decided_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    analysis.decision_note = note
    if decision == "reject":
        for case in db.scalars(
            select(IncrementalCorrectionCase)
            .where(IncrementalCorrectionCase.analysis_id == analysis.id)
        ).all():
            case.status = "open"
            case.analysis_id = None
    db.flush()
    return analysis


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 生成器：让调优模型读分析报告，提出对原生工作流的定向修改
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


def editable_surface(manifest: Mapping[str, Any], db: Session) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """交给模型看的「可改面」。

    只给它能改的东西：提示词正文、规则模块内容，外加节点身份和用途说明。
    连接器配置、模型快照、凭据引用一概不出现在提示里——模型看不到就无从改起，
    也就不会出现「模型提了个越界修改被拒、整次分析失败」的无谓损耗。
    """

    surface: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for node in manifest.get("nodes", []):
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping):
            continue
        kind = str(node.get("kind") or "")
        if kind not in EDITABLE_BY_KIND:
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
        entry: dict[str, Any] = {
            "node_instance_id": node.get("node_instance_id"),
            "kind": kind,
            "display_name": node.get("display_name"),
        }
        if kind == "prompt":
            prompt = db.get(PromptVersion, config.get("prompt_version_id"))
            if prompt is None:
                continue
            entry["system_prompt"] = prompt.system_prompt
            entry["user_prompt"] = prompt.user_prompt
        else:
            snapshot = config.get("component_snapshot")
            snapshot = snapshot if isinstance(snapshot, Mapping) else {}
            entry["module_type_key"] = config.get("module_type_key")
            entry["content"] = _json(snapshot.get("content_json"), {})
        surface.append(entry)
    return surface


class RegisteredNativeTuningGenerator:
    """用注册中心里的调优模型，读报告提修改。"""

    SYSTEM_PROMPT = (
        "你是特鹏标签中台的原生工作流调优器。人工纠偏是真值，模型原判不是。"
        "根据纠偏报告，只提出对下列可改节点的定向修改：提示词节点可改 system_prompt "
        "与 user_prompt；规则模块节点可改 content。不得新增或删除节点、不得改连线、"
        "不得改模型或连接器配置——这些字段你也看不到。只针对报告中真正体现出的偏差"
        "提修改，没有证据支持的节点不要动。只输出合法 JSON，形如 "
        '{"edits": {"<node_instance_id>": {"system_prompt": "..."}}, '
        '"summary": "一句话说明改了什么", "rationale": "为什么这样改能纠正报告里的偏差"}。'
    )

    def __init__(self, entry: ModelRegistryEntry, config: Any) -> None:
        self.entry = entry
        self.config = config

    def identity(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """留在候选记录里：这份修改是注册中心里哪一条调优模型给的。"""

        return {"registry_entry_id": self.entry.id, "name": self.entry.name,
                "provider": self.entry.provider, "model_id": self.entry.model_id}

    def generate(self, *, db, analysis, manifest, report) -> GeneratedCandidate:
        from .doubao_client import DoubaoClient

        payload = {
            "schema_version": "incremental-correction-generator-input-v1",
            "category_key": analysis.category_key,
            "correction_report": report,
            "editable_nodes": editable_surface(manifest, db),
        }
        client = DoubaoClient(self.config)
        trace: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        last: AnalysisError | None = None
        for attempt in (1, 2):
            response = asyncio.run(client.chat_json(
                self.SYSTEM_PROMPT,
                json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False),
                output_budget=min(max(1, int(self.entry.max_tokens)), 12000),
                reasoning_effort="high" if self.entry.level in {"advanced", "expert"} else "medium",
                structured_output=True,
            ))
            trace.append({
                "attempt": attempt,
                "request_correlation_id": getattr(response, "request_correlation_id", None),
                "usage": {
                    "input_tokens": getattr(response, "input_tokens", None),
                    "output_tokens": getattr(response, "output_tokens", None),
                },
            })
            try:
                parsed = json.loads(str(getattr(response, "raw_text", "") or "{}"))
            except ValueError as exc:
                last = AnalysisError("incremental_analysis_candidate_unparsable",
                                     "调优模型没有返回合法 JSON")
                continue
            if not isinstance(parsed, Mapping):
                last = AnalysisError("incremental_analysis_candidate_unparsable",
                                     "调优模型返回的不是一个对象")
                continue
            try:
                edits = normalize_edits(parsed.get("edits"), manifest)
            except AnalysisError as exc:
                # 第一次越界就把原因回灌给模型，它通常能改对；两次都不行才算失败。
                last = exc
                payload["previous_rejection"] = str(exc)
                continue
            return GeneratedCandidate(
                edits=edits,
                summary=str(parsed.get("summary") or "")[:500],
                rationale=str(parsed.get("rationale") or "")[:2000],
                trace=trace,
            )
        raise last or AnalysisError("incremental_analysis_candidate_empty", "调优模型没有给出可用修改")


def configured_native_generator(db: Session) -> CandidateGenerator:
    from .tuning_model_registry import resolve_tuning_entry, skipped_suffix

    entry, skipped = resolve_tuning_entry(db)
    if entry is None:
        raise AnalysisError(
            "incremental_analysis_tuning_model_missing",
            "未配置可用的调优模型；请先在模型登记里启用一个调优角色的模型"
            + skipped_suffix(skipped),
        )
    if not entry.structured_output or entry.max_tokens < 1:
        raise AnalysisError(
            "incremental_analysis_tuning_model_invalid",
            "调优模型必须支持结构化输出并配置有效输出上限",
        )
    source: Any = None
    if entry.source_optimizer_config_id is not None:
        source = db.get(OptimizerConfig, entry.source_optimizer_config_id)
    elif entry.source_model_config_id is not None:
        source = db.get(ModelConfig, entry.source_model_config_id)
    encrypted = entry.encrypted_api_key or getattr(source, "encrypted_api_key", None)
    if not encrypted:
        raise AnalysisError(
            "incremental_analysis_tuning_model_credential_missing", "调优模型尚未配置凭据")
    from types import SimpleNamespace

    return RegisteredNativeTuningGenerator(entry, SimpleNamespace(
        provider=entry.provider, protocol=entry.protocol, base_url=entry.base_url,
        api_path=entry.api_path, model_id=entry.model_id, encrypted_api_key=encrypted,
        temperature=entry.temperature, max_tokens=entry.max_tokens,
        timeout_seconds=entry.timeout_seconds, max_retries=entry.max_retries,
        structured_output=entry.structured_output, thinking_mode=entry.thinking_mode,
    ))


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 读模型：案例列表、分析列表、分析详情
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #

_MAX_PAGE = 200


def _page(statement, db: Session, *, limit: int, offset: int):
    from sqlalchemy import func

    size = min(max(int(limit), 1), _MAX_PAGE)
    start = max(int(offset), 0)
    total = db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(statement.subquery())) or 0
    rows = db.scalars(statement.offset(start).limit(size)).all()
    return rows, {"total": int(total), "offset": start, "limit": size,
                  "has_more": start + len(rows) < int(total)}


def _case_payload(case: IncrementalCorrectionCase) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": case.id,
        "correction_key": case.correction_key,
        "evaluation_id": case.evaluation_id,
        "production_run_id": case.production_run_id,
        "asset_id": case.asset_id,
        "category_key": case.category_key,
        "workflow_version_id": case.workflow_version_id,
        "kind": case.kind,
        "node_instance_id": case.node_instance_id,
        "field_path": case.field_path,
        "ai_value": _json(case.ai_value_json, None),
        "human_value": _json(case.human_value_json, None),
        "reason": case.reason,
        "corrector": case.corrector,
        "status": case.status,
        "analysis_id": case.analysis_id,
        "corrected_at": case.corrected_at,
        "created_at": case.created_at,
    }


def list_cases(
    db: Session,
    *,
    status: str | None = None,
    category_key: str | None = None,
    workflow_version_id: int | None = None,
    kind: str | None = None,
    corrector: str | None = None,
    limit: int = 50,
    offset: int = 0,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    from .incremental_correction_models import CASE_STATUSES

    if status is not None and status not in CASE_STATUSES:
        raise AnalysisError("incremental_analysis_case_status_invalid",
                            "案例状态只能是 " + "、".join(CASE_STATUSES), status_code=422)
    statement = select(IncrementalCorrectionCase)
    if status:
        statement = statement.where(IncrementalCorrectionCase.status == status)
    if category_key:
        statement = statement.where(IncrementalCorrectionCase.category_key == category_key)
    if workflow_version_id is not None:
        statement = statement.where(
            IncrementalCorrectionCase.workflow_version_id == workflow_version_id)
    if kind:
        statement = statement.where(IncrementalCorrectionCase.kind == kind)
    if corrector:
        statement = statement.where(IncrementalCorrectionCase.corrector == corrector)
    rows, page = _page(
        statement.order_by(IncrementalCorrectionCase.id.desc()), db, limit=limit, offset=offset)
    return {"items": [_case_payload(row) for row in rows], **page}


def case_filter_options(db: Session) -> dict[str, Any]:
    from sqlalchemy import func

    from .incremental_correction_models import CASE_STATUSES

    rows = db.execute(
        select(IncrementalCorrectionCase.category_key,
               IncrementalCorrectionCase.workflow_version_id,
               IncrementalCorrectionCase.status,
               func.count(IncrementalCorrectionCase.id))
        .group_by(IncrementalCorrectionCase.category_key,
                  IncrementalCorrectionCase.workflow_version_id,
                  IncrementalCorrectionCase.status)
    ).all()
    categories: dict[str, int] = {}
    workflows: dict[int, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    statuses = {name: 0 for name in CASE_STATUSES}
    for category_key, version_id, status, count in rows:
        categories[str(category_key)] = categories.get(str(category_key), 0) + int(count)
        bucket = workflows.setdefault(int(version_id), {
            "workflow_version_id": int(version_id), "category_key": str(category_key), "total": 0})
        bucket["total"] += int(count)
        statuses[status] = statuses.get(status, 0) + int(count)
    return {
        "categories": [{"category_key": key, "total": value}
                       for key, value in sorted(categories.items())],
        "workflows": sorted(workflows.values(), key=lambda item: -item["total"]),
        "statuses": [{"key": key, "total": statuses.get(key, 0)} for key in CASE_STATUSES],
        "kinds": [{"key": "level", "label": "等级"}, {"key": "field", "label": "内容字段"}],
    }


def dismiss_cases(db: Session, *, case_ids: list[int], actor: str, reason: str) -> int:
    """明确判定「这批不值得进分析」。不是删除——案例还在，只是不再出现在待办里。"""

    if not (reason or "").strip():
        raise AnalysisError("incremental_analysis_note_required", "忽略案例也要写明理由", status_code=422)
    cases = _load_cases(db, case_ids)
    for case in cases:
        case.status = "dismissed"
        case.reason = (case.reason + f"\n[{actor} 忽略] {reason.strip()}").strip()
    db.flush()
    return len(cases)


def _analysis_payload(analysis: IncrementalCorrectionAnalysis, *, full: bool) -> dict[str, Any]:
    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
        "id": analysis.id,
        "category_key": analysis.category_key,
        "workflow_version_id": analysis.workflow_version_id,
        "case_ids": _json(analysis.case_ids_json, []),
        "case_count": len(_json(analysis.case_ids_json, [])),
        "status": analysis.status,
        "stage": analysis.stage,
        "progress": analysis.progress,
        "candidate_workflow_version_id": analysis.candidate_workflow_version_id,
        "error_code": analysis.error_code,
        "error_message": analysis.error_message,
        "decision": analysis.decision,
        "decided_by": analysis.decided_by,
        "decided_at": analysis.decided_at,
        "decision_note": analysis.decision_note,
        "created_by": analysis.created_by,
        "created_at": analysis.created_at,
        "finished_at": analysis.finished_at,
    }
    if full:
        payload["report"] = _json(analysis.report_json, {})
        payload["generator"] = _json(analysis.generator_json, {})
        payload["input_snapshot"] = _json(analysis.input_snapshot_json, {})
        payload["blockers"] = _json(analysis.blockers_json, [])
        payload["regression_handoff"] = _json(analysis.regression_handoff_json, {})
    return payload


def list_analyses(
    db: Session,
    *,
    status: str | None = None,
    category_key: str | None = None,
    limit: int = 50,
    offset: int = 0,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    from .incremental_correction_models import ANALYSIS_STATUSES

    if status is not None and status not in ANALYSIS_STATUSES:
        raise AnalysisError("incremental_analysis_status_invalid",
                            "分析状态只能是 " + "、".join(ANALYSIS_STATUSES), status_code=422)
    statement = select(IncrementalCorrectionAnalysis)
    if status:
        statement = statement.where(IncrementalCorrectionAnalysis.status == status)
    if category_key:
        statement = statement.where(IncrementalCorrectionAnalysis.category_key == category_key)
    rows, page = _page(
        statement.order_by(IncrementalCorrectionAnalysis.id.desc()), db, limit=limit, offset=offset)
    return {"items": [_analysis_payload(row, full=False) for row in rows], **page}


def get_analysis(db: Session, analysis_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
    analysis = db.get(IncrementalCorrectionAnalysis, analysis_id)
    if analysis is None:
        raise AnalysisError("incremental_analysis_not_found", "纠偏分析不存在", status_code=404)
    payload = _analysis_payload(analysis, full=True)
    payload["cases"] = [
        _case_payload(case)
        for case in db.scalars(
            select(IncrementalCorrectionCase)
            .where(IncrementalCorrectionCase.id.in_(payload["case_ids"]))
            .order_by(IncrementalCorrectionCase.id)
        ).all()
    ]
    candidate = (
        db.get(WorkflowVersion, analysis.candidate_workflow_version_id)
        if analysis.candidate_workflow_version_id is not None else None
    )
    base = db.get(WorkflowVersion, analysis.workflow_version_id)
    payload["candidate_version"] = (
        {"id": candidate.id, "version": candidate.version, "status": candidate.status}
        if candidate is not None else None
    )
    payload["base_version"] = (
        {"id": base.id, "version": base.version, "status": base.status}
        if base is not None else None
    )
    return payload

===== FILE backend/app/incremental_correction_analysis_api.py =====
"""增量纠偏分析页的接口：案例列表、发起分析、分析列表与详情、人工确认。

写操作三个，权限都按 reviews:write 收：忽略案例、发起分析、人工确认。
发起分析会真实调用调优模型（要花钱），所以它和确认一样需要幂等键和明确理由。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Callable

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, field_validator
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .database import get_db
from .incremental_correction_analysis import (
    AnalysisError,
    case_filter_options,
    decide_analysis,
    dismiss_cases,
    get_analysis,
    list_analyses,
    list_cases,
    start_analysis,
)


def _not_blank(value: str) -> str:
    if not value.strip():
        raise ValueError("不能只填空白")
    return value


class StartAnalysisRequest(BaseModel):
    case_ids: list[int] = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200)
    idempotency_key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=160)

    @field_validator("case_ids")
    @classmethod
    def _positive(cls, value: list[int]) -> list[int]:
        if any(item <= 0 for item in value):
            raise ValueError("案例 id 必须是正整数")
        return sorted(set(value))


class DismissRequest(BaseModel):
    case_ids: list[int] = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200)
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=500)

    _reason = field_validator("reason")(_not_blank)


class DecisionRequest(BaseModel):
    #: adopt=立即生效；regression=交给存量回归自己验证；reject=否决
    decision: str = Field(pattern="^(adopt|regression|reject)$")
    note: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=1000)
    #: 采纳时用来防并发：别人刚改过这个类目的机制指针就不许盖过去
    expected_lock_version: int | None = None

    _note = field_validator("note")(_not_blank)


def _http(exc: AnalysisError) -> HTTPException:
    return HTTPException(status_code=exc.status_code,
                         detail={"code": exc.code, "message": str(exc)})


def build_incremental_correction_analysis_router(
    require_user: Callable[..., Any],
    require_reviewer: Callable[..., Any],
) -> APIRouter:
    router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/incremental-correction-analysis",
                       tags=["incremental-correction-analysis"])

    def _actor(user: Any) -> str:
        return str(getattr(user, "username", None)
                   or getattr(user, "display_name", None) or "unknown")

    @router.get("/cases")
    def cases(
        status: str | None = None,
        category_key: str | None = None,
        workflow_version_id: int | None = None,
        kind: str | None = None,
        corrector: str | None = None,
        limit: int = Query(default=50, ge=1, le=200),
        offset: int = Query(default=0, ge=0),
        _user: Any = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return list_cases(db, status=status, category_key=category_key,
                              workflow_version_id=workflow_version_id, kind=kind,
                              corrector=corrector, limit=limit, offset=offset)
        except AnalysisError as exc:
            raise _http(exc) from exc

    @router.get("/cases/filters")
    def case_filters(
        _user: Any = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return case_filter_options(db)

    @router.post("/cases/dismiss")
    def dismiss(
        payload: DismissRequest,
        user: Any = Depends(require_reviewer),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            count = dismiss_cases(db, case_ids=payload.case_ids,
                                  actor=_actor(user), reason=payload.reason.strip())
        except AnalysisError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http(exc) from exc
        db.commit()
        return {"dismissed": count}

    @router.get("")
    def analyses(
        status: str | None = None,
        category_key: str | None = None,
        limit: int = Query(default=50, ge=1, le=200),
        offset: int = Query(default=0, ge=0),
        _user: Any = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return list_analyses(db, status=status, category_key=category_key,
                                 limit=limit, offset=offset)
        except AnalysisError as exc:
            raise _http(exc) from exc

    @router.post("")
    def start(
        payload: StartAnalysisRequest,
        user: Any = Depends(require_reviewer),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            analysis = start_analysis(db, case_ids=payload.case_ids, actor=_actor(user),
                                      idempotency_key=payload.idempotency_key)
        except AnalysisError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http(exc) from exc
        db.commit()
        return get_analysis(db, analysis.id)

    @router.get("/{analysis_id}")
    def detail(
        analysis_id: int,
        _user: Any = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return get_analysis(db, analysis_id)
        except AnalysisError as exc:
            raise _http(exc) from exc

    @router.post("/{analysis_id}/decision")
    def decide(
        analysis_id: int,
        payload: DecisionRequest,
        user: Any = Depends(require_reviewer),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            decide_analysis(db, analysis_id=analysis_id, decision=payload.decision,
                            actor=_actor(user), note=payload.note.strip(),
                            expected_lock_version=payload.expected_lock_version)
        except AnalysisError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http(exc) from exc
        db.commit()
        return get_analysis(db, analysis_id)

    return router

===== FILE backend/app/incremental_correction_api.py =====
"""增量纠偏页的读写接口。

写只有一条：列表直接改等级。逐字段改内容仍然走既有的纠偏合同接口，因为那条路要
冻结合同、逐节点校验、必要时重跑提示词——不是列表该承担的事。两条路最后都落到
同一处：覆盖 AI 结果，按 revision+1 重发下游。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Callable

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, field_validator
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .database import get_db
from .incremental_correction import (
    CORRECTION_STATES,
    IncrementalCorrectionError,
    batch_correct_incremental_level,
    correct_incremental_field,
    correct_incremental_level,
    correction_dashboard,
    correction_filter_options,
    incremental_correction_detail,
    list_incremental_corrections,
)


class LevelCorrectionRequest(BaseModel):
    level: str = Field(min_length=2, max_length=10)
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=500)
    idempotency_key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=120)
    #: 运营看到的等级。多人同时处理时，它保证不会把别人刚改完的结果又盖回去。
    expected_level: str | None = None

    @field_validator("reason")
    @classmethod
    def _reason_not_blank(cls, value: str) -> str:
        if not value.strip():
            raise ValueError("纠偏必须填写理由")
        return value


class BatchLevelItem(BaseModel):
    evaluation_id: int = Field(ge=1)
    #: 勾选时看到的等级；别人先改了就跳过这条而不是盖回去。
    expected_level: str | None = None


class BatchLevelCorrectionRequest(BaseModel):
    items: list[BatchLevelItem] = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200)
    level: str = Field(min_length=2, max_length=10)
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=500)
    idempotency_key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=120)

    @field_validator("reason")
    @classmethod
    def _reason_not_blank(cls, value: str) -> str:
        if not value.strip():
            raise ValueError("纠偏必须填写理由")
        return value


class FieldCorrectionRequest(BaseModel):
    node_instance_id: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=160)
    field_path: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=300)
    value: Any = None
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=500)
    idempotency_key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=120)
    #: 改上游内容会重跑提示词并产生真实模型费用；必须由人明确确认。
    real_execution_confirmed: bool = False

    @field_validator("reason")
    @classmethod
    def _reason_not_blank(cls, value: str) -> str:
        if not value.strip():
            raise ValueError("纠偏必须填写理由")
        return value


def _http(exc: IncrementalCorrectionError) -> HTTPException:
    return HTTPException(
        status_code=exc.status_code,
        detail={"code": exc.code, "message": str(exc)},
    )


def build_incremental_correction_router(
    require_user: Callable[..., Any],
    require_reviewer: Callable[..., Any],
) -> APIRouter:
    router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/incremental-corrections", tags=["incremental-correction"])

    @router.get("")
    def list_queue(
        category_key: str | None = None,
        workflow_version_id: int | None = None,
        state: str | None = Query(default=None, pattern="|".join(CORRECTION_STATES)),
        level: str | None = None,
        upstream_class_id: int | None = Query(default=None, ge=1),
        corrected_by: str | None = Query(default=None, max_length=80),
        published_from: str | None = Query(default=None, max_length=10),
        published_to: str | None = Query(default=None, max_length=10),
        search: str | None = Query(default=None, max_length=160),
        limit: int = Query(default=50, ge=1, le=200),
        offset: int = Query(default=0, ge=0),
        _user: Any = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return list_incremental_corrections(
                db,
                category_key=category_key,
                workflow_version_id=workflow_version_id,
                state=state,
                level=level,
                upstream_class_id=upstream_class_id,
                corrected_by=corrected_by,
                published_from=published_from,
                published_to=published_to,
                search=search,
                limit=limit,
                offset=offset,
            )
        except IncrementalCorrectionError as exc:
            raise _http(exc) from exc

    # 放在 /{evaluation_id}/level 前面：路径先按声明顺序匹配，"batch" 不是整数。
    @router.put("/batch/level")
    def batch_correct_level(
        payload: BatchLevelCorrectionRequest,
        user: Any = Depends(require_reviewer),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        actor = str(
            getattr(user, "username", None)
            or getattr(user, "display_name", None)
            or "unknown"
        )
        try:
            return batch_correct_incremental_level(
                db,
                items=[item.model_dump() for item in payload.items],
                level=payload.level,
                reason=payload.reason.strip(),
                actor=actor,
                idempotency_key=payload.idempotency_key,
            )
        except IncrementalCorrectionError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http(exc) from exc

    @router.get("/filters")
    def filters(
        _user: Any = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return correction_filter_options(db)

    @router.put("/{evaluation_id}/level")
    def correct_level(
        evaluation_id: int,
        payload: LevelCorrectionRequest,
        user: Any = Depends(require_reviewer),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        actor = str(
            getattr(user, "username", None)
            or getattr(user, "display_name", None)
            or "unknown"
        )
        try:
            response = correct_incremental_level(
                db,
                evaluation_id=evaluation_id,
                level=payload.level,
                expected_level=payload.expected_level,
                reason=payload.reason.strip(),
                actor=actor,
                idempotency_key=payload.idempotency_key,
            )
        except IncrementalCorrectionError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http(exc) from exc
        db.commit()
        return response

    @router.get("/dashboard")
    def dashboard(
        category_key: str | None = None,
        workflow_version_id: int | None = None,
        window: int = Query(default=1000, ge=1, le=5000),
        _user: Any = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return correction_dashboard(
            db,
            category_key=category_key,
            workflow_version_id=workflow_version_id,
            window=window,
        )

    @router.get("/{evaluation_id}/detail")
    def detail(
        evaluation_id: int,
        _user: Any = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return incremental_correction_detail(db, evaluation_id=evaluation_id)
        except IncrementalCorrectionError as exc:
            raise _http(exc) from exc

    @router.put("/{evaluation_id}/field")
    def correct_field(
        evaluation_id: int,
        payload: FieldCorrectionRequest,
        user: Any = Depends(require_reviewer),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        actor = str(
            getattr(user, "username", None)
            or getattr(user, "display_name", None)
            or "unknown"
        )
        try:
            response = correct_incremental_field(
                db,
                evaluation_id=evaluation_id,
                node_instance_id=payload.node_instance_id,
                field_path=payload.field_path,
                value=payload.value,
                reason=payload.reason.strip(),
                actor=actor,
                idempotency_key=payload.idempotency_key,
                real_execution_confirmed=payload.real_execution_confirmed,
            )
        except IncrementalCorrectionError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http(exc) from exc
        db.commit()
        return response

    return router

===== FILE backend/app/incremental_correction_models.py =====
"""增量纠偏分析的两张表：纠偏案例，和对一批案例做的一次分析。

为什么不复用存量那套 ``BaselineCorrectionRun``：它的 ``baseline_run_id`` 非空、
外键指向基准回归，而增量纠偏没有基准 run；更根本的是它的候选生成绑死在 v3 规则图
（``CategoryEvaluationV3Revision`` ＋ A/B 两段提示词）上，而增量走的是原生工作流，
结果里连 ``v3_context`` 都没有。分析结论那一半是可以共用的——
``deterministic_correction_report`` 是纯函数，只吃一个冻结快照——所以两条链路用同一套
分析语言，只有候选生成各走各的。

案例是「一次人工改动」，不是「一条素材」：同一条素材被改了等级又改了字段，就是两个
案例，因为它们指向的问题不同。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone

from sqlalchemy import (
    CheckConstraint,
    DateTime,
    ForeignKey,
    Integer,
    String,
    Text,
    UniqueConstraint,
)
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from .database import Base


def utcnow() -> datetime:
    # 本地定义而不是从 models.py 取：models.py 在文件末尾反过来 import 本模块，
    # 借过去就成环了。
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)

#: 案例的流转。dismissed 是运营明确判定「这条不值得进分析」，不是删除。
CASE_STATUSES = ("open", "analyzing", "analyzed", "dismissed")
#: 分析的流转。handed_to_regression 表示运营选择去存量回归自己验证，不是失败。
ANALYSIS_STATUSES = (
    "processing",
    "awaiting_decision",
    "approved",
    "handed_to_regression",
    "rejected",
    "failed",
)
ANALYSIS_STAGES = ("analysis", "candidate_generation", "ready")


class IncrementalCorrectionCase(Base):
    __tablename__ = "incremental_correction_cases"
    __table_args__ = (
        # 一次人工改动只能有一个案例；correction_key 由纠偏写入时生成。
        UniqueConstraint("correction_key", name="uq_incremental_correction_case_key"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('open','analyzing','analyzed','dismissed')",
            name="ck_incremental_correction_case_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "kind IN ('level','field')",
            name="ck_incremental_correction_case_kind",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    correction_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), index=True)
    evaluation_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("evaluation_results.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), index=True
    )
    production_run_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True, index=True)
    asset_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True, index=True)
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    workflow_version_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, index=True)
    kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), index=True)
    #: 字段纠偏才有；等级纠偏留空。
    node_instance_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160), default="", server_default="")
    field_path: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(300), default="", server_default="")
    #: 改之前/之后的值，JSON 文本。等级纠偏就是两个等级字符串。
    ai_value_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="null", server_default="null")
    human_value_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="null", server_default="null")
    reason: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="", server_default="")
    corrector: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="", server_default="", index=True)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(20), default="open", server_default="open", index=True
    )
    analysis_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True, index=True)
    corrected_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, index=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, onupdate=utcnow
    )


class IncrementalCorrectionAnalysis(Base):
    __tablename__ = "incremental_correction_analyses"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("idempotency_key", name="uq_incremental_correction_analysis_key"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('processing','awaiting_decision','approved',"
            "'handed_to_regression','rejected','failed')",
            name="ck_incremental_correction_analysis_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "stage IN ('analysis','candidate_generation','ready')",
            name="ck_incremental_correction_analysis_stage",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "progress BETWEEN 0 AND 100",
            name="ck_incremental_correction_analysis_progress",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    idempotency_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160), index=True)
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    #: 这批案例共同的冻结机制版本；跨版本的案例不允许进同一次分析。
    workflow_version_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, index=True)
    case_ids_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="[]", server_default="[]")
    input_snapshot_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}", server_default="{}")
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(30), default="processing", server_default="processing", index=True
    )
    stage: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(30), default="analysis", server_default="analysis", index=True
    )
    progress: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default="0")
    #: deterministic_correction_report 的产出，和存量那边同一套语言。
    report_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}", server_default="{}")
    #: 模型生成候选的请求与回答，留痕用；不含密钥。
    generator_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}", server_default="{}")
    candidate_workflow_version_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        Integer, nullable=True, index=True
    )
    blockers_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="[]", server_default="[]")
    error_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="", server_default="")
    error_message: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="", server_default="")
    decision: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(30), nullable=True)
    decided_by: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(80), nullable=True)
    decided_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    decision_note: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="", server_default="")
    #: 交给存量回归时冻结的入口参数，运营照着它发起验证。
    regression_handoff_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}", server_default="{}")
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="system", server_default="system")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, index=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, onupdate=utcnow
    )
    finished_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)

===== FILE backend/app/incremental_dashboard.py =====
"""Read-only daily workflow counts; upstream census is explicitly unavailable.

Business materials are deduplicated by content record (fallback: ingress event).
A runtime retry does not create an additional handled material. No billing or
provider calls occur here. Model and manual completions share the earliest
usable-result timestamp; downstream delivery is a different metric.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import json
from collections import Counter
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta, timezone
from sqlalchemy import String, case, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from .models import ContentIngressEvent, ProductionRun, WorkflowDefinition
from .failure_resolution_models import ManualFailureResolution

ZONE = timezone(timedelta(hours=8))


def local_day(value: datetime) -> date:
    return (value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc) if value.tzinfo is None else value).astimezone(ZONE).date()


def business_date_isoformat(business_date: date) -> str:
    """Serialize a calendar date, never a timestamp lacking its offset."""
    if type(business_date) is not date:
        raise TypeError('业务日期必须是 date，不能是 datetime')
    return business_date.isoformat()


def comparison_day(day: date, mode: str) -> date | None:
    if mode == 'none': return None
    if mode == 'day': return day - timedelta(days=1)
    if mode == 'week': return day - timedelta(days=7)
    try:
        if mode == 'year': return day.replace(year=day.year-1)
        if mode == 'month': return day.replace(year=day.year-(day.month == 1), month=day.month-1 or 12)
    except ValueError: return None  # no Feb 29 / 31 counterpart; never silently duplicate dates
    raise ValueError('不支持的比较期间')


def aggregate_days(facts, start: date, end: date, mode: str, *, today: date):
    if start > end or (end-start).days > 365 or end > today:
        raise ValueError('请选择不超过366天且结束日期不晚于今天的有效范围')
    if mode not in {'none','day','week','month','year'}: raise ValueError('不支持的比较期间')
    admitted=Counter(local_day(r['admitted']) for r in facts)
    completed=Counter(local_day(r['completed']) for r in facts if r['completed'])
    first=min(admitted,default=None)
    def point(day):
        known=day is not None and first is not None and first<=day<=today
        return {'date':business_date_isoformat(day) if day else None,'platform_admitted':admitted[day] if known else None,
                'platform_success':completed[day] if known else None,'upstream_increment':None,'coverage':None}
    days=[start+timedelta(days=n) for n in range((end-start).days+1)]
    current=[point(d) for d in days]
    previous=[point(comparison_day(d,mode)) for d in days] if mode!='none' else []
    def total(points,field):
        return sum(p[field] for p in points) if points and all(p[field] is not None for p in points) else None
    return {'timezone':'Asia/Shanghai','start':business_date_isoformat(start),'end':business_date_isoformat(end),'comparison':mode,
            'history_start':business_date_isoformat(first) if first else None,'series':current,'comparison_series':previous,
            'summary':{'platform_admitted':total(current,'platform_admitted'),
                       'platform_success':total(current,'platform_success'),
                       'comparison_success':total(previous,'platform_success'),
                       'cumulative_success':sum(n for d,n in completed.items() if d<=end) if first and first<=end else None,
                       'upstream_increment':None,'coverage':None},
            'upstream_status':'not_connected',
            'scope_note':'仅真实模型增量运行；按业务素材去重。平台建单量不等于上游全部新增量；不包含尚未建立运行的接入积压。',
            'history_note':'可见记录起点之前显示无数据；起点当天可能是不完整日，今天尚未结束，与历史整日不可直接比较。尚未接入上游全量历史，不能计算处置覆盖率。'}


def workflow_dashboard(db: Session, *, category_key: str, workflow_definition_id: int,
                       start: date, end: date, comparison: str, now: datetime | None = None):
    definition=db.get(WorkflowDefinition,workflow_definition_id)
    if definition is None or category_key not in json.loads(definition.allowed_categories_json or '[]'):
        raise LookupError('类目与工作流不匹配')
    # Grouping in SQL bounds transfer by distinct material count rather than physical calls.
    key=case((ContentIngressEvent.content_record_id.is_not(None), 'content:'+func.cast(ContentIngressEvent.content_record_id, String)),
             (ProductionRun.source_id.is_not(None), 'event:'+func.cast(ProductionRun.source_id, String)),
             else_='run:'+func.cast(ProductionRun.id, String))
    rows=db.execute(select(key.label('material'),func.min(ProductionRun.created_at).label('admitted'),
            func.min(case((ProductionRun.status=='succeeded',ProductionRun.finished_at))).label('model_done'),
            func.min(ManualFailureResolution.created_at).label('manual_done'))
        .outerjoin(ContentIngressEvent,ContentIngressEvent.id==ProductionRun.source_id)
        .outerjoin(ManualFailureResolution,(ManualFailureResolution.production_run_id==ProductionRun.id)&(ManualFailureResolution.status=='published'))
        .where(ProductionRun.source_type=='content_ingress_event',ProductionRun.run_mode=='incremental',
               ProductionRun.model_execution_mode=='real',ProductionRun.category_key==category_key,
               ProductionRun.workflow_definition_id==workflow_definition_id)
        .group_by(key)).mappings().all()
    facts=[{'admitted':r['admitted'],'completed':min([v for v in [r['model_done'],r['manual_done']] if v is not None],default=None)} for r in rows]
    result=aggregate_days(facts,start,end,comparison,today=local_day(now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)))
    return {**result,'category_key':category_key,'workflow_definition_id':workflow_definition_id,'workflow_name':definition.name}

===== FILE backend/app/incremental_evidence.py =====
"""Source-version comparisons and bounded, source-backed operations evidence.

Imported references are operator assertions, not independently verified ground truth.
No function activates routing, evaluates a model, or writes to a downstream system.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import math
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import JSON, cast, case, func, or_, select, type_coerce, update
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .api_datetime_encoding import platform_isoformat
from .audit import append_audit_event
from .database_capabilities import DatabaseCapabilities
from .incremental_evidence_models import IncrementalReferenceResult, IncrementalCapacitySamplingPolicy, IncrementalCapacitySample
from .models import (WorkflowDefinition, WorkflowVersion, IncrementalShadowResult, IncrementalEntityAssignment,
    ProductionRun, WorkflowModelInvocation, RuntimeOutboxEvent, DownstreamPublishLedger, MaterialPackage,
    BaselineRegressionRun, BaselineRegressionItem, ContentRecord)

MAX_IMPORT = 100
MAX_PAGE = 200
MAX_POLICIES_PER_TICK = 20
MAX_JSON_BYTES = 65536
MAX_WINDOW = timedelta(days=31)


class EvidenceError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, message: str, *, code='incremental_evidence_invalid', status_code=422):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code, self.status_code = code, status_code


def _utc(value: datetime) -> datetime:
    if value.tzinfo is None or value.utcoffset() is None:
        raise EvidenceError('时间必须包含时区')
    return value.astimezone(timezone.utc)


def _stored_utc(value: datetime) -> datetime:
    return value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc) if value.tzinfo is None else value.astimezone(timezone.utc)


def _json(value: Any) -> str:
    try:
        encoded = json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':'), allow_nan=False)
    except (ValueError, TypeError, RecursionError) as exc:
        raise EvidenceError('结果必须是有限、有效的 JSON') from exc
    if len(encoded.encode('utf-8')) > MAX_JSON_BYTES:
        raise EvidenceError('单条结果不能超过 64 KiB')
    def validate(item, depth=0):
        if depth > 20:
            raise EvidenceError('结果嵌套不能超过 20 层')
        if isinstance(item, dict):
            if any(not isinstance(key, str) for key in item):
                raise EvidenceError('JSON 字段名必须是字符串')
            for child in item.values(): validate(child, depth + 1)
        elif isinstance(item, list):
            for child in item: validate(child, depth + 1)
    validate(value)
    return encoded


def _text(value, field, maximum):
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip() or len(value) > maximum:
        raise EvidenceError(f'{field} 不能为空且不能超过 {maximum} 字符')
    return value.strip()


def _integer(value, minimum, maximum, field):
    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int) or not minimum <= value <= maximum:
        raise EvidenceError(f'{field} 必须在 {minimum} 到 {maximum} 之间')
    return value


def _scope(db, category_key, workflow_definition_id):
    category_key = _text(category_key, 'category_key', 40)
    _integer(workflow_definition_id, 1, 2147483647, 'workflow_definition_id')
    definition = db.get(WorkflowDefinition, workflow_definition_id)
    if definition is None:
        raise EvidenceError('工作流不存在', status_code=404)
    categories = json.loads(definition.allowed_categories_json or '[]')
    if categories and category_key not in categories:
        raise EvidenceError('类目与工作流不匹配')
    return category_key


def _audit(db, action, subject_id, actor, reason, **payload):
    append_audit_event(db, category='incremental_evidence', action=action,
        subject_type='incremental_evidence', subject_id=subject_id,
        actor=actor, payload={'reason': reason, **payload})


def _identity(values):
    return hashlib.sha256(_json([values[key] for key in (
        'category_key','workflow_definition_id','workflow_version_id','source_system','entity_id','source_version','step_key')]).encode()).hexdigest()


def import_reference_results(db: Session, *, items: list[dict], reason: str, actor: str) -> dict:
    reason, actor = _text(reason,'reason',2000), _text(actor,'actor',80)
    if not isinstance(items, list) or not 1 <= len(items) <= MAX_IMPORT:
        raise EvidenceError('每批参考结果必须为 1 到 100 条')
    prepared = []
    for item in items:
        values = {key: _text(item.get(key),key,size) for key,size in (
            ('category_key',40),('source_system',120),('entity_id',320),('source_version',200),
            ('step_key',120),('reference_source',500))}
        values['workflow_definition_id'] = item.get('workflow_definition_id')
        values['workflow_version_id'] = item.get('workflow_version_id')
        _scope(db, values['category_key'], values['workflow_definition_id'])
        _integer(values['workflow_version_id'],1,2147483647,'workflow_version_id')
        version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, values['workflow_version_id'])
        if version is None or version.workflow_definition_id != values['workflow_definition_id']:
            raise EvidenceError('参考结果目标机制版本与工作流不匹配')
        if item.get('evidence_kind') not in ('actual','synthetic'):
            raise EvidenceError('evidence_kind 必须是 actual 或 synthetic')
        if not isinstance(item.get('result'), dict):
            raise EvidenceError('result 必须是 JSON 对象')
        result_json = _json(item['result'])
        values.update(evidence_kind=item['evidence_kind'],result_json=result_json,
            result_hash=hashlib.sha256(result_json.encode()).hexdigest())
        values['identity_hash'] = _identity(values)
        prepared.append(values)
    imported, reused, output = 0, 0, []
    # Savepoint makes a mixed batch atomic even for non-HTTP callers.
    with db.begin_nested():
        for values in prepared:
            row = db.scalar(select(IncrementalReferenceResult).where(
                IncrementalReferenceResult.identity_hash == values['identity_hash']))
            if row is None:
                try:
                    with db.begin_nested():
                        row = IncrementalReferenceResult(**values,reason=reason,actor=actor)
                        db.add(row); db.flush()
                    imported += 1
                    _audit(db,'reference_import',row.id,actor,reason,identity_hash=row.identity_hash,
                        evidence_kind=row.evidence_kind,reference_source=row.reference_source)
                except IntegrityError:
                    row = db.scalar(select(IncrementalReferenceResult).where(
                        IncrementalReferenceResult.identity_hash == values['identity_hash']))
                    if row is None: raise
                    reused += 1
            else:
                reused += 1
            if any(getattr(row,key) != values[key] for key in ('result_hash','workflow_version_id','evidence_kind','reference_source')):
                raise EvidenceError('相同源版本参考结果不可覆盖',code='reference_identity_conflict',status_code=409)
            output.append({'id':row.id,'identity_hash':row.identity_hash,'result_hash':row.result_hash})
    return {'imported':imported,'reused':reused,'items':output}


def _differences(old: Any, new: Any, path='') -> list[dict]:
    numeric = lambda value: isinstance(value,(int,float)) and not isinstance(value,bool)
    if isinstance(old,dict) and isinstance(new,dict):
        output=[]
        for key in sorted(old.keys() | new.keys()):
            child=path+'/'+key.replace('~','~0').replace('/','~1')
            if key not in old or key not in new:
                output.append({'path':child,'kind':'missing_field','reference_present':key in old,
                    'shadow_present':key in new,'reference':old.get(key),'shadow':new.get(key)})
            else: output.extend(_differences(old[key],new[key],child))
        return output
    if isinstance(old,list) and isinstance(new,list):
        output=[]
        for index in range(max(len(old),len(new))):
            child=path+'/'+str(index)
            if index >= len(old) or index >= len(new):
                output.append({'path':child,'kind':'missing_item','reference_present':index < len(old),
                    'shadow_present':index < len(new),'reference':old[index] if index < len(old) else None,
                    'shadow':new[index] if index < len(new) else None})
            else: output.extend(_differences(old[index],new[index],child))
        return output
    if type(old) is type(new) and old == new or numeric(old) and numeric(new) and old == new:
        return []
    row={'path':path or '/','kind':'value' if type(old) is type(new) or numeric(old) and numeric(new) else 'type',
        'reference':old,'shadow':new}
    if numeric(old) and numeric(new):
        delta=new-old
        if isinstance(delta,int) or math.isfinite(delta): row['numeric_delta']=delta
    return [row]


def comparison_for_shadow(db, shadow, assignment):
    identity={'category_key':assignment.category_key,'workflow_definition_id':assignment.workflow_definition_id,
        'source_system':assignment.source_system,'entity_id':assignment.entity_id,
        'source_version':shadow.source_version,'step_key':shadow.step_key,'workflow_version_id':shadow.workflow_version_id}
    reference=db.scalar(select(IncrementalReferenceResult).where(IncrementalReferenceResult.identity_hash == _identity(identity)))
    current_records=list(db.scalars(select(ContentRecord).where(ContentRecord.source_system==assignment.source_system,
        ContentRecord.category_key==assignment.category_key,or_(ContentRecord.content_key==assignment.entity_id,
            ContentRecord.source_content_id==assignment.entity_id)).limit(2)))
    current=current_records[0] if len(current_records)==1 else None
    source_status=('deleted' if current.status=='deleted' else 'current' if current.source_version==shadow.source_version
        else 'historical') if current else 'unknown'
    run=db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.run_key == shadow.run_key))
    result={**identity,'shadow_id':shadow.id,'run_key':shadow.run_key,'workflow_version_id':shadow.workflow_version_id,
        'source_version_status':source_status,'created_at':platform_isoformat(_stored_utc(shadow.created_at)),'reference_id':reference.id if reference else None,
        'destination':'shadow','evidence_kind':('actual' if run.model_execution_mode == 'real' else 'synthetic') if run else 'unknown',
        'reference_evidence_kind':reference.evidence_kind if reference else None,
        'reference_source':reference.reference_source if reference else None,
        'comparison_status':'missing_reference','differences':[], 'differences_truncated':False}
    if reference is None:
        other_version=db.scalar(select(IncrementalReferenceResult.id).where(*[
            getattr(IncrementalReferenceResult,key)==value for key,value in identity.items() if key!='workflow_version_id'
        ],IncrementalReferenceResult.workflow_version_id!=shadow.workflow_version_id).limit(1))
        if other_version is not None:
            return {**result,'comparison_status':'incompatible','incompatibility_reason':'workflow_version_mismatch'}
        return result
    if reference.workflow_version_id != shadow.workflow_version_id:
        return {**result,'comparison_status':'incompatible','incompatibility_reason':'workflow_version_mismatch'}
    try:
        old,new=json.loads(reference.result_json),json.loads(shadow.result_json)
        _json(new)
    except (ValueError,TypeError,RecursionError):
        return {**result,'comparison_status':'incompatible','incompatibility_reason':'invalid_result'}
    if not isinstance(new,dict) or not old or not new:
        return {**result,'comparison_status':'incompatible','incompatibility_reason':'missing_result_contract'}
    differences=_differences(old,new)
    result.update(comparison_status='different' if differences else 'equal',differences=differences[:200],
        differences_truncated=len(differences)>200)
    return result


def list_comparisons(db, *, category_key, workflow_definition_id, before_id=None, limit=50, since=None, until=None):
    category_key=_scope(db,category_key,workflow_definition_id)
    _integer(limit,1,MAX_PAGE,'limit')
    query=select(IncrementalShadowResult,IncrementalEntityAssignment).join(IncrementalEntityAssignment,
        IncrementalEntityAssignment.id == IncrementalShadowResult.assignment_id).where(
        IncrementalEntityAssignment.category_key == category_key,
        IncrementalEntityAssignment.workflow_definition_id == workflow_definition_id)
    if before_id is not None:
        _integer(before_id,1,2147483647,'before_id')
        query=query.where(IncrementalShadowResult.id < before_id)
    if since is not None: query=query.where(IncrementalShadowResult.created_at >= _utc(since))
    if until is not None: query=query.where(IncrementalShadowResult.created_at < _utc(until))
    rows=list(db.execute(query.order_by(IncrementalShadowResult.id.desc()).limit(limit+1)))
    return {'items':[comparison_for_shadow(db,*row) for row in rows[:limit]],
        'next_before_id':rows[limit-1][0].id if len(rows)>limit else None,'truncated':len(rows)>limit}


def sampling_policy(db, *, category_key, workflow_definition_id):
    category_key=_scope(db,category_key,workflow_definition_id)
    row=db.scalar(select(IncrementalCapacitySamplingPolicy).where(
        IncrementalCapacitySamplingPolicy.category_key==category_key,
        IncrementalCapacitySamplingPolicy.workflow_definition_id==workflow_definition_id))
    return _policy_payload(row) if row else {'category_key':category_key,'workflow_definition_id':workflow_definition_id,
        'enabled':False,'revision':0,'interval_seconds':300,'next_sample_at':None}


def _policy_payload(row):
    return {'id':row.id,'category_key':row.category_key,'workflow_definition_id':row.workflow_definition_id,
        'enabled':row.enabled,'revision':row.revision,'interval_seconds':row.interval_seconds,
        'next_sample_at':platform_isoformat(_stored_utc(row.next_sample_at)),'updated_at':platform_isoformat(_stored_utc(row.updated_at))}


def configure_capacity_sampling(db, *, category_key, workflow_definition_id, enabled, expected_revision,
        interval_seconds, reason, actor, now=None):
    category_key=_scope(db,category_key,workflow_definition_id)
    _integer(interval_seconds,60,86400,'interval_seconds')
    _integer(expected_revision,0,2147483647,'expected_revision')
    if not isinstance(enabled,bool): raise EvidenceError('enabled 必须是布尔值')
    reason,actor=_text(reason,'reason',2000),_text(actor,'actor',80)
    now=_utc(now or datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    DatabaseCapabilities.for_dialect(db.get_bind().dialect.name).reserve_claim_transaction(db)
    row=db.scalar(select(IncrementalCapacitySamplingPolicy).where(
        IncrementalCapacitySamplingPolicy.category_key==category_key,
        IncrementalCapacitySamplingPolicy.workflow_definition_id==workflow_definition_id).with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    if (row.revision if row else 0)!=expected_revision:
        raise EvidenceError('采样配置已改变，请刷新后重试',code='sampling_revision_conflict',status_code=409)
    values=dict(enabled=enabled,interval_seconds=interval_seconds,revision=expected_revision+1,
        next_sample_at=now,updated_at=now,reason=reason,actor=actor)
    if row is None:
        row=IncrementalCapacitySamplingPolicy(category_key=category_key,workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id,**values)
        db.add(row)
    else:
        changed=db.execute(update(IncrementalCapacitySamplingPolicy).where(
            IncrementalCapacitySamplingPolicy.id==row.id, IncrementalCapacitySamplingPolicy.revision==expected_revision
        ).values(**values).execution_options(synchronize_session=False))
        if changed.rowcount != 1:
            raise EvidenceError('采样配置已改变，请刷新后重试',code='sampling_revision_conflict',status_code=409)
        db.refresh(row)
    db.flush()
    _audit(db,'sampling_configure',row.id,actor,reason,revision=row.revision,enabled=enabled,interval_seconds=interval_seconds)
    return _policy_payload(row)


def _backlog(db, policy, now):
    versions=select(WorkflowVersion.id).where(WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id==policy.workflow_definition_id)
    run_filters=(ProductionRun.category_key==policy.category_key,
        ProductionRun.workflow_definition_id==policy.workflow_definition_id,ProductionRun.source_type=='content_ingress_event',
        ProductionRun.status.not_in(['succeeded','failed','canceled']))
    pending,oldest=db.execute(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id),func.min(ProductionRun.created_at)).where(*run_filters)).one()
    packages,package_oldest=db.execute(select(func.count(MaterialPackage.id),func.min(MaterialPackage.created_at)).where(
        MaterialPackage.category_key==policy.category_key,MaterialPackage.workflow_version_id.in_(versions),
        MaterialPackage.status.in_(['incremental_pending','incremental_blocked']))).one()
    # Ledger join avoids attributing unrelated runs using the same workflow version.
    run_keys=select(ProductionRun.run_key).where(ProductionRun.category_key==policy.category_key,
        ProductionRun.workflow_definition_id==policy.workflow_definition_id,ProductionRun.source_type=='content_ingress_event')
    outbox_ids=select(DownstreamPublishLedger.outbox_event_id).where(DownstreamPublishLedger.origin_run_key.in_(run_keys))
    outbox,outbox_oldest=db.execute(select(func.count(RuntimeOutboxEvent.id),func.min(RuntimeOutboxEvent.created_at)).where(
        RuntimeOutboxEvent.id.in_(outbox_ids),RuntimeOutboxEvent.status.in_(['queued','leased']))).one()
    times=[_stored_utc(t) for t in (oldest,package_oldest,outbox_oldest) if t is not None]
    return {'source':'platform_database','pending_runs':pending,'pending_packages':packages,'pending_outbox':outbox,
        'backlog_work_units':pending+packages+outbox,'backlog_unit':'pending_stage_records_not_unique_entities',
        'oldest_age_seconds':max(0,(now-min(times)).total_seconds()) if times else None,
        'groups':_backlog_groups(db,policy,now,packages,package_oldest),
        'aggregate_acceptance_eligible':False,'evidence_kind':'mixed_platform_state','scope_limitations':['external_source_backlog_unknown','pre_dispatch_material_backlog_not_included']}


def collect_due_capacity_samples(db: Session, *, now=None) -> dict:
    now=_utc(now or datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    capabilities=DatabaseCapabilities.for_dialect(db.get_bind().dialect.name)
    capabilities.reserve_claim_transaction(db)
    query=select(IncrementalCapacitySamplingPolicy).where(IncrementalCapacitySamplingPolicy.enabled.is_(True),
        IncrementalCapacitySamplingPolicy.next_sample_at<=now).order_by(
        IncrementalCapacitySamplingPolicy.next_sample_at,IncrementalCapacitySamplingPolicy.id).limit(MAX_POLICIES_PER_TICK)
    policies=list(db.scalars(capabilities.claim_lock_statement(query)))
    for policy in policies:
        evidence=_backlog(db,policy,now)
        db.add(IncrementalCapacitySample(policy_id=policy.id,policy_revision=policy.revision,
            category_key=policy.category_key,workflow_definition_id=policy.workflow_definition_id,
            observed_at=now,evidence_json=_json(evidence)))
        policy.next_sample_at=now+timedelta(seconds=policy.interval_seconds)
    db.flush()
    return {'sampled':len(policies),'limit':MAX_POLICIES_PER_TICK}


def acceptance_report(db, *, category_key, workflow_definition_id, since, until, limit=100):
    category_key=_scope(db,category_key,workflow_definition_id)
    since,until=_utc(since),_utc(until)
    if until<=since or until-since>MAX_WINDOW: raise EvidenceError('报告时间窗必须大于零且不超过 31 天')
    _integer(limit,1,MAX_PAGE,'limit')
    run_scope=(ProductionRun.category_key==category_key,ProductionRun.workflow_definition_id==workflow_definition_id,
        ProductionRun.source_type=='content_ingress_event',ProductionRun.created_at>=since,ProductionRun.created_at<until)
    document=cast(ProductionRun.snapshot_json,JSON) if db.get_bind().dialect.name=='postgresql' else type_coerce(ProductionRun.snapshot_json,JSON)
    destination=func.coalesce(document['runtime_context']['incremental_route']['destination'].as_string(),'unknown')
    runs=[{'destination':dest,'evidence_kind':'actual' if mode=='real' else 'synthetic','status':status,'count':count}
        for dest,mode,status,count in db.execute(select(destination,ProductionRun.model_execution_mode,ProductionRun.status,
            func.count(ProductionRun.id)).where(*run_scope).group_by(destination,ProductionRun.model_execution_mode,ProductionRun.status))]
    costs=[{'destination':dest,'evidence_kind':'actual' if mode=='real' else 'synthetic','status':status,'error_code':error,
        'calls':count,'recorded_actual_micros':actual or 0,'reserved_estimate_micros':(estimate or 0) if status=='reserved' or error=='model_invocation_outcome_unknown' else 0,
        'unresolved_calls':count if status=='reserved' or error=='model_invocation_outcome_unknown' else 0}
        for dest,mode,status,error,count,actual,estimate in db.execute(select(destination,ProductionRun.model_execution_mode,
            WorkflowModelInvocation.status,WorkflowModelInvocation.error_code,func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id),
            func.sum(WorkflowModelInvocation.actual_cost_micros),func.sum(WorkflowModelInvocation.estimated_cost_micros)).join(
            ProductionRun,ProductionRun.id==WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id).where(*run_scope,
            WorkflowModelInvocation.created_at>=since,WorkflowModelInvocation.created_at<until).group_by(destination,
            ProductionRun.model_execution_mode,WorkflowModelInvocation.status,WorkflowModelInvocation.error_code).limit(1000))]
    ledgers=[{'destination':dest,'evidence_kind':'actual' if mode=='real' else 'synthetic','delivery_status':status,'count':count}
        for dest,mode,status,count in db.execute(select(destination,ProductionRun.model_execution_mode,DownstreamPublishLedger.delivery_status,
            func.count(DownstreamPublishLedger.id)).join(ProductionRun,ProductionRun.run_key==DownstreamPublishLedger.origin_run_key).where(
            *run_scope,DownstreamPublishLedger.created_at>=since,DownstreamPublishLedger.created_at<until).group_by(
            destination,ProductionRun.model_execution_mode,DownstreamPublishLedger.delivery_status))]
    rows=list(db.scalars(select(IncrementalCapacitySample).where(IncrementalCapacitySample.category_key==category_key,
        IncrementalCapacitySample.workflow_definition_id==workflow_definition_id,IncrementalCapacitySample.observed_at>=since,
        IncrementalCapacitySample.observed_at<until).order_by(IncrementalCapacitySample.observed_at.desc(),IncrementalCapacitySample.id.desc()).limit(limit+1)))
    truncated=len(rows)>limit
    rows=list(reversed(rows[:limit]))
    samples=[{'id':row.id,'policy_revision':row.policy_revision,'observed_at':platform_isoformat(_stored_utc(row.observed_at)),
        **json.loads(row.evidence_json)} for row in rows]
    slope,drain=_trend(rows,samples)
    groups=[]
    identities=sorted({(group['destination'],group['evidence_kind']) for sample in samples for group in sample.get('groups',[])})
    for dest,kind in identities:
        group_samples=[]
        group_rows=[]
        for row,sample in zip(rows,samples,strict=True):
            group=next((g for g in sample.get('groups',[]) if g['destination']==dest and g['evidence_kind']==kind),None)
            # Missing historical group evidence is unknown, not an inferred zero.
            if group is not None:
                group_samples.append(group);group_rows.append(row)
        group_slope,group_drain=_trend(group_rows,group_samples)
        groups.append({'destination':dest,'evidence_kind':kind,'sample_count':len(group_samples),
            'backlog_slope_per_second':group_slope,'observed_drain_seconds':group_drain,
            'latest':group_samples[-1] if group_samples else None})
    baseline_runs,baseline_truncated=_baseline_evidence(db,category_key,workflow_definition_id,since,until,min(limit,20))
    baseline_actual=[row for row in baseline_runs if row['evidence_kind']=='actual' and row['accuracy'] is not None]
    baseline_accuracy=baseline_actual[0]['accuracy'] if len(baseline_actual)==1 else None
    missing=[]
    if not baseline_actual: missing.append('actual_baseline_truth_metrics')
    if not any(group['evidence_kind']=='actual' and group['sample_count']>=2 for group in groups):
        missing.append('actual_capacity_samples')
