    if not any(row['evidence_kind']=='actual' and row['destination']=='formal' and row['delivery_status']=='delivered' for row in ledgers):
        missing.append('actual_formal_delivery')
    if truncated or baseline_truncated: missing.append('complete_untruncated_window')
    comparisons=list_comparisons(db,category_key=category_key,workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id,
        since=since,until=until,limit=limit)
    counts={status:sum(item['comparison_status']==status for item in comparisons['items'])
        for status in ('equal','different','missing_reference','incompatible')}
    return {'schema_version':1,'category_key':category_key,'workflow_definition_id':workflow_definition_id,
        'since':platform_isoformat(since),'until':platform_isoformat(until),'source':'platform_database',
        'acceptance_status':'evidence_available_for_review' if baseline_actual or any(row['evidence_kind']=='actual' for row in runs) else 'insufficient_evidence',
        'automatic_acceptance':False,'missing_evidence':missing,
        'cohort_definition':'runs_created_in_window; execution and delivery records also created in window',
        'runs':runs,'deliveries':ledgers,'cost':{'source':'workflow_model_invocations','invoice_reconciled':False,
            'recorded_amounts_may_include_conservative_estimates':True,'groups':costs,'truncated':len(costs)==1000},
        'quality':{'human_accuracy':None,'baseline_truth_accuracy':baseline_accuracy,'baseline_runs':baseline_runs,
            'baseline_truncated':baseline_truncated,'accuracy_source':'frozen_baseline_truth_not_external_population',
            'reference_agreement_is_accuracy':False,
            'comparison_counts':counts,'comparison_scope':'bounded_shadow_results_created_in_window',
            'truncated':comparisons['truncated'],'items':comparisons['items']},
        'capacity':{'samples':samples,'groups':groups,'aggregate_acceptance_eligible':False,'truncated':truncated,'backlog_slope_per_second':slope,
            'observed_drain_seconds':drain,'drain_measurement':'sampled_nonzero_to_zero_duration_not_exact_recovery_time',
            'external_source_backlog':None,'latest':samples[-1] if samples else None},
        'unknowns':['human_validated_accuracy','business_acceptance_thresholds','supplier_invoice_reconciliation',
            'external_source_backlog','exact_recovery_time'],
        'limitations':['reference_provenance_is_operator_declared','synthetic_evidence_is_not_production_acceptance',
            'run_and_delivery_statuses_are_current_state_not_historical_snapshots']}


def _destination_expression(db):
    document=cast(ProductionRun.snapshot_json,JSON) if db.get_bind().dialect.name=='postgresql' else type_coerce(ProductionRun.snapshot_json,JSON)
    return func.coalesce(document['runtime_context']['incremental_route']['destination'].as_string(),'unknown')


def _backlog_groups(db, policy, now, packages, package_oldest):
    destination=_destination_expression(db)
    scoped=(ProductionRun.category_key==policy.category_key,ProductionRun.workflow_definition_id==policy.workflow_definition_id,
        ProductionRun.source_type=='content_ingress_event')
    pending=ProductionRun.status.not_in(['succeeded','failed','canceled'])
    groups={}
    for dest,mode,count,oldest in db.execute(select(destination,ProductionRun.model_execution_mode,
            func.sum(case((pending,1),else_=0)),func.min(case((pending,ProductionRun.created_at),else_=None))).where(*scoped).group_by(
            destination,ProductionRun.model_execution_mode)):
        kind='actual' if mode=='real' else 'synthetic'
        groups[(dest,kind)]={'destination':dest,'evidence_kind':kind,'pending_runs':count or 0,'pending_outbox':0,
            'pending_packages':0,'backlog_work_units':count or 0,'oldest_age_seconds':
            max(0,(now-_stored_utc(oldest)).total_seconds()) if oldest else None}
    pending_outbox=RuntimeOutboxEvent.status.in_(['queued','leased'])
    # Aggregate distinct outbox records; the ledger is unique per revision/event.
    for dest,mode,count,oldest in db.execute(select(destination,ProductionRun.model_execution_mode,
            func.count(func.distinct(case((pending_outbox,RuntimeOutboxEvent.id),else_=None))),
            func.min(case((pending_outbox,RuntimeOutboxEvent.created_at),else_=None))).select_from(ProductionRun).join(
            DownstreamPublishLedger,DownstreamPublishLedger.origin_run_key==ProductionRun.run_key).join(
            RuntimeOutboxEvent,RuntimeOutboxEvent.id==DownstreamPublishLedger.outbox_event_id).where(*scoped).group_by(
            destination,ProductionRun.model_execution_mode)):
        kind='actual' if mode=='real' else 'synthetic'
        group=groups.setdefault((dest,kind),{'destination':dest,'evidence_kind':kind,'pending_runs':0,'pending_outbox':0,
            'pending_packages':0,'backlog_work_units':0,'oldest_age_seconds':None})
        group['pending_outbox']=count;group['backlog_work_units']+=count
        if oldest is not None:
            group['oldest_age_seconds']=max(group['oldest_age_seconds'] or 0,(now-_stored_utc(oldest)).total_seconds())
    # Packages do not yet have a frozen run execution mode; do not guess it.
    groups[('unassigned','unknown')]={'destination':'unassigned','evidence_kind':'unknown','pending_runs':0,
        'pending_outbox':0,'pending_packages':packages,'backlog_work_units':packages,
        'oldest_age_seconds':max(0,(now-_stored_utc(package_oldest)).total_seconds()) if package_oldest else None}
    return [groups[key] for key in sorted(groups)]


def _trend(rows,samples):
    if len(samples)<2 or rows[0].policy_revision!=rows[-1].policy_revision:
        return None,None
    elapsed=(_stored_utc(rows[-1].observed_at)-_stored_utc(rows[0].observed_at)).total_seconds()
    if elapsed<=0: return None,None
    slope=(samples[-1]['backlog_work_units']-samples[0]['backlog_work_units'])/elapsed
    start,drain=None,None
    for row,sample in zip(rows,samples,strict=True):
        if sample['backlog_work_units']>0 and start is None: start=_stored_utc(row.observed_at)
        elif sample['backlog_work_units']==0 and start is not None:
            drain=(_stored_utc(row.observed_at)-start).total_seconds();start=None
    return slope,drain


def _object_json(value):
    try:
        parsed=json.loads(value or '{}')
        _json(parsed)
        return parsed if isinstance(parsed,dict) else {}
    except (ValueError,TypeError,RecursionError): return {}


def _baseline_evidence(db,category_key,workflow_definition_id,since,until,limit):
    from .native_baseline_regression import compute_truth_metrics
    versions=select(WorkflowVersion.id).where(WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id==workflow_definition_id)
    runs=list(db.scalars(select(BaselineRegressionRun).where(BaselineRegressionRun.category_key==category_key,
        BaselineRegressionRun.workflow_version_id.in_(versions),BaselineRegressionRun.created_at>=since,
        BaselineRegressionRun.created_at<until).order_by(BaselineRegressionRun.id.desc()).limit(limit+1)))
    output=[]
    for run in runs[:limit]:
        items=list(db.scalars(select(BaselineRegressionItem).where(BaselineRegressionItem.run_id==run.id).order_by(
            BaselineRegressionItem.id).limit(MAX_PAGE+1)))
        truncated=len(items)>MAX_PAGE
        items=items[:MAX_PAGE]
        metric_rows=[]
        for item in items:
            expected=_object_json(item.expected_result_json)
            if not expected and item.expected_level: expected={'level':item.expected_level}
            snapshot=_object_json(item.result_snapshot_json)
            predicted=snapshot.get('normalized_result')
            # Legacy snapshots with only level remain explicit level evidence.
            if not isinstance(predicted,dict) and isinstance(snapshot.get('predicted_level'),str):
                predicted={'level':snapshot['predicted_level']}
            metric_rows.append({'status':'manual_required' if snapshot.get('manual_required') is True else item.status,
                'expected_result':expected,'predicted_result':predicted if isinstance(predicted,dict) else None})
        metrics=compute_truth_metrics(metric_rows)
        # Existing metric helpers use 0 for division by zero. This evidence API
        # distinguishes an observed zero rate from an absent denominator.
        aggregate=metrics.get('field_aggregate') or {}
        if not aggregate.get('compared'): aggregate['accuracy']=None
        if not aggregate.get('true_positive',0)+aggregate.get('false_positive',0): aggregate['precision']=None
        if not aggregate.get('true_positive',0)+aggregate.get('false_negative',0): aggregate['recall']=None
        if not metrics.get('total'): metrics['coverage']=None
        level_metrics=metrics.get('level_metrics')
        if level_metrics is not None and not level_metrics.get('denominator'):
            level_metrics['exact_accuracy']=None;level_metrics['adjacent_accuracy']=None
        # Having all item rows does not mean the run has produced every result.
        # Retain partial metrics for review, but withhold the cohort accuracy
        # until the frozen run and its items have actually finished.
        sample_complete=(not truncated and len(items)==run.total and run.status=='completed'
            and metrics.get('completed',0)==run.total)
        execution=_object_json(run.execution_snapshot_json)
        mode=execution.get('model_execution_mode')
        kind='actual' if mode=='real' else 'synthetic' if mode=='simulated' else 'unknown'
        fields=metrics.get('field_aggregate') or {}
        levels=metrics.get('level_metrics') or {}
        accuracy=fields.get('accuracy') if fields.get('compared',0)>0 else levels.get('exact_accuracy') if levels.get('denominator',0)>0 else None
        version=db.get(WorkflowVersion,run.workflow_version_id)
        output.append({'id':run.id,'baseline_set_id':run.baseline_set_id,'workflow_version_id':run.workflow_version_id,
            'workflow_version':version.version if version else None,'workflow_canonical_hash':version.canonical_hash if version else None,
            'execution_engine':run.execution_engine,'evidence_kind':kind,'destination':'baseline_regression',
            'status':run.status,'total':run.total,'completed':run.completed,'valid_predictions':run.valid_predictions,
            'failed':run.failed,'baseline_set_fingerprint':run.baseline_set_fingerprint,
            'correction_contract_hash':run.correction_contract_hash,'created_at':platform_isoformat(_stored_utc(run.created_at)),
            'metrics':metrics,'metrics_source':'frozen_baseline_regression_items','sample_count':len(items),
            'items_truncated':truncated,'sample_evidence_complete':sample_complete,
            'accuracy':accuracy if sample_complete else None,
            'stored_metrics':_object_json(run.metrics_json),
            'truth_contract_keys':sorted({item.truth_contract_key for item in items if item.truth_contract_key}),
            'field_contract_identities':execution.get('field_contract_identities',[]),
            'automatic_acceptance':False})
    return output,len(runs)>limit or any(row['items_truncated'] for row in output)

===== FILE backend/app/incremental_evidence_api.py =====
"""Authenticated operator endpoints for comparison and acceptance evidence."""
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any, Callable, Literal
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Path, Query
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError, OperationalError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from .authz import has_permission
from .database import get_db
from .models import User
from .incremental_evidence import (EvidenceError, import_reference_results, list_comparisons,
    sampling_policy, configure_capacity_sampling, acceptance_report)


class ReferenceItem(BaseModel):
    model_config=ConfigDict(extra='forbid')
    category_key: str=Field(min_length=1,max_length=40)
    workflow_definition_id: int=Field(ge=1,strict=True)
    workflow_version_id: int=Field(ge=1,strict=True)
    source_system: str=Field(min_length=1,max_length=120)
    entity_id: str=Field(min_length=1,max_length=320)
    source_version: str=Field(min_length=1,max_length=200)
    step_key: str=Field(min_length=1,max_length=120)
    result: dict[str,Any]
    evidence_kind: Literal['actual','synthetic']
    reference_source: str=Field(min_length=1,max_length=500)


class ReferenceImportRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config=ConfigDict(extra='forbid')
    items: list[ReferenceItem]=Field(min_length=1,max_length=100)
    reason: str=Field(min_length=1,max_length=2000)


class CapacitySamplingRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config=ConfigDict(extra='forbid')
    enabled: bool=Field(strict=True)
    expected_revision: int=Field(ge=0,strict=True)
    interval_seconds: int=Field(ge=60,le=86400,strict=True)
    reason: str=Field(min_length=1,max_length=2000)


def _authorize(user, write=False):
    permission='incremental_mechanisms:activate' if write else 'reports:read'
    if not has_permission(user,permission):
        raise HTTPException(403,detail={'code':'incremental_evidence_forbidden','message':'当前账号无权执行该证据操作'})


def _call(db, operation, *, write=False):
    try:
        result=operation()
        if write: db.commit()
        return result
    except EvidenceError as exc:
        if write: db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(exc.status_code,detail={'code':exc.code,'message':str(exc)}) from exc
    except (IntegrityError,OperationalError) as exc:
        db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(409,detail={'code':'incremental_evidence_conflict','message':'并发操作冲突，请刷新后重试'}) from exc


def build_incremental_evidence_router(require_user: Callable[..., Any]) -> APIRouter:
    router=APIRouter(prefix='/api/incremental-evidence',tags=['incremental-evidence'])

    @router.post('/references')
    def import_references(payload: ReferenceImportRequest, user: User=Depends(require_user), db: Session=Depends(get_db)):
        _authorize(user,True)
        return _call(db,lambda: import_reference_results(db,items=[item.model_dump() for item in payload.items],
            reason=payload.reason,actor=user.username),write=True)

    @router.get('/comparisons')
    def comparisons(category_key: str=Query(min_length=1,max_length=40), workflow_definition_id: int=Query(ge=1),
            before_id: int|None=Query(default=None,ge=1),limit: int=Query(default=50,ge=1,le=200),
            user: User=Depends(require_user),db: Session=Depends(get_db)):
        _authorize(user)
        return _call(db,lambda: list_comparisons(db,category_key=category_key,workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id,
            before_id=before_id,limit=limit))

    @router.get('/sampling/{category_key}/{workflow_definition_id}')
    def get_sampling(category_key: str=Path(min_length=1,max_length=40),workflow_definition_id: int=Path(ge=1),
            user: User=Depends(require_user),db: Session=Depends(get_db)):
        _authorize(user)
        return _call(db,lambda: sampling_policy(db,category_key=category_key,workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id))

    @router.put('/sampling/{category_key}/{workflow_definition_id}')
    def put_sampling(payload: CapacitySamplingRequest,category_key: str=Path(min_length=1,max_length=40),
            workflow_definition_id: int=Path(ge=1),user: User=Depends(require_user),db: Session=Depends(get_db)):
        _authorize(user,True)
        return _call(db,lambda: configure_capacity_sampling(db,category_key=category_key,
            workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id,**payload.model_dump(),actor=user.username),write=True)

    @router.get('/report')
    def report(since: datetime,until: datetime,category_key: str=Query(min_length=1,max_length=40),
            workflow_definition_id: int=Query(ge=1),limit: int=Query(default=100,ge=1,le=200),
            user: User=Depends(require_user),db: Session=Depends(get_db)):
        _authorize(user)
        return _call(db,lambda: acceptance_report(db,category_key=category_key,workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id,
            since=since,until=until,limit=limit))
    return router

===== FILE backend/app/incremental_evidence_models.py =====
"""Additive schema 105: immutable reference imports and opt-in capacity evidence."""
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from sqlalchemy import Boolean, CheckConstraint, DateTime, ForeignKey, Integer, String, Text, UniqueConstraint
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column
from .database import Base


def utcnow():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)


class IncrementalReferenceResult(Base):
    __tablename__ = 'incremental_reference_results'
    __table_args__ = (CheckConstraint("evidence_kind IN ('actual','synthetic')", name='ck_incremental_reference_kind'),)
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    identity_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), unique=True)
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    workflow_definition_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey('workflow_definitions.id', ondelete='RESTRICT'), index=True)
    workflow_version_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey('workflow_versions.id', ondelete='RESTRICT'))
    source_system: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120))
    entity_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(320))
    source_version: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200))
    step_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120))
    result_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    result_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64))
    evidence_kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20))
    reference_source: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(500))
    reason: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    actor: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)


class IncrementalCapacitySamplingPolicy(Base):
    __tablename__ = 'incremental_capacity_sampling_policies'
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint('category_key','workflow_definition_id', name='uq_incremental_capacity_scope'),
        CheckConstraint('interval_seconds >= 60 AND interval_seconds <= 86400', name='ck_incremental_capacity_interval'),)
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    workflow_definition_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey('workflow_definitions.id', ondelete='RESTRICT'), index=True)
    enabled: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False)
    revision: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=1)
    interval_seconds: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=300)
    next_sample_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), index=True)
    reason: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    actor: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80))
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)


class IncrementalCapacitySample(Base):
    __tablename__ = 'incremental_capacity_samples'
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint('policy_id','observed_at', name='uq_incremental_capacity_observation'),)
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    policy_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey('incremental_capacity_sampling_policies.id', ondelete='RESTRICT'), index=True)
    policy_revision: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    workflow_definition_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey('workflow_definitions.id', ondelete='RESTRICT'), index=True)
    observed_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), index=True)
    evidence_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)

===== FILE backend/app/incremental_input.py =====
"""Immutable input evidence for content-ingress workflow runs.

The ``ContentRecord`` row is a latest-state projection.  It must never be used
to reconstruct an older queued event, because a later version of the same
content can update its asset and source version while the earlier event waits
for a batch.  This module freezes the event-specific input into the material
package and validates that evidence without consulting mutable projection
fields.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from collections.abc import Mapping
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .models import (
    Asset,
    AssetVersion,
    ContentIngressEvent,
    ContentRecord,
    MaterialPackage,
    MaterialPackageItem,
)


INPUT_SCHEMA_VERSION = "content-ingress-runtime-input-v1"
# 上游素材类型字段名：与基线真值 workflow_input.material_type、前置短路模块 field_path 默认值一致
UPSTREAM_MATERIAL_TYPE_FIELD = "material_type"
MAX_INCREMENTAL_BATCH_SIZE = 1_000


class IncrementalInputError(ValueError):
    pass


class IncrementalCaseAwaitingMaterial(IncrementalInputError):
    """A normalized case event is valid but not yet complete enough to run."""


def _frozen_case_input(
    db: Session,
    *,
    payload: Mapping[str, Any],
    category_key: str,
    content_id: str,
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], list[Asset]] | None:
    raw_case = payload.get("case")
    if raw_case is None:
        return None
    if not isinstance(raw_case, Mapping):
        raise IncrementalInputError("payload.case 必须是对象")
    case = dict(raw_case)
    if case.get("id") != content_id:
        raise IncrementalInputError("payload.case.id 必须与 content_id 相同")
    expected = case.get("expected_image_ids")
    images = case.get("images")
    if (
        not isinstance(expected, list)
        or not expected
        or any(not isinstance(value, str) or not value for value in expected)
        or len(set(expected)) != len(expected)
    ):
        raise IncrementalInputError("payload.case.expected_image_ids 必须是非空且不重复的图片身份数组")
    if case.get("ready") is not True:
        raise IncrementalCaseAwaitingMaterial("案例尚未声明齐备")
    if not isinstance(images, list):
        raise IncrementalCaseAwaitingMaterial("案例图片尚未齐备")
    actual_ids = [item.get("cloud_id") if isinstance(item, Mapping) else None for item in images]
    if actual_ids != expected:
        raise IncrementalCaseAwaitingMaterial("案例图片集合与齐备声明不一致")

    assets: list[Asset] = []
    for image in images:
        if not isinstance(image, Mapping):
            raise IncrementalInputError("payload.case.images 每项必须是对象")
        asset_id = image.get("asset_id")
        sha256 = image.get("asset_sha256")
        if (
            isinstance(asset_id, bool)
            or not isinstance(asset_id, int)
            or asset_id < 1
            or not isinstance(sha256, str)
            or len(sha256) != 64
        ):
            raise IncrementalInputError("案例图片缺少有效 asset_id/asset_sha256")
        asset = db.get(Asset, asset_id)
        if asset is None or asset.status == "deleted":
            raise IncrementalCaseAwaitingMaterial("案例图片素材尚未齐备")
        if asset.category_key != category_key or asset.sha256 != sha256:
            raise IncrementalInputError("案例图片与本地不可变素材证据不一致")
        assets.append(asset)
    if len({asset.id for asset in assets}) != len(assets):
        raise IncrementalInputError("案例图片不能重复绑定同一素材")
    return case, assets


def _json_object(raw: str | None, *, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        value = json.loads(raw or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise IncrementalInputError(f"{label}损坏") from exc
    if not isinstance(value, dict):
        raise IncrementalInputError(f"{label}不是对象")
    return value


def frozen_input_hash(input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(
        canonical_json(dict(input_manifest)).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()


def incremental_lane_key(
    *,
    category_key: str,
    workflow_version_id: int,
    snapshot_hash: str,
) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(
        f"{category_key}:{workflow_version_id}:{snapshot_hash}".encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()


def incremental_autorun_policy(
    workflow_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    workflow = workflow_snapshot.get("workflow")
    manifest = workflow.get("manifest") if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else None
    resource_policy = (
        manifest.get("resource_policy") if isinstance(manifest, Mapping) else None
    )
    configured = (
        resource_policy.get("incremental_autorun")
        if isinstance(resource_policy, Mapping)
        else None
    )
    configured = dict(configured) if isinstance(configured, Mapping) else {}
    mode = 'real' if configured.get('model_execution_mode') == 'real' else 'simulated'
    runtime = workflow_snapshot.get('runtime_context')
    runtime = runtime if isinstance(runtime, Mapping) else {}
    execution = runtime.get('incremental_execution_policy')
    if execution is not None:
        if (not isinstance(execution, Mapping)
                or execution.get('authority') != 'incremental_binding'
                or runtime.get('run_mode') != 'incremental'
                or type(execution.get('binding_id')) is not int or execution['binding_id'] < 1
                or type(execution.get('binding_lock_version')) is not int or execution['binding_lock_version'] < 1
                or execution['binding_id'] != runtime.get('incremental_binding_id')
                or execution['binding_lock_version'] != runtime.get('incremental_binding_lock_version')
                or execution.get('model_execution_mode') not in ('real', 'simulated')):
            raise IncrementalInputError('增量运行策略与冻结绑定身份或版本不一致')
        if configured.get('model_execution_mode') != 'simulated':
            mode = execution['model_execution_mode']
    batch_size = configured.get("batch_size", 1)
    max_wait_seconds = configured.get("max_wait_seconds", 0)
    priorities = configured.get("immediate_priorities", ["P0"])
    if (
        isinstance(batch_size, bool)
        or not isinstance(batch_size, int)
        or not 1 <= batch_size <= MAX_INCREMENTAL_BATCH_SIZE
    ):
        raise IncrementalInputError(
            f"自动增量 batch_size 必须在 1..{MAX_INCREMENTAL_BATCH_SIZE} 之间"
        )
    if (
        isinstance(max_wait_seconds, bool)
        or not isinstance(max_wait_seconds, int)
        or not 0 <= max_wait_seconds <= 604_800
    ):
        raise IncrementalInputError(
            "自动增量 max_wait_seconds 必须在 0..604800 之间"
        )
    if not isinstance(priorities, list) or any(
        not isinstance(item, str) or not item for item in priorities
    ):
        raise IncrementalInputError(
            "自动增量 immediate_priorities 必须是字符串数组"
        )
    return {
        "enabled": (configured.get("enabled", True) is True
                    and not (execution is None and mode == 'real' and configured.get('enabled') is not True)),
        "batch_size": batch_size,
        "max_wait_seconds": max_wait_seconds,
        "immediate_priorities": tuple(priorities),
        "model_execution_mode": mode,
    }


def build_frozen_content_ingress_input(
    db: Session,
    *,
    event: ContentIngressEvent,
    record: ContentRecord,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Freeze the exact event/version/asset evidence at package creation."""

    payload = _json_object(event.payload_json, label="内容接入事件载荷")
    content_id = payload.get("content_id")
    source_version = payload.get("content_version")
    category_key = payload.get("category_key")
    if (
        not isinstance(content_id, str)
        or not content_id
        or not isinstance(source_version, str)
        or not source_version
        or not isinstance(category_key, str)
        or not category_key
        or event.content_record_id != record.id
        or record.source_system != event.source_system
        or record.source_content_id != content_id
    ):
        raise IncrementalInputError("内容接入事件缺少可冻结的来源身份")

    frozen_case = _frozen_case_input(
        db,
        payload=payload,
        category_key=category_key,
        content_id=content_id,
    )
    if frozen_case is not None:
        case, assets = frozen_case
        anchor = assets[0]
        frozen_content_key = f"{event.source_system}:{content_id}"
        identity_snapshot = _json_object(
            event.identity_snapshot_json,
            label="内容接入身份快照",
        ) if event.identity_snapshot_json else {}
        if isinstance(identity_snapshot.get("content_key"), str) and identity_snapshot["content_key"]:
            frozen_content_key = identity_snapshot["content_key"]
        frozen_case_input = {
            "schema_version": INPUT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
            "content_ingress_event_db_id": event.id,
            "content_ingress_event_id": event.event_id,
            "content_record_id": record.id,
            "content_key": frozen_content_key,
            "source_system": event.source_system,
            "source_content_id": content_id,
            "category_key": category_key,
            "asset_id": anchor.id,
            "asset_version_id": None,
            "asset_sha256": anchor.sha256,
            "storage_backend": anchor.storage_backend,
            "source_uri": anchor.source_uri,
            "mime_type": anchor.mime_type,
            "size_bytes": anchor.size_bytes,
            "source_version": source_version,
            "case": case,
        }
        return _with_upstream_material_type(frozen_case_input, payload)

    payload_asset_id = payload.get("asset_id")
    if payload_asset_id is not None and (
        isinstance(payload_asset_id, bool)
        or not isinstance(payload_asset_id, int)
    ):
        raise IncrementalInputError("内容接入事件 asset_id 无效")

    identity_snapshot = _json_object(
        event.identity_snapshot_json,
        label="内容接入身份快照",
    ) if event.identity_snapshot_json else {}
    asset_binding = identity_snapshot.get("asset_binding")
    asset_binding = (
        dict(asset_binding) if isinstance(asset_binding, Mapping) else None
    )
    binding_asset_id = (
        asset_binding.get("asset_id") if asset_binding is not None else None
    )
    if binding_asset_id is not None and (
        isinstance(binding_asset_id, bool)
        or not isinstance(binding_asset_id, int)
        or binding_asset_id < 1
    ):
        raise IncrementalInputError("内容接入事件素材证据无效")

    version = db.scalar(
        select(AssetVersion).where(
            AssetVersion.source_system == event.source_system,
            AssetVersion.source_content_id == content_id,
            AssetVersion.source_version == source_version,
        )
    )
    selected_asset_id = (
        version.asset_id
        if version is not None
        else payload_asset_id
        if payload_asset_id is not None
        else binding_asset_id
    )
    if selected_asset_id is None:
        raise IncrementalInputError("内容接入事件缺少事件级素材证据")
    if payload_asset_id is not None and payload_asset_id != selected_asset_id:
        raise IncrementalInputError("内容接入事件与不可变素材版本不一致")
    if binding_asset_id is not None and binding_asset_id != selected_asset_id:
        raise IncrementalInputError("内容接入事件冻结素材证据不一致")

    asset = db.get(Asset, selected_asset_id)
    if asset is None or asset.status == "deleted":
        raise IncrementalInputError("内容接入素材尚不可运行")
    if asset.category_key != category_key:
        raise IncrementalInputError("内容接入素材与事件类目不一致")
    if version is not None and version.asset_id != asset.id:
        raise IncrementalInputError("内容接入素材版本与素材不一致")

    frozen_content_key = identity_snapshot.get("content_key")
    if not isinstance(frozen_content_key, str) or not frozen_content_key:
        frozen_content_key = f"{event.source_system}:{content_id}"

    evidence = (
        {
            "asset_sha256": version.asset_sha256,
            "storage_backend": version.storage_backend,
            "source_uri": version.source_uri,
            "mime_type": version.mime_type,
            "size_bytes": version.size_bytes,
        }
        if version is not None
        else asset_binding
        if asset_binding is not None
        else {
            "asset_sha256": asset.sha256,
            "storage_backend": asset.storage_backend,
            "source_uri": asset.source_uri,
            "mime_type": asset.mime_type,
            "size_bytes": asset.size_bytes,
        }
    )
    frozen = {
        "schema_version": INPUT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "content_ingress_event_db_id": event.id,
        "content_ingress_event_id": event.event_id,
        "content_record_id": record.id,
        "content_key": frozen_content_key,
        "source_system": event.source_system,
        "source_content_id": content_id,
        "category_key": category_key,
        "asset_id": asset.id,
        "asset_version_id": version.id if version is not None else None,
        "asset_sha256": evidence.get("asset_sha256"),
        "storage_backend": evidence.get("storage_backend"),
        "source_uri": evidence.get("source_uri"),
        "mime_type": evidence.get("mime_type"),
        "size_bytes": evidence.get("size_bytes"),
        "source_version": source_version,
    }
    return _with_upstream_material_type(frozen, payload)


def _with_upstream_material_type(
    frozen: dict[str, Any], payload: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """上游事件带了素材类型（用户手动选的，如「全景图」）就原样冻结进输入，供前置短路节点判定。"""
    material_type = payload.get(UPSTREAM_MATERIAL_TYPE_FIELD)
    if isinstance(material_type, str) and material_type.strip():
        frozen[UPSTREAM_MATERIAL_TYPE_FIELD] = material_type.strip()
    elif material_type is not None and not isinstance(material_type, str):
        raise IncrementalInputError("内容接入事件 material_type 必须是字符串")
    if frozen.get('source_system') == 'factory-test-material':
        frozen['factory_task'] = validate_factory_task_identity(frozen, payload.get('factory_task'))
        from .factory_classification import MAPPING_VERSION, factory_class_name
        task = frozen['factory_task']
        # Factory's numeric class is authoritative. Do not let a conflicting
        # free-text value turn an unknown/SU class into a panorama.
        frozen.pop(UPSTREAM_MATERIAL_TYPE_FIELD, None)
        label = factory_class_name(task['res_type'], task['class_id_1'])
        if label:
            frozen[UPSTREAM_MATERIAL_TYPE_FIELD] = label
        frozen['factory_class_mapping_version'] = MAPPING_VERSION
        frozen['source_time_kind'] = 'platform_first_observed'
    if frozen.get('source_system') == 'inspiration-factory-material':
        from .inspiration_factory_identity import validate_frozen_identity
        try:
            task = validate_frozen_identity(frozen, payload.get('inspiration_task'))
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise IncrementalInputError('灵感图任务身份与内容来源不一致') from exc
        material = payload.get('material')
        if not isinstance(material, Mapping) or material.get('url') != task['image_url']:
            raise IncrementalInputError('灵感图领取图片与素材来源不一致')
        frozen['inspiration_task'] = task
        frozen['source_time_kind'] = 'platform_first_observed'
    return frozen


def validate_factory_task_identity(frozen: Mapping[str, Any], task: Any) -> dict[str, int]:
    """Keep downstream task association bound to the frozen content identity."""
    keys = ('task_id', 'res_type', 'll_id', 'img_id', 'class_id_1')
    if (not isinstance(task, Mapping) or any(
            type(task.get(key)) is not int or not 0 < task[key] <= 2**63-1 for key in keys)
            or task['res_type'] not in (1, 6)):
        raise IncrementalInputError('Factory任务身份无效')
    from .factory_test_claim import UPSTREAMS
    # The identity prefix and the version prefix must come from the same
    # upstream. A production content id carrying a test version string, or the
    # reverse, is a mismatch even though each half looks individually valid.
    for prefixes in UPSTREAMS.values():
        identity = (f"{prefixes.identity}:{task['res_type']}:{task['ll_id']}"
                    f":{task['img_id']}:task:{task['task_id']}")
        if frozen.get('source_content_id') != identity:
            continue
        if frozen.get('source_version') != prefixes.version + str(task['task_id']):
            raise IncrementalInputError('Factory任务身份与内容身份不一致')
        return {key: task[key] for key in keys}
    raise IncrementalInputError('Factory任务身份与内容身份不一致')


def validate_frozen_content_ingress_input(
    db: Session,
    *,
    event: ContentIngressEvent,
    input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    input_manifest_hash: str,
    package_category_key: str,
    package_item_asset_id: int | None = None,
    package_item_asset_ids: list[int] | None = None,
) -> None:
    """Validate a frozen package input against immutable event/asset facts."""

    manifest = dict(input_manifest)
    payload = _json_object(event.payload_json, label="内容接入事件载荷")
    content_id = payload.get("content_id")
    source_version = payload.get("content_version")
    category_key = payload.get("category_key")
    if (
        manifest.get("schema_version") != INPUT_SCHEMA_VERSION
        or manifest.get("content_ingress_event_db_id") != event.id
        or manifest.get("content_ingress_event_id") != event.event_id
        or manifest.get("content_record_id") != event.content_record_id
        or manifest.get("source_system") != event.source_system
        or manifest.get("source_content_id") != content_id
        or manifest.get("source_version") != source_version
        or manifest.get("category_key") != category_key
        or category_key != package_category_key
        or frozen_input_hash(manifest) != input_manifest_hash
    ):
        raise IncrementalInputError("冻结增量输入与接入事件不一致")

    if manifest.get('source_system') == 'factory-test-material':
        expected_task = validate_factory_task_identity(manifest, payload.get('factory_task'))
        if (manifest.get('factory_task') != expected_task or
                manifest.get('source_time_kind') != 'platform_first_observed'):
            raise IncrementalInputError('Factory冻结任务身份与接入事件不一致')

    if manifest.get('source_system') == 'inspiration-factory-material':
        expected = _with_upstream_material_type(dict(manifest), payload)
        if (manifest.get('inspiration_task') != expected.get('inspiration_task')
                or manifest.get('source_time_kind') != 'platform_first_observed'):
            raise IncrementalInputError('灵感图冻结任务身份与接入事件不一致')

    if "case" in manifest:
        rebuilt = build_frozen_content_ingress_input(
            db,
            event=event,
            record=event.content_record,
        )
        if canonical_json(rebuilt) != canonical_json(manifest):
            raise IncrementalInputError("冻结案例输入与事件证据不一致")
        image_ids = [int(item["asset_id"]) for item in manifest["case"]["images"]]
        supplied_ids = package_item_asset_ids
        if supplied_ids is None:
            package = db.scalar(
                select(MaterialPackage).where(
                    MaterialPackage.incremental_event_id == event.id
                )
            )
            if package is not None:
                supplied_ids = list(
                    db.scalars(
                        select(MaterialPackageItem.asset_id)
                        .where(MaterialPackageItem.package_id == package.id)
                        .order_by(MaterialPackageItem.position)
                    ).all()
                )
        if supplied_ids is None and package_item_asset_id is not None:
            supplied_ids = [package_item_asset_id]
        if supplied_ids != image_ids:
            raise IncrementalInputError("冻结案例图片与素材包项目不一致")
        return

    if manifest.get("asset_id") != package_item_asset_id:
        raise IncrementalInputError("冻结增量输入与素材包项目不一致")

    asset_id = manifest.get("asset_id")
    if isinstance(asset_id, bool) or not isinstance(asset_id, int):
        raise IncrementalInputError("冻结增量输入缺少素材身份")
    asset = db.get(Asset, asset_id)
    if asset is None or asset.status == "deleted":
        raise IncrementalInputError("冻结增量输入素材不可用")

    version_id = manifest.get("asset_version_id")
    version = None
    if version_id is not None:
        if isinstance(version_id, bool) or not isinstance(version_id, int):
            raise IncrementalInputError("冻结增量输入的素材版本无效")
        version = db.get(AssetVersion, version_id)
        if version is None:
            raise IncrementalInputError("冻结增量输入的素材版本不存在")

    payload_asset_id = payload.get("asset_id")
    identity_snapshot = _json_object(
        event.identity_snapshot_json,
        label="内容接入身份快照",
    ) if event.identity_snapshot_json else {}
    asset_binding = identity_snapshot.get("asset_binding")
    asset_binding = (
        dict(asset_binding) if isinstance(asset_binding, Mapping) else None
    )
    if version is None and payload_asset_id is None and asset_binding is None:
        raise IncrementalInputError("冻结增量输入缺少事件级素材证据")
    if payload_asset_id is not None and payload_asset_id != asset_id:
        raise IncrementalInputError("冻结增量输入与事件素材身份不一致")
    if asset_binding is not None and asset_binding.get("asset_id") != asset_id:
        raise IncrementalInputError("冻结增量输入与事件素材证据不一致")

    expected = (
        {
            "asset_sha256": version.asset_sha256,
            "storage_backend": version.storage_backend,
            "source_uri": version.source_uri,
            "mime_type": version.mime_type,
            "size_bytes": version.size_bytes,
        }
        if version is not None
        else {
            key: asset_binding.get(key)
            for key in (
                "asset_sha256",
                "storage_backend",
                "source_uri",
                "mime_type",
                "size_bytes",
            )
        }
        if asset_binding is not None
        else {
            "asset_sha256": asset.sha256,
            "storage_backend": asset.storage_backend,
            "source_uri": asset.source_uri,
            "mime_type": asset.mime_type,
            "size_bytes": asset.size_bytes,
        }
    )
    if any(manifest.get(key) != value for key, value in expected.items()):
        raise IncrementalInputError("冻结增量输入的素材证据发生漂移")
    current_asset_evidence = {
        "asset_sha256": asset.sha256,
        "storage_backend": asset.storage_backend,
        "source_uri": asset.source_uri,
        "mime_type": asset.mime_type,
        "size_bytes": asset.size_bytes,
    }
    if version is None and current_asset_evidence != expected:
        raise IncrementalInputError("冻结增量输入的素材事实发生漂移")
    if version is not None and (
        version.asset_id != asset.id
        or version.source_system != event.source_system
        or version.source_content_id != content_id
        or version.source_version != source_version
    ):
        raise IncrementalInputError("冻结增量输入的不可变版本归属不一致")

===== FILE backend/app/incremental_material_api.py =====
"""Authenticated operators submit immutable material manifests and retry failures."""
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .authz import has_permission
from .database import get_db
from .incremental_material_models import IncrementalMaterialJob
from .incremental_materials import MaterialError, material_job_payload, retry_material_job, submit_manifest


class MaterialSubmitRequest(BaseModel):
    source_system: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=80)
    submission_id: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=160)
    occurred_at: datetime
    payload: dict[str, Any]


class MaterialRetryRequest(BaseModel):
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=500)


def build_incremental_material_router(require_user):
    router = APIRouter(prefix='/api/incremental-materials', tags=['incremental-materials'])

    def writer(user=Depends(require_user)):
        if not has_permission(user, 'assets:write'):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail='缺少权限：assets:write')
        return user

    @router.get('/jobs')
    def list_jobs(category_key: str | None = None, status: str | None = None,
                  before_id: int | None = Query(default=None, ge=1), limit: int = Query(default=50, ge=1, le=100),
                  user=Depends(require_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        query = select(IncrementalMaterialJob)
        if category_key: query = query.where(IncrementalMaterialJob.category_key == category_key)
        if status: query = query.where(IncrementalMaterialJob.status == status)
        if before_id: query = query.where(IncrementalMaterialJob.id < before_id)
        rows = db.scalars(query.order_by(IncrementalMaterialJob.id.desc()).limit(limit)).all()
        return {'items': [material_job_payload(row) for row in rows], 'next_before_id': rows[-1].id if len(rows) == limit else None}

    @router.get('/jobs/{job_id}')
    def get_job(job_id: int, user=Depends(require_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        job = db.get(IncrementalMaterialJob, job_id)
        if job is None: raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail='material_job_not_found')
        return material_job_payload(job)

    @router.post('/jobs')
    def submit(body: MaterialSubmitRequest, user=Depends(writer), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        try:
            row = submit_manifest(db, **body.model_dump(), actor=user.username)
            db.commit()
            return material_job_payload(row)
        except MaterialError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(status_code=exc.status_code, detail=exc.code) from None

    @router.post('/jobs/{job_id}/retry')
    def retry(job_id: int, body: MaterialRetryRequest, user=Depends(require_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        if not has_permission(user, 'production:rerun'):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail='缺少权限：production:rerun')
        try:
            row = retry_material_job(db, job_id, actor=user.username, reason=body.reason)
            db.commit()
            return material_job_payload(row)
        except MaterialError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(status_code=exc.status_code, detail=exc.code) from None

    return router

===== FILE backend/app/incremental_material_models.py =====
"""Durable material manifests. Input JSON is immutable after submission."""
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from sqlalchemy import DateTime, ForeignKey, Index, Integer, String, Text, UniqueConstraint
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from .database import Base


class IncrementalMaterialJob(Base):
    __tablename__ = "incremental_material_jobs"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("source_system", "submission_id", name="uq_material_submission"),
        UniqueConstraint("identity_hash", name="uq_material_source_version"),
        Index("ix_material_due", "status", "retry_at", "id"),
    )
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    source_system: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80))
    submission_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160))
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    source_content_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160))
    source_version: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120))
    identity_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64))
    manifest_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64))
    manifest_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    resolved_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}")
    validation_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}", server_default="{}")
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(30), default="queued")
    attempt_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    retry_generation: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    error_code: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(80), nullable=True)
    routing_status: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(80), nullable=True)
    event_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("content_ingress_events.id"), nullable=True)
    occurred_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    retry_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80))

===== FILE backend/app/incremental_materials.py =====
"""Bounded, durable image acquisition followed by the real content ingress path.

No network capability exists until deployment explicitly configures a source.
A job claim is a transactional compare/update: a crashed transaction releases
its claim and retries the same immutable manifest on the next worker tick.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import hashlib
import http.client
import io
import ipaddress
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import socket
import ssl
import tempfile
import time
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

from PIL import Image, ImageOps, UnidentifiedImageError
from sqlalchemy import or_, select, update
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError

from .audit import append_audit_event, canonical_json
from .incremental_material_models import IncrementalMaterialJob
from .models import Asset, ContentRecord, ContentIngressEvent, EvaluationCategoryProfile

MAX_JOBS_PER_TICK = 4
MAX_ATTEMPTS = 3
MAX_TICK_SECONDS = 20


class MaterialError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code, *, status_code=409):
        super().__init__(code)
        self.code, self.status_code = code, status_code


class OriginalStoreUnavailable(OSError):
    """原图目录（机械盘挂载点）没到位：可重试，不能当成素材本身的错误。"""
    code = 'material_original_store_unavailable'


# 增量原图落到上传目录下的这个子目录（.85 上是绑定到机械盘的挂载点）。不配就沿用上传目录根，
# 配了就必须在该目录看到标记文件，证明挂载到位——否则宁可让任务重试，也不悄悄写回固态盘。
ORIGINAL_STORE_ENV = 'LABEL_SYSTEM_ORIGINAL_STORE_SUBDIR'
ORIGINAL_STORE_MARKER = '.label-original-store'


def _aware(value):
    return value if value.tzinfo else value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)


def _hash(value):
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(value).encode()).hexdigest()


def _policy(source):
    try:
        config = json.loads(os.environ.get('INCREMENTAL_MATERIAL_SOURCES_JSON') or '{}')
        value = config.get(source, {})
        hosts = value.get('allowed_hosts', [])
        if not isinstance(hosts, list) or any(not isinstance(h, str) or not h for h in hosts):
            raise ValueError()
        insecure = value.get('insecure_http', False)
        if type(insecure) is not bool:
            raise ValueError()
        return {'allowed_hosts': [h.lower() for h in hosts],
                'max_bytes': min(25 * 1024 * 1024, max(1, int(value.get('max_bytes', 25 * 1024 * 1024)))),
                'max_images': min(100, max(1, int(value.get('max_images', 32)))),
                # 像素上限：默认 4000 万；灵感图（Pinterest 原图常见 7000 万+）可按来源放宽到 1.2 亿，
                # 解码时按 JPEG draft 缩小，不在进料容器里完整解码大图
                'max_pixels': min(120_000_000, max(1_000_000, int(value.get('max_pixels', 40_000_000)))),
                # 缩放阈值：超过它的图在进料时按比例缩到阈值内再入库（0 = 不缩）。灵感图配 6000 万，
                # 对应冻结在机制版本里的 Qwen205 能力档案 max_source_pixels；模型侧本来就会 smart_resize 到 1MP，
                # 缩小原图不改变评测输入，只是让 7000 万像素的 Pinterest 原图不再卡在能力档案门口
                'downscale_pixels': max(0, int(value.get('downscale_pixels', 0))),
                # 明文 HTTP + 内网地址只给测试环境的 mock 素材源；生产环境即使配了也当 False。
                'insecure_http': bool(insecure) and _non_production_deployment()}
    except (TypeError, ValueError, AttributeError):
        raise MaterialError('material_source_config_invalid') from None


def _non_production_deployment():
    return (os.environ.get('LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV') or 'production').strip().lower() in ('local', 'dev', 'test', 'ci')


def _url(url, policy):
    if not isinstance(url, str) or len(url) > 2048:
        raise MaterialError('material_url_invalid')
    try:
        parts = urlsplit(url)
        insecure = bool(policy.get('insecure_http'))
        scheme_ok = parts.scheme in ('http', 'https') if insecure else parts.scheme == 'https'
        port_ok = True if insecure else parts.port in (None, 443)
        if (not scheme_ok or parts.hostname not in policy['allowed_hosts']
                or not port_ok or parts.username or parts.password or parts.fragment
                or any(ord(c) < 32 for c in url)):
            raise ValueError()
    except ValueError:
        raise MaterialError('material_url_not_allowed') from None
    return parts


def _download_insecure(parts, policy):
    """测试环境 mock 素材源：明文 HTTP、允许内网地址；其余限制（无重定向、大小、读取时限）照旧。"""
    port = parts.port or (443 if parts.scheme == 'https' else 80)
    if parts.scheme == 'https':
        connection = http.client.HTTPSConnection(parts.hostname, port, timeout=5, context=ssl._create_unverified_context())
    else:
        connection = http.client.HTTPConnection(parts.hostname, port, timeout=5)
    try:
        path = (parts.path or '/') + ('?' + parts.query if parts.query else '')
        connection.request('GET', path, headers={'Accept': 'image/*'})
        response = connection.getresponse()
        if response.status in (408, 429) or response.status >= 500:
            raise OSError('material_source_temporary')
        if response.status != 200:
            raise MaterialError('material_http_rejected')
        if response.getheader('Content-Encoding', 'identity') != 'identity':
            raise MaterialError('material_encoding_rejected')
        data = bytearray(); deadline = time.monotonic() + 10
        while True:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                raise OSError('material_read_timeout')
            chunk = response.read(min(65536, policy['max_bytes'] + 1 - len(data)))
            if not chunk: break
            data.extend(chunk)
            if len(data) > policy['max_bytes']: raise MaterialError('material_size_invalid')
        return bytes(data)
    finally:
        connection.close()


def _download(url, policy):
    """Pin a public DNS address; TLS still verifies the configured hostname.

    No proxies, redirects, URL credentials or private/reserved destination IPs.
    Response size and the total body read time are bounded.
    """
    parts = _url(url, policy)
    if policy.get('insecure_http') and (parts.scheme == 'http' or parts.port not in (None, 443)):
        return _download_insecure(parts, policy)
    addresses = socket.getaddrinfo(parts.hostname, 443, type=socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    ips = [item[4][0] for item in addresses]
    if not ips or any(not ipaddress.ip_address(ip).is_global for ip in ips):
        raise MaterialError('material_destination_not_public')
    connection = http.client.HTTPSConnection(parts.hostname, timeout=5)
    raw = socket.create_connection((ips[0], 443), timeout=5)
    try:
        connection.sock = ssl.create_default_context().wrap_socket(raw, server_hostname=parts.hostname)
        path = (parts.path or '/') + ('?' + parts.query if parts.query else '')
        connection.request('GET', path, headers={'Host': parts.hostname, 'Accept': 'image/*'})
        response = connection.getresponse()
        if response.status in (408, 429) or response.status >= 500:
            raise OSError('material_source_temporary')
        if response.status != 200:
            raise MaterialError('material_http_rejected')
        if response.getheader('Content-Encoding', 'identity') != 'identity':
            raise MaterialError('material_encoding_rejected')
        data = bytearray(); deadline = time.monotonic() + 10
        while True:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                raise OSError('material_read_timeout')
            chunk = response.read(min(65536, policy['max_bytes'] + 1 - len(data)))
            if not chunk: break
            data.extend(chunk)
            if len(data) > policy['max_bytes']: raise MaterialError('material_size_invalid')
        return bytes(data)
    finally:
        connection.close(); raw.close()


def _items(payload):
    case = payload.get('case')
    if case is None:
        item = payload.get('material') or {'asset_id': payload.get('asset_id'), 'sha256': payload.get('asset_sha256')}
        return [('image', item)]
    if not isinstance(case, dict) or str(case.get('id', '')) != payload['content_id']:
        raise MaterialError('material_case_identity_invalid')
    images = case.get('images', [])
    expected = case.get('expected_image_ids', [])
    if (not isinstance(images, list) or not isinstance(expected, list) or not expected
            or any(not isinstance(x, str) or not x for x in expected) or len(set(expected)) != len(expected)):
        raise MaterialError('material_case_expected_images_invalid')
    if any(not isinstance(item, dict) for item in images): raise MaterialError('material_manifest_invalid')
    pairs = [(str(item.get('cloud_id', '')), item) for item in images]
    if len(set(key for key, _ in pairs)) != len(pairs) or [key for key, _ in pairs] != expected:
        raise MaterialError('material_case_manifest_incomplete')
    return pairs


def submit_manifest(db, *, source_system, submission_id, occurred_at, payload, actor):
    source_system, submission_id = source_system.strip(), submission_id.strip()
    if not source_system or len(source_system) > 80 or not submission_id or len(submission_id) > 160:
        raise MaterialError('material_identity_invalid', status_code=400)
    if not isinstance(payload, dict): raise MaterialError('material_manifest_invalid', status_code=400)
    for key, limit in [('content_id', 160), ('content_version', 120), ('category_key', 40)]:
        if not isinstance(payload.get(key), str) or not payload[key].strip() or len(payload[key]) > limit:
            raise MaterialError('material_identity_invalid', status_code=400)
    serialized = canonical_json(payload)
    if len(serialized.encode()) > 256 * 1024: raise MaterialError('material_manifest_too_large')
    occurred_at = _aware(occurred_at)
    identity = _hash([source_system, payload['category_key'], payload['content_id'], payload['content_version']])
    digest = _hash([payload, occurred_at.isoformat()])
    existing = db.scalar(select(IncrementalMaterialJob).where(or_(
        (IncrementalMaterialJob.source_system == source_system) & (IncrementalMaterialJob.submission_id == submission_id),
        IncrementalMaterialJob.identity_hash == identity)))
    if existing:
        if existing.manifest_hash != digest or existing.identity_hash != identity:
            raise MaterialError('material_idempotency_conflict')
        return existing
    profile = db.scalar(select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == payload['category_key'], EvaluationCategoryProfile.status == 'active'))
    if profile is None: raise MaterialError('material_category_invalid')
    policy = _policy(source_system)
    items = _items(payload)
    if not items or len(items) > policy['max_images']: raise MaterialError('material_image_count_invalid')
    for _, item in items:
        if not isinstance(item, dict): raise MaterialError('material_manifest_invalid')
        expected = item.get('sha256') or item.get('asset_sha256')
        if expected is not None and (not isinstance(expected, str) or len(expected) != 64 or any(c not in '0123456789abcdef' for c in expected)):
            raise MaterialError('material_hash_invalid')
        if item.get('asset_id') is not None:
            if isinstance(item['asset_id'], bool) or not isinstance(item['asset_id'], int) or item['asset_id'] <= 0:
                raise MaterialError('material_asset_id_invalid', status_code=400)
            asset = db.get(Asset, item['asset_id'])
            if asset is None or asset.category_key != payload['category_key'] or asset.status == 'deleted':
                raise MaterialError('material_asset_category_invalid')
        else: _url(item.get('url') or item.get('oss_url'), policy)
    job = IncrementalMaterialJob(source_system=source_system, submission_id=submission_id,
        category_key=payload['category_key'], source_content_id=payload['content_id'], source_version=payload['content_version'],
        identity_hash=identity, manifest_hash=digest, manifest_json=serialized, occurred_at=occurred_at, created_by=actor)
    try:
        with db.begin_nested():
            db.add(job); db.flush()
    except IntegrityError:
        existing = db.scalar(select(IncrementalMaterialJob).where(or_(IncrementalMaterialJob.identity_hash == identity,
            (IncrementalMaterialJob.source_system == source_system) & (IncrementalMaterialJob.submission_id == submission_id))))
        if not existing or existing.manifest_hash != digest or existing.identity_hash != identity:
            raise MaterialError('material_idempotency_conflict') from None
        return existing
    append_audit_event(db, category='materials', action='incremental_material_submitted', subject_type='incremental_material_job', subject_id=job.id, actor=actor, payload={'manifest_hash': digest, 'source_version': job.source_version})
    return job


def _storage_dir():
    from .config import get_settings
    return get_settings().upload_dir


def _original_store():
    root = Path(_storage_dir())
    subdir = os.environ.get(ORIGINAL_STORE_ENV, '').strip()
    if not subdir:
        root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        return root, ''
    if not subdir.replace('-', '').replace('_', '').isalnum() or not subdir.isascii() or len(subdir) > 40:
        raise MaterialError('material_original_store_invalid')
    directory = root / subdir
    if directory.is_symlink() or not (directory / ORIGINAL_STORE_MARKER).is_file():
        raise OriginalStoreUnavailable(OriginalStoreUnavailable.code)
    return directory, subdir + '/'


def _validate_bytes(data, expected, policy, profile):
    if not data or len(data) > policy['max_bytes']: raise MaterialError('material_size_invalid')
    digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
    if expected and expected != digest: raise MaterialError('material_hash_mismatch')
    try:
        with Image.open(io.BytesIO(data)) as image:
            fmt, width, height = image.format, image.width, image.height
            image.verify()
    except (UnidentifiedImageError, OSError, ValueError, Image.DecompressionBombError):
        raise MaterialError('material_image_invalid') from None
    # 像素上限在 verify 之后单独判：以前放在同一个 try 里，MaterialError 是 ValueError 会被吞成 material_image_invalid
    if width * height > policy['max_pixels']: raise MaterialError('material_pixels_exceeded')
    # 非 JPEG 没有缩样解码，验证时的 load 就是全尺寸展开，预算见 NON_JPEG_MAX_PIXELS
    if fmt != 'JPEG' and width * height > NON_JPEG_MAX_PIXELS: raise MaterialError('material_pixels_exceeded')
    try:
        with Image.open(io.BytesIO(data)) as image:
            if width * height > 4_000_000 and image.format == 'JPEG':
                # 大图只做缩样解码证明可读：JPEG 走 DCT 缩放，7000 万像素也不用在进料容器里展开 200 MB
                image.draft(image.mode, (max(1, width // 8), max(1, height // 8)))
            image.load()
    except (UnidentifiedImageError, OSError, ValueError, Image.DecompressionBombError):
        raise MaterialError('material_image_invalid') from None
    mime = {'JPEG': 'image/jpeg', 'PNG': 'image/png', 'WEBP': 'image/webp'}.get(fmt)
    allowed = json.loads(profile.allowed_mime_types_json or '[]')
    if not mime or (allowed and mime not in allowed): raise MaterialError('material_type_invalid')
    return digest, mime, width, height


DOWNSCALE_JPEG_QUALITY = 92
# 没有 DCT 缩样解码的格式（PNG/WEBP）只能全尺寸展开：3000 万像素 RGBA 已是 120 MB，再加转换副本就逼近
# 进料容器 512 MiB 的预算，所以这类格式的像素上限固定在这里，不随来源 max_pixels 放宽（评审 R1）
NON_JPEG_MAX_PIXELS = 30_000_000


def _fit(width, height, limit):
    scale = (limit / (width * height)) ** 0.5
    target = (max(1, int(width * scale)), max(1, int(height * scale)))
    while target[0] * target[1] > limit:
        target = (max(1, target[0] - 1), max(1, target[1] - 1))
    return target


def _downscale(data, width, height, limit):
    """把图缩到 limit 像素以内并重编码成 JPEG；返回新字节与尺寸。

    内存边界（评审 R1）：JPEG 只用 DCT 缩样解码（1/2、1/4、1/8 里第一个落进 limit 的档），从不全尺寸展开，
    也不再做 LANCZOS 二次缩放；非 JPEG 没有缩样，只有 NON_JPEG_MAX_PIXELS 以内的才允许全解码后缩放。
    内容正确性（评审 R2）：先按 EXIF 方向摆正，透明图按仓库既有规则合成白底；动图不缩（拒），不静默丢帧。
    """
    try:
        with Image.open(io.BytesIO(data)) as opened:
            if int(getattr(opened, 'n_frames', 1) or 1) > 1:
                raise MaterialError('material_pixels_exceeded')
            if opened.format == 'JPEG':
                reduction = 1
                while (width // reduction) * (height // reduction) > limit and reduction < 8:
                    reduction *= 2
                if (width // reduction) * (height // reduction) > limit:
                    raise MaterialError('material_pixels_exceeded')
                opened.draft(opened.mode, (max(1, width // reduction), max(1, height // reduction)))
            elif width * height > NON_JPEG_MAX_PIXELS:
                raise MaterialError('material_pixels_exceeded')
            image = ImageOps.exif_transpose(opened)
            if image.mode in {'RGBA', 'LA'} or 'transparency' in image.info:
                rgba = image.convert('RGBA')
                background = Image.new('RGBA', rgba.size, (255, 255, 255, 255))
                background.alpha_composite(rgba)
                image = background.convert('RGB')
                del rgba, background
            else:
                image = image.convert('RGB')
        if image.width * image.height > limit:
            # 只有非 JPEG 或缩样后仍差一点的情况会走到这里，此时尺寸已在预算内
            image = image.resize(_fit(image.width, image.height, limit), Image.LANCZOS)
        out = io.BytesIO(); image.save(out, format='JPEG', quality=DOWNSCALE_JPEG_QUALITY)
        size = image.size
        image.close()
    except MaterialError:
        raise
    except (UnidentifiedImageError, OSError, ValueError, Image.DecompressionBombError):
        raise MaterialError('material_image_invalid') from None
    return out.getvalue(), size


def _resolve(db, job, item, policy):
    from .nas_storage import resolve_asset_path
    profile = db.scalar(select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == job.category_key, EvaluationCategoryProfile.status == 'active'))
    if profile is None: raise MaterialError('material_category_invalid')
    expected = item.get('sha256') or item.get('asset_sha256')
    asset = db.get(Asset, item['asset_id']) if item.get('asset_id') else None
    if item.get('asset_id'):
        if asset is None or asset.category_key != job.category_key or asset.status == 'deleted': raise MaterialError('material_asset_category_invalid')
        from types import SimpleNamespace
        from .config import get_settings
        settings = get_settings() if asset.storage_backend != 'local' else SimpleNamespace(upload_dir=_storage_dir())
        path = resolve_asset_path(asset, settings)
        with path.open('rb') as source: data = source.read(policy['max_bytes'] + 1)
        digest, mime, width, height = _validate_bytes(data, expected or asset.sha256, policy, profile)
        if digest != asset.sha256: raise MaterialError('material_hash_mismatch')
        return asset
    data = _download(item.get('url') or item.get('oss_url'), policy)
    digest, mime, width, height = _validate_bytes(data, expected, policy, profile)
    downscaled_from = None
    if policy['downscale_pixels'] and width * height > policy['downscale_pixels']:
        downscaled_from = (width, height, digest)
        data, _ = _downscale(data, width, height, policy['downscale_pixels'])
        # 缩后的字节重新走一遍完整校验（尺寸、像素、MIME），入库身份以缩后字节为准
        digest, mime, width, height = _validate_bytes(data, None, policy, profile)
    # 同字节的旧素材可能已按保留期删了原图（inspiration_original_retention），依次试下去，
    # 都不可用才落一份新拷贝；否则每次都卡在最老那条上，重复存同一张图。
    for existing in db.scalars(select(Asset).where(Asset.sha256 == digest, Asset.category_key == job.category_key, Asset.status != 'deleted').order_by(Asset.id).limit(20)):
        try:
            return _resolve(db, job, {'asset_id': existing.id, 'sha256': digest}, policy)
        except (OSError, MaterialError):
            continue  # Keep the old evidence; a valid new copy receives a fresh identity.
    directory, prefix = _original_store()
    extension = {'image/png': '.png', 'image/jpeg': '.jpg', 'image/webp': '.webp'}[mime]
    # Per-job deterministic path means rollback/crash reuses the exact bytes.
    name = 'material-' + job.identity_hash + '-' + digest[:24] + extension
    target = directory / name
    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(dir=directory, prefix='.material-', delete=False) as stream:
        temporary = Path(stream.name)
        stream.write(data); stream.flush(); os.fsync(stream.fileno())
    try: os.replace(temporary, target)
    finally: temporary.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    asset = Asset(original_name='incremental' + extension, stored_name=prefix + name, mime_type=mime,
                  width=width, height=height, size_bytes=len(data), sha256=digest, category_key=job.category_key)
    db.add(asset); db.flush()
    if downscaled_from is not None:
        append_audit_event(db, category='materials', action='incremental_material_downscaled', subject_type='asset', subject_id=asset.id,
                           actor=job.created_by, payload={'job_id': job.id, 'source_width': downscaled_from[0], 'source_height': downscaled_from[1],
                                                          'source_sha256': downscaled_from[2], 'width': width, 'height': height,
                                                          'limit_pixels': policy['downscale_pixels']})
    return asset


def _complete(db, job, actor):
    from .label_governance import ingest_content_event
    from .incremental_autorun import dispatch_content_event
    payload = json.loads(job.manifest_json)
    resolved = json.loads(job.resolved_json)
    for key, item in _items(payload): item.update(resolved[key])
    if 'case' in payload:
        payload['case']['ready'] = True
    else:
        payload['asset_id'] = resolved['image']['asset_id']
        payload['asset_sha256'] = resolved['image']['asset_sha256']
    payload['material_completion'] = {'manifest_hash': job.manifest_hash, 'source_occurred_at': _aware(job.occurred_at).isoformat()}
    occurred = _aware(job.occurred_at)
    # Match ingress lock order before checking the current authoritative source.
    # The subsequent ingestion uses these same locks in this transaction.
    from .incremental_mechanisms import lock_incremental_category
    from .incremental_automation import _lock_definition
    from .models import CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding, WorkflowVersion
    from sqlalchemy import JSON, cast, type_coerce
    lock_incremental_category(db, job.category_key)
    binding = db.scalar(select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding).where(
        CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding.category_key == job.category_key
    ).with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    if binding is not None:
        bound_version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, binding.workflow_version_id)
        if bound_version is not None:
            _lock_definition(db, bound_version.workflow_definition_id)
    record = db.scalar(select(ContentRecord).where(
        ContentRecord.source_system == job.source_system,
        ContentRecord.source_content_id == job.source_content_id,
    ).with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    if record:
        document = (cast(ContentIngressEvent.identity_snapshot_json, JSON)
                    if db.get_bind().dialect.name == 'postgresql'
                    else type_coerce(ContentIngressEvent.identity_snapshot_json, JSON))
        sequence_event = db.scalar(select(ContentIngressEvent.id).where(
            ContentIngressEvent.content_record_id == record.id,
            ContentIngressEvent.status != 'stale',
            document['source_change_order']['stream'].as_string().is_not(None),
        ).order_by(ContentIngressEvent.id.desc()).limit(1).with_for_update())
        if sequence_event is not None:
            # A material manifest has no authority to advance a sequence stream.
            # Only complete the currently accepted live version, regardless of time.
            if record.status == 'deleted' or record.source_version != job.source_version:
                job.status = 'superseded'; return
        else:
            # Keep the timestamp contract for manifest-only and legacy sources.
            if record.status == 'deleted' and _aware(record.source_occurred_at) >= occurred:
                job.status = 'superseded'; return
            if record.source_version != job.source_version and _aware(record.source_occurred_at) >= occurred:
                job.status = 'superseded'; return
        if record.source_version == job.source_version:
            pending = record.status == 'awaiting_material'
            latest = db.scalar(select(ContentIngressEvent).where(
                ContentIngressEvent.content_record_id == record.id, ContentIngressEvent.status != 'stale'
            ).order_by(ContentIngressEvent.id.desc()).limit(1).with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
            if latest is not None:
                prior = json.loads(latest.payload_json)
                case = prior.get('case')
                if prior.get('content_version') == job.source_version:
                    # The legacy projection may retain v6's anchor and report
                    # ready while the immutable v7 case explicitly awaits images.
                    pending = pending or (isinstance(case, dict) and (case.get('ready') is not True
                        or not case.get('images') or any(not image.get('asset_id') for image in case['images'])))
                    pending = pending or (case is None and prior.get('asset_id') is None)
            if pending:
                occurred = max(occurred, _aware(record.source_occurred_at) + timedelta(microseconds=1))
    event, record, duplicate = ingest_content_event(db, event_id='material:' + job.identity_hash,
        schema_version='content-ingress-v1', event_type='content.updated', source_system=job.source_system,
        occurred_at=occurred, payload=payload, received_by=actor)
    if event.status != 'stale':
        from .incremental_input import build_frozen_content_ingress_input, IncrementalInputError
        try:
            build_frozen_content_ingress_input(db, event=event, record=record)
        except IncrementalInputError:
            raise MaterialError('material_frozen_input_invalid') from None
    result = dispatch_content_event(db, event=event, record=record, duplicate=duplicate, actor=actor)
    job.event_id, job.routing_status = event.id, result.routing_status
    job.status = 'superseded' if event.status == 'stale' else 'completed'


def process_due_material_jobs(db, actor, now=None):
    def signature(job, binding, validation_context):
        from .nas_storage import resolve_asset_path
        from .config import get_settings
        from types import SimpleNamespace
        import stat
        asset = db.get(Asset, binding['asset_id'], populate_existing=True)
        if (asset is None or asset.category_key != job.category_key or asset.status == 'deleted'
                or asset.sha256 != binding.get('asset_sha256')):
            raise MaterialError('material_asset_category_invalid')
        settings = get_settings() if asset.storage_backend != 'local' else SimpleNamespace(upload_dir=_storage_dir())
        info = resolve_asset_path(asset, settings).stat()
        if not stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode): raise MaterialError('material_file_invalid')
        return {'asset_id': asset.id, 'sha256': asset.sha256, 'validation_context': validation_context,
                'stat': [info.st_dev, info.st_ino, info.st_size, info.st_mtime_ns, info.st_ctime_ns]}

    current = _aware(now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)); deadline = time.monotonic() + MAX_TICK_SECONDS
    ids = db.scalars(select(IncrementalMaterialJob.id).where(IncrementalMaterialJob.status.in_(['queued', 'retry_wait']),
        IncrementalMaterialJob.retry_at <= current).order_by(IncrementalMaterialJob.retry_at, IncrementalMaterialJob.id).limit(MAX_JOBS_PER_TICK)).all()
    processed = []
    for job_id in ids:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
        claim = db.execute(update(IncrementalMaterialJob).where(IncrementalMaterialJob.id == job_id,
            IncrementalMaterialJob.status.in_(['queued', 'retry_wait']), IncrementalMaterialJob.retry_at <= current)
            .values(status='processing').execution_options(synchronize_session=False))
        if claim.rowcount != 1: continue
        job = db.get(IncrementalMaterialJob, job_id, populate_existing=True)
        try:
            policy = _policy(job.source_system)
            profile = db.scalar(select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
                EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == job.category_key,
                EvaluationCategoryProfile.status == 'active').execution_options(populate_existing=True))
            if profile is None: raise MaterialError('material_category_invalid')
            validation_context = _hash([policy, profile.allowed_mime_types_json])
            resolved = json.loads(job.resolved_json)
            validated = json.loads(job.validation_json or '{}')
            manifest_items = _items(json.loads(job.manifest_json))
            checked = 0
            for key, item in manifest_items:
                binding = resolved.get(key)
                before = signature(job, binding, validation_context) if binding else None
                # A persisted byte-validation proof may be reused only while
                # inode, size and both modification timestamps are unchanged.
                # This lets slow cases advance without rereading their prefix.
                if before is not None and validated.get(key) == before:
                    checked += 1
                    continue
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
                with db.begin_nested():
                    if binding is None:
                        asset = _resolve(db, job, item, policy)
                        binding = {'asset_id': asset.id, 'asset_sha256': asset.sha256}
                        before = signature(job, binding, validation_context)
                    # Verify the stored file between two signatures, including
                    # newly downloaded bytes, so a concurrent change cannot be
                    # cached as if it were part of the validated read.
                    asset = _resolve(db, job, binding, policy)
                    after = signature(job, binding, validation_context)
                    if before != after: raise OSError('material_changed_during_validation')
                checked += 1
                resolved[key] = {'asset_id': asset.id, 'asset_sha256': asset.sha256}
                validated[key] = after
                job.resolved_json = canonical_json(resolved)
                job.validation_json = canonical_json(validated)
                db.flush()
            # Recheck the complete set immediately before ingress. Earlier
            # files can disappear or change while later files are acquired.
            complete = checked == len(manifest_items) and all(
                signature(job, resolved[key], validation_context) == validated.get(key) for key, _ in manifest_items)
            if complete:
                with db.begin_nested(): _complete(db, job, actor)
            else:
                job.status, job.retry_at = 'queued', current + timedelta(seconds=1)
            job.error_code = None
        except Exception as exc:
