            job.attempt_count += 1
            job.error_code = getattr(exc, 'code', None) if isinstance(exc, (MaterialError, OriginalStoreUnavailable)) else 'material_processing_failed'
            job.status = 'failed' if isinstance(exc, MaterialError) or job.attempt_count >= MAX_ATTEMPTS else 'retry_wait'
            job.retry_at = current + timedelta(seconds=min(300, 5 * 2 ** job.attempt_count))
        processed.append(job.id)
        db.flush()
    return {'processed_ids': processed}


def retry_material_job(db, job_id, *, actor, reason):
    if not reason.strip() or len(reason) > 500: raise MaterialError('material_retry_reason_required', status_code=400)
    row = db.scalar(select(IncrementalMaterialJob).where(IncrementalMaterialJob.id == job_id).with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    if row is None: raise MaterialError('material_job_not_found', status_code=404)
    if row.status not in ('failed', 'retry_wait'): raise MaterialError('material_retry_status_conflict')
    changed = db.execute(update(IncrementalMaterialJob).where(
        IncrementalMaterialJob.id == row.id, IncrementalMaterialJob.status == row.status,
        IncrementalMaterialJob.retry_generation == row.retry_generation).values(
            status='queued', attempt_count=0, error_code=None, retry_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
            retry_generation=row.retry_generation + 1).execution_options(synchronize_session=False))
    if changed.rowcount != 1: raise MaterialError('material_retry_status_conflict')
    db.refresh(row)
    append_audit_event(db, category='materials', action='incremental_material_retry', subject_type='incremental_material_job', subject_id=row.id, actor=actor,
        payload={'reason': reason.strip(), 'retry_generation': row.retry_generation, 'manifest_hash': row.manifest_hash})
    db.flush(); return row


def material_job_payload(row):
    return {'id': row.id, 'source_system': row.source_system, 'submission_id': row.submission_id,
            'category_key': row.category_key, 'source_content_id': row.source_content_id, 'source_version': row.source_version,
            'manifest_hash': row.manifest_hash, 'status': row.status, 'attempt_count': row.attempt_count,
            'retry_generation': row.retry_generation, 'error_code': row.error_code, 'routing_status': row.routing_status,
            'event_id': row.event_id, 'resolved_count': len(json.loads(row.resolved_json)),
            'expected_count': len(_items(json.loads(row.manifest_json))), 'retry_at': row.retry_at,
            'created_at': row.created_at}

===== FILE backend/app/incremental_mechanisms.py =====
"""Per-category authority for automated incremental evaluation mechanisms."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Mapping

from sqlalchemy import select, update
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError, OperationalError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .models import (
    CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding,
    CategoryIncrementalMechanismEvent,
    EvaluationCategoryProfile,
    LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping,
    ModelConfig,
    ModelRegistryEntry,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from .workflow_composer_runtime import ensure_composer_fixture_version
from .workflow_node_executors import (
    NodeExecutorError,
    assert_native_workflow_executable,
)
from .workflow_prompt_execution import prompt_execution_node
from .workflow_registry import (
    WorkflowRegistryError,
    canonical_workflow_snapshot,
    persisted_workflow_manifest,
)
from .workflow_v3 import validate_managed_workflow_v3
from .worker_v3_authoritative import (
    V3AuthoritativeError,
    v3_authoritative_category,
)


class IncrementalMechanismError(ValueError):
    def __init__(
        self,
        code: str,
        message: str,
        *,
        status_code: int = 409,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FrozenIncrementalMechanism:
    binding_id: int
    binding_lock_version: int
    workflow_version_id: int
    workflow_snapshot: dict[str, Any]
    snapshot_hash: str


def _validation_report(version: WorkflowVersion) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        report = json.loads(version.validation_report_json or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise IncrementalMechanismError(
            "incremental_mechanism_validation_invalid",
            "所选评测机制的校验报告损坏",
        ) from exc
    if not isinstance(report, dict) or report.get("ok") is not True:
        raise IncrementalMechanismError(
            "incremental_mechanism_validation_failed",
            "所选评测机制尚未通过完整校验",
        )
    return report


def require_incremental_compatible_workflow(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    workflow_version_id: int,
) -> tuple[WorkflowVersion, dict[str, Any]]:
    version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, workflow_version_id)
    if version is None:
        raise IncrementalMechanismError(
            "incremental_mechanism_not_found",
            "所选完整评测机制版本不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    if version.workflow_schema_version != "workflow-v3":
        raise IncrementalMechanismError(
            "incremental_mechanism_schema_invalid",
            "增量评测只能启用工作流管理器生成的完整评测机制版本",
        )
    _validation_report(version)
    manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(version)
    if manifest.get("category_key") != category_key:
        raise IncrementalMechanismError(
            "incremental_mechanism_category_mismatch",
            "所选评测机制属于其他业务类目",
        )
    if "incremental" not in (manifest.get("run_modes") or []):
        raise IncrementalMechanismError(
            "incremental_mechanism_run_mode_invalid",
            "所选评测机制没有声明可用于增量评测",
        )
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list) or not nodes:
        raise IncrementalMechanismError(
            "incremental_mechanism_nodes_missing",
            "所选评测机制没有可执行步骤",
        )
    for node in nodes:
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping):
            continue
        kind = node.get("kind")
        config = node.get("config")
        config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
        if kind in {"prompt", "rule_module"} and not isinstance(
            config.get("component_snapshot"), Mapping
        ):
            raise IncrementalMechanismError(
                "incremental_mechanism_component_not_frozen",
                "所选评测机制仍有未冻结的提示词或规则模块版本",
            )
        if kind == "prompt" and not isinstance(config.get("model_snapshot"), Mapping):
            raise IncrementalMechanismError(
                "incremental_mechanism_model_not_frozen",
                "所选评测机制仍有未冻结的模型与运行参数",
            )
        if isinstance(kind, str):
            try:
                ensure_composer_fixture_version(db, kind)
            except KeyError as exc:
                raise IncrementalMechanismError(
                    "incremental_mechanism_executor_unavailable",
                    f"流程步骤暂不支持受控执行：{kind}",
                ) from exc

    legacy_mapping = db.scalar(
        select(LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping).where(
            LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping.workflow_version_id == version.id
        )
    )
    if legacy_mapping is not None:
        try:
            compatibility = json.loads(
                legacy_mapping.compatibility_report_json or "{}"
            )
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise IncrementalMechanismError(
                "incremental_mechanism_compatibility_invalid",
                "历史机制兼容报告损坏",
            ) from exc
        if not isinstance(compatibility, dict) or compatibility.get("compatible") is not True:
            raise IncrementalMechanismError(
                "incremental_mechanism_compatibility_failed",
                "历史机制尚未通过模块化重组等价性验证",
            )
    try:
        v3_authoritative_category(db, category_key)
        # Every incremental graph is dispatched through create_production_run.
        # Validate that native graph, preserving each node's frozen parameters
        # and short-circuit rules instead of projecting it into legacy A/B.
        errors = validate_managed_workflow_v3(db, manifest)
        if errors:
            raise IncrementalMechanismError(
                "incremental_mechanism_executor_unavailable",
                "原生增量工作流未通过校验："
                + "; ".join(error.message for error in errors[:5]),
            )
        assert_native_workflow_executable(manifest)
        for node in nodes:
            prompt = prompt_execution_node(node)
            if prompt is None:
                continue
            config = prompt["config"]
            registry = db.get(ModelRegistryEntry, config["model_registry_id"])
            snapshot = config.get("model_snapshot") or {}
            source_id = snapshot.get(
                "source_model_config_id", registry.source_model_config_id
            )
            source = (
                db.get(ModelConfig, source_id)
                if isinstance(source_id, int)
                else None
            )
            if source is None or not source.active:
                raise IncrementalMechanismError(
                    "incremental_mechanism_executor_unavailable",
                    "所选节点的冻结模型没有可用的真实执行配置",
                )
    except V3AuthoritativeError as exc:
        raise IncrementalMechanismError(
            "incremental_mechanism_executor_unavailable",
            f"当前类目还没有可供真实增量执行的规则底座：{exc}",
        ) from exc
    except NodeExecutorError as exc:
        raise IncrementalMechanismError(
            "incremental_mechanism_executor_unavailable",
            f"当前真实增量执行器不能准确运行这个机制：{exc}",
        ) from exc
    return version, manifest


def get_incremental_mechanism_binding(
    db: Session,
    category_key: str,
) -> CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding | None:
    return db.scalar(
        select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding).where(
            CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding.category_key == category_key
        )
    )


def list_incremental_mechanism_events(
    db: Session,
    category_key: str,
) -> list[CategoryIncrementalMechanismEvent]:
    return list(
        db.scalars(
            select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismEvent)
            .where(CategoryIncrementalMechanismEvent.category_key == category_key)
            .order_by(CategoryIncrementalMechanismEvent.id.asc())
        ).all()
    )


def _required_reason(reason: str) -> str:
    normalized = reason.strip()
    if not normalized:
        raise IncrementalMechanismError(
            "incremental_switch_reason_required",
            "切换原因必须填写",
            status_code=422,
        )
    return normalized


def lock_incremental_category(db: Session, category_key: str):
    """Stable category lock also serializes the first binding insertion."""
    return db.scalar(select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
        EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == category_key).with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True))


def set_category_service_class(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    service_class: str,
    expected_lock_version: int,
    reason: str,
    actor: str,
):
    """Set a category's queue priority. Does not touch the bound mechanism.

    'high' wins submission slots ahead of 'normal' under fair submission, and is
    the only class entitled to the 24 h online fallback. Already-running
    materials keep the class frozen into their own snapshot, so this only
    affects work enqueued from here on - which is why it is safe to change
    without draining.
    """
    from .incremental_sla import SERVICE_CLASSES
    reason = _required_reason(reason)
    if service_class not in SERVICE_CLASSES:
        raise IncrementalMechanismError(
            'incremental_service_class_invalid', '优先级只能是高或普通', status_code=422)
    lock_incremental_category(db, category_key)
    current = get_incremental_mechanism_binding(db, category_key)
    if current is None:
        raise IncrementalMechanismError(
            'incremental_mechanism_not_found', '该类目还没有启用的评测机制', status_code=404)
    if current.lock_version != expected_lock_version:
        raise IncrementalMechanismError(
            'incremental_pointer_conflict', '当前启用版本已变化，请刷新后重试')
    previous = current.service_class
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    result = db.execute(
        update(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding)
        .where(
            CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding.id == current.id,
            CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding.lock_version == expected_lock_version,
        )
        .values(service_class=service_class, lock_version=expected_lock_version + 1,
                updated_by=actor, updated_at=now)
    )
    if result.rowcount != 1:
        raise IncrementalMechanismError(
            'incremental_pointer_conflict', '当前启用版本已变化，请刷新后重试')
    db.flush()
    db.refresh(current)
    # Same event trail the mechanism pointer uses. The bound version does not
    # move here, so both sides carry it and the classes ride the mode columns.
    db.add(
        CategoryIncrementalMechanismEvent(
            category_key=category_key,
            action='service_class',
            previous_workflow_version_id=current.workflow_version_id,
            next_workflow_version_id=current.workflow_version_id,
            previous_model_execution_mode=previous,
            next_model_execution_mode=service_class,
            reason=reason,
            actor=actor,
        )
    )
    db.flush()
    return current


def set_incremental_mechanism(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    workflow_version_id: int,
    expected_lock_version: int | None,
    reason: str,
    actor: str,
    model_execution_mode: str | None = None,
    _action: str = "set",
) -> CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding:
    reason = _required_reason(reason)
    if model_execution_mode not in (None, 'real', 'simulated'):
        raise IncrementalMechanismError('incremental_execution_mode_invalid', '执行模式必须为真实或模拟', status_code=422)
    lock_incremental_category(db, category_key)
    version, _manifest = require_incremental_compatible_workflow(
        db,
        category_key=category_key,
        workflow_version_id=workflow_version_id,
    )
    current = get_incremental_mechanism_binding(db, category_key)
    previous_id = current.workflow_version_id if current is not None else None
    previous_mode = current.model_execution_mode if current is not None else None
    manifest_policy = _manifest.get('resource_policy', {}).get('incremental_autorun', {})
    if model_execution_mode == 'real' and manifest_policy.get('model_execution_mode') == 'simulated':
        raise IncrementalMechanismError('incremental_execution_mode_forbidden', '该冻结机制明确仅允许模拟执行', status_code=422)
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if current is None:
        if expected_lock_version is not None:
            raise IncrementalMechanismError(
                "incremental_pointer_conflict",
                "当前启用版本已变化，请刷新后重试",
            )
        candidate = CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding(
            category_key=category_key,
            workflow_version_id=version.id,
            lock_version=1,
            model_execution_mode=model_execution_mode,
            updated_by=actor,
            updated_at=now,
        )
        try:
            with db.begin_nested():
                db.add(candidate)
                db.flush()
        except (IntegrityError, OperationalError) as exc:
            raise IncrementalMechanismError(
                "incremental_pointer_conflict",
                "当前启用版本已变化，请刷新后重试",
            ) from exc
        current = candidate
    else:
        if expected_lock_version is None or current.lock_version != expected_lock_version:
            raise IncrementalMechanismError(
                "incremental_pointer_conflict",
                "当前启用版本已变化，请刷新后重试",
            )
        result = db.execute(
            update(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding)
            .where(
                CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding.id == current.id,
                CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding.lock_version
                == expected_lock_version,
            )
            .values(
                workflow_version_id=version.id,
                lock_version=expected_lock_version + 1,
                model_execution_mode=model_execution_mode,
                updated_by=actor,
                updated_at=now,
            )
            .execution_options(synchronize_session=False)
        )
        if result.rowcount != 1:
            raise IncrementalMechanismError(
                "incremental_pointer_conflict",
                "当前启用版本已变化，请刷新后重试",
            )
        db.expire_all()
        current = get_incremental_mechanism_binding(db, category_key)
        if current is None:
            raise IncrementalMechanismError(
                "incremental_pointer_conflict",
                "当前启用版本已变化，请刷新后重试",
            )
    db.add(
        CategoryIncrementalMechanismEvent(
            category_key=category_key,
            action=_action,
            previous_workflow_version_id=previous_id,
            next_workflow_version_id=version.id,
            previous_model_execution_mode=previous_mode,
            next_model_execution_mode=model_execution_mode,
            reason=reason,
            actor=actor,
        )
    )
    db.flush()
    return current


def rollback_incremental_mechanism(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    event_id: int,
    expected_lock_version: int,
    reason: str,
    actor: str,
) -> CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding:
    event = db.get(CategoryIncrementalMechanismEvent, event_id)
    if (
        event is None
        or event.category_key != category_key
        or event.previous_workflow_version_id is None
    ):
        raise IncrementalMechanismError(
            "incremental_rollback_target_invalid",
            "所选记录没有可回滚的上一版本",
            status_code=422,
        )
    return set_incremental_mechanism(
        db,
        category_key=category_key,
        workflow_version_id=event.previous_workflow_version_id,
        expected_lock_version=expected_lock_version,
        reason=reason,
        actor=actor,
        model_execution_mode=event.previous_model_execution_mode,
        _action="rollback",
    )


def incremental_binding_runtime_context(binding: CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Pure projection shared by enqueue and read-only operator readiness."""
    return {
        'run_mode': 'incremental',
        'incremental_binding_id': binding.id,
        'incremental_binding_lock_version': binding.lock_version,
        **({'incremental_execution_policy': {
            'authority': 'incremental_binding',
            'binding_id': binding.id,
            'binding_lock_version': binding.lock_version,
            'model_execution_mode': binding.model_execution_mode,
        }} if binding.model_execution_mode is not None else {}),
    }


def resolve_incremental_mechanism_for_enqueue(
    db: Session,
    category_key: str,
    *,
    locked_refresh: bool = False,
) -> FrozenIncrementalMechanism:
    if locked_refresh:
        lock_incremental_category(db, category_key)
        binding = db.scalar(select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding).where(
            CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding.category_key == category_key
        ).with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    else:
        binding = get_incremental_mechanism_binding(db, category_key)
    if binding is None:
        raise IncrementalMechanismError(
            "incremental_mechanism_not_configured",
            "该类目尚未设置增量评测当前启用版本",
        )
    version, _manifest = require_incremental_compatible_workflow(
        db,
        category_key=category_key,
        workflow_version_id=binding.workflow_version_id,
    )
    try:
        snapshot, digest = canonical_workflow_snapshot(
            db,
            version.id,
            {
                "environment": "dry_run",
                "run_mode": "incremental",
                "category_key": category_key,
                "queue_class": "production_batch",
                **incremental_binding_runtime_context(binding),
            },
        )
    except WorkflowRegistryError as exc:
        raise IncrementalMechanismError(
            exc.code,
            str(exc),
            status_code=exc.status_code,
        ) from exc
    return FrozenIncrementalMechanism(
        binding_id=binding.id,
        binding_lock_version=binding.lock_version,
        workflow_version_id=version.id,
        workflow_snapshot=snapshot,
        snapshot_hash=digest,
    )

===== FILE backend/app/incremental_mechanisms_api.py =====
"""Operator API for each category's automated incremental mechanism pointer."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import date, datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Callable, Literal

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Path, Query
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError, OperationalError

from .authz import has_permission
from .database import get_db
from .incremental_mechanisms import (
    IncrementalMechanismError,
    get_incremental_mechanism_binding,
    list_incremental_mechanism_events,
    rollback_incremental_mechanism,
    set_category_service_class,
    set_incremental_mechanism,
)
from .models import User, WorkflowVersion
from .workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest
from .incremental_automation import automation_cards, set_automation_control, set_automation_operation
from .workflow_runtime import WorkflowRuntimeError
from .incremental_autorun import IncrementalAutorunError


class AutomationControlRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    enabled: bool = Field(strict=True)
    expected_revision: int = Field(ge=0, strict=True)
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=2000)


class AutomationArchiveRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    archived: bool = Field(strict=True)
    expected_revision: int = Field(ge=1, strict=True)
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=2000)


class RoutingPolicyRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    expected_revision: int = Field(ge=0, strict=True)
    mode: Literal["shadow", "split"]
    ratio_percent: int = Field(ge=0, le=100, strict=True)
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=2000)
    ownership_contract: str = Field(default="", max_length=500)


class AutomationOperationRequest(AutomationControlRequest):
    expected_routing_revision: int = Field(ge=0, strict=True)


class AdmissionRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    expected_revision: int = Field(ge=1, strict=True)
    paused: bool = Field(strict=True)
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=2000)


class HandoverRequest(RoutingPolicyRequest):
    watermark: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=500)
    assignment_ids: list[int] = Field(max_length=1000)
    destination: Literal["shadow", "excluded", "formal", "old"]


class RecoveryRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    expected_revision: int = Field(ge=1, strict=True)
    event_ids: list[int] = Field(min_length=1, max_length=100)
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=2000)


class IncrementalMechanismSetRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    workflow_version_id: int = Field(ge=1)
    expected_lock_version: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1)
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=2000)
    model_execution_mode: Literal['real', 'simulated'] | None = None


class CategoryServiceClassRequest(BaseModel):
    service_class: Literal["high", "normal"]
    expected_lock_version: int = Field(ge=1)
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=500)


class IncrementalMechanismRollbackRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    event_id: int = Field(ge=1)
    expected_lock_version: int = Field(ge=1)
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=2000)


def _http_error(exc: IncrementalMechanismError) -> HTTPException:
    return HTTPException(
        status_code=exc.status_code,
        detail={"code": exc.code, "message": str(exc)},
    )


def _require_activation(user: User) -> None:
    if has_permission(user, "incremental_mechanisms:activate"):
        return
    raise HTTPException(
        status_code=403,
        detail={
            "code": "incremental_mechanism_activation_forbidden",
            "message": "只有系统管理员可以切换增量评测当前启用版本",
        },
    )


def _binding_payload(db: Session, binding) -> dict[str, Any]:
    version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, binding.workflow_version_id)
    if version is None:
        raise IncrementalMechanismError(
            "incremental_mechanism_not_found",
            "当前启用的评测机制版本不存在",
            status_code=404,
        )
    try:
        report = json.loads(version.validation_report_json or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        report = {"ok": False, "errors": []}
    return {
        "id": binding.id,
        "category_key": binding.category_key,
        "workflow_version_id": binding.workflow_version_id,
        "workflow_version": version.version,
        "workflow_canonical_hash": version.canonical_hash,
        "workflow_manifest": persisted_workflow_manifest(version),
        "validation_report": report,
        "lock_version": binding.lock_version,
        "model_execution_mode": binding.model_execution_mode,
        "service_class": binding.service_class,
        "updated_by": binding.updated_by,
        "updated_at": binding.updated_at,
    }


def _state_payload(db: Session, category_key: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    binding = get_incremental_mechanism_binding(db, category_key)
    return {
        "category_key": category_key,
        "binding": _binding_payload(db, binding) if binding is not None else None,
    }


def _event_payload(event) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": event.id,
        "category_key": event.category_key,
        "action": event.action,
        "previous_workflow_version_id": event.previous_workflow_version_id,
        "next_workflow_version_id": event.next_workflow_version_id,
        "previous_model_execution_mode": event.previous_model_execution_mode,
        "next_model_execution_mode": event.next_model_execution_mode,
        "reason": event.reason,
        "actor": event.actor,
        "created_at": event.created_at,
    }


def build_incremental_mechanisms_router(
    require_user: Callable[..., Any],
) -> APIRouter:
    router = APIRouter(
        prefix="/api/incremental-mechanisms",
        tags=["incremental-mechanisms"],
    )

    @router.get("/automation/cards")
    def get_automation_cards(include_costs: bool = True,
        user: User = Depends(require_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {**automation_cards(db, include_costs=include_costs),
            "can_control": has_permission(user, "incremental_mechanisms:activate")}

    @router.get("/automation/{category_key}/{workflow_definition_id}/costs")
    def get_automation_costs(category_key: str = Path(min_length=1, max_length=40),
        workflow_definition_id: int = Path(ge=1),
        _user: User = Depends(require_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        from .incremental_observability import cost_summary
        started_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        return {"costs": cost_summary(db, category_key=category_key,
            workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id), "updated_at": started_at}

    @router.get("/automation/{category_key}/{workflow_definition_id}/dashboard")
    def get_dashboard(category_key: str, workflow_definition_id: int,
        start: date, end: date, comparison: Literal["none", "day", "week", "month", "year"] = "none",
        _user: User = Depends(require_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        from .incremental_dashboard import workflow_dashboard
        try:
            return workflow_dashboard(db, category_key=category_key, workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id,
                                      start=start, end=end, comparison=comparison)
        except LookupError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=str(exc)) from exc
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=str(exc)) from exc

    @router.put("/automation/{category_key}/{workflow_definition_id}")
    def put_automation_control(payload: AutomationControlRequest,
        category_key: str = Path(min_length=1, max_length=40),
        workflow_definition_id: int = Path(ge=1),
        user: User = Depends(require_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_activation(user)
        try:
            result = set_automation_control(db, category_key=category_key,
                workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id, enabled=payload.enabled,
                expected_revision=payload.expected_revision, reason=payload.reason, actor=user.username)
            db.commit()
            return result
        except (IncrementalMechanismError, WorkflowRuntimeError, IncrementalAutorunError) as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc
        except (IntegrityError, OperationalError) as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail={"code": "automation_conflict", "message": "并发操作冲突，请刷新后重试"}) from exc

    @router.put("/automation/{category_key}/{workflow_definition_id}/archive")
    def put_automation_archive(payload: AutomationArchiveRequest, category_key: str,
                               workflow_definition_id: int, user: User = Depends(require_user),
                               db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict:
        _require_activation(user)
        from .incremental_automation import archive_automation_control
        try:
            result = archive_automation_control(db, category_key=category_key,
                workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id, **payload.model_dump(), actor=user.username)
            db.commit()
            return result
        except IncrementalMechanismError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.put("/categories/{category_key}/service-class")
    def put_category_service_class(payload: CategoryServiceClassRequest,
        category_key: str = Path(min_length=1, max_length=40),
        user: User = Depends(require_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_activation(user)
        try:
            binding = set_category_service_class(db, category_key=category_key,
                service_class=payload.service_class,
                expected_lock_version=payload.expected_lock_version,
                reason=payload.reason, actor=user.username)
            result = _binding_payload(db, binding)
            db.commit()
            return result
        except (IncrementalMechanismError, WorkflowRuntimeError) as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc
        except (IntegrityError, OperationalError) as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail={"code": "automation_conflict", "message": "并发操作冲突，请刷新后重试"}) from exc

    @router.put("/automation/{category_key}/{workflow_definition_id}/operation")
    def put_automation_operation(payload: AutomationOperationRequest,
        category_key: str = Path(min_length=1, max_length=40), workflow_definition_id: int = Path(ge=1),
        user: User = Depends(require_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        _require_activation(user)
        try:
            result = set_automation_operation(db, category_key=category_key,
                workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id, **payload.model_dump(), actor=user.username)
            db.commit()
            return result
        except (IncrementalMechanismError, WorkflowRuntimeError, IncrementalAutorunError) as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc
        except (IntegrityError, OperationalError) as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail={"code": "automation_conflict", "message": "并发操作冲突，请刷新后重试"}) from exc

    def routing_mutation(db, user, operation, category_key, workflow_definition_id, payload):
        from .incremental_routing import configure_policy, set_admission, handover, recover_events
        _require_activation(user)
        try:
            card = next((c for c in automation_cards(db, workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id)["items"]
                if c["category_key"] == category_key), None)
            if card is None:
                raise IncrementalMechanismError("routing_scope_missing", "该类目工作流不存在", status_code=404)
            if operation == "admission" and not payload.paused and (card["enable_blocker"] or not card["bound"]):
                raise IncrementalMechanismError("routing_not_ready", card["enable_blocker"] or "工作流尚未绑定")
            fn = {"policy": configure_policy, "admission": set_admission, "handover": handover, "recover": recover_events}[operation]
            result = fn(db, category_key=category_key, workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id,
                **payload.model_dump(), actor=user.username)
            db.commit()
            return result
        except (IncrementalMechanismError, WorkflowRuntimeError, IncrementalAutorunError) as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc
        except (IntegrityError, OperationalError) as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail={"code": "routing_conflict", "message": "并发操作冲突，请刷新"}) from exc

    @router.put("/automation/{category_key}/{workflow_definition_id}/policy")
    def put_routing_policy(payload: RoutingPolicyRequest, category_key: str = Path(min_length=1, max_length=40),
        workflow_definition_id: int = Path(ge=1), user: User = Depends(require_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        return routing_mutation(db, user, "policy", category_key, workflow_definition_id, payload)

    @router.put("/automation/{category_key}/{workflow_definition_id}/admission")
    def put_routing_admission(payload: AdmissionRequest, category_key: str = Path(min_length=1, max_length=40),
        workflow_definition_id: int = Path(ge=1), user: User = Depends(require_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        return routing_mutation(db, user, "admission", category_key, workflow_definition_id, payload)

    @router.post("/automation/{category_key}/{workflow_definition_id}/handover")
    def post_routing_handover(payload: HandoverRequest, category_key: str = Path(min_length=1, max_length=40),
        workflow_definition_id: int = Path(ge=1), user: User = Depends(require_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        return routing_mutation(db, user, "handover", category_key, workflow_definition_id, payload)

    @router.post("/automation/{category_key}/{workflow_definition_id}/recover")
    def post_routing_recover(payload: RecoveryRequest, category_key: str = Path(min_length=1, max_length=40),
        workflow_definition_id: int = Path(ge=1), user: User = Depends(require_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        return routing_mutation(db, user, "recover", category_key, workflow_definition_id, payload)

    @router.get("/automation/{category_key}/{workflow_definition_id}/trace")
    def get_routing_trace(category_key: str = Path(min_length=1, max_length=40), workflow_definition_id: int = Path(ge=1),
        source_system: str = Query(min_length=1, max_length=120), entity_id: str = Query(min_length=1, max_length=320),
        before_id: int | None = Query(default=None, ge=1), user: User = Depends(require_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        from .incremental_observability import entity_trace
        return entity_trace(db, category_key=category_key, workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id,
            source_system=source_system, entity_id=entity_id, before_id=before_id)

    @router.get("/{category_key}")
    def get_pointer(
        category_key: str = Path(min_length=1, max_length=40),
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return _state_payload(db, category_key)
        except IncrementalMechanismError as exc:
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.get("/{category_key}/events")
    def get_events(
        category_key: str = Path(min_length=1, max_length=40),
        _user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "category_key": category_key,
            "items": [
                _event_payload(event)
                for event in list_incremental_mechanism_events(db, category_key)
            ],
        }

    @router.put("/{category_key}")
    def set_pointer(
        payload: IncrementalMechanismSetRequest,
        category_key: str = Path(min_length=1, max_length=40),
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_activation(user)
        try:
            set_incremental_mechanism(
                db,
                category_key=category_key,
                workflow_version_id=payload.workflow_version_id,
                model_execution_mode=payload.model_execution_mode,
                expected_lock_version=payload.expected_lock_version,
                reason=payload.reason,
                actor=user.username,
            )
            db.commit()
            return _state_payload(db, category_key)
        except IncrementalMechanismError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    @router.post("/{category_key}/rollback")
    def rollback_pointer(
        payload: IncrementalMechanismRollbackRequest,
        category_key: str = Path(min_length=1, max_length=40),
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_activation(user)
        try:
            rollback_incremental_mechanism(
                db,
                category_key=category_key,
                event_id=payload.event_id,
                expected_lock_version=payload.expected_lock_version,
                reason=payload.reason,
                actor=user.username,
            )
            db.commit()
            return _state_payload(db, category_key)
        except IncrementalMechanismError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise _http_error(exc) from exc

    return router

===== FILE backend/app/incremental_observability.py =====
"""Bounded business trace and cost evidence for automation operators."""
from .workflow_snapshot_store import read_run_snapshot_document
import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from sqlalchemy import select, func, JSON, cast, type_coerce
from .models import (ContentRecord, ContentIngressEvent, ProductionRun, WorkflowModelInvocation,
    IncrementalEntityAssignment, IncrementalShadowResult, DownstreamPublishLedger)
from .incremental_routing import assignment_payload
from .incremental_evidence import comparison_for_shadow


def waiting_material_count(db, category_key):
    return db.scalar(select(func.count(ContentRecord.id)).where(
        ContentRecord.category_key == category_key, ContentRecord.status == "awaiting_material")) or 0


def entity_trace(db, *, category_key, workflow_definition_id, source_system, entity_id, before_id=None):
    assignment = db.scalar(select(IncrementalEntityAssignment).where(
        IncrementalEntityAssignment.category_key == category_key,
        IncrementalEntityAssignment.workflow_definition_id == workflow_definition_id,
        IncrementalEntityAssignment.source_system == source_system,
        IncrementalEntityAssignment.entity_id == entity_id))
    query = select(ContentIngressEvent).join(ContentRecord, ContentRecord.id == ContentIngressEvent.content_record_id).where(
        ContentRecord.category_key == category_key, ContentRecord.source_system == source_system,
        ContentRecord.source_content_id == entity_id)
    if before_id is not None:
        query = query.where(ContentIngressEvent.id < before_id)
    events = list(db.scalars(query.order_by(ContentIngressEvent.id.desc()).limit(50)))
    items = []
    for event in events:
        runs = list(db.scalars(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.source_type == "content_ingress_event",
            ProductionRun.source_id == event.id, ProductionRun.workflow_definition_id == workflow_definition_id)))
        run_items = []
        for run in runs:
            calls = db.execute(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id), func.sum(WorkflowModelInvocation.actual_cost_micros),
                func.sum(WorkflowModelInvocation.estimated_cost_micros)).where(WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id == run.id)).one()
            shadows = list(db.scalars(select(IncrementalShadowResult).where(IncrementalShadowResult.run_key == run.run_key).limit(30)))
            publications = list(db.scalars(select(DownstreamPublishLedger).where(DownstreamPublishLedger.origin_run_key == run.run_key).order_by(DownstreamPublishLedger.id.desc()).limit(30)))
            run_items.append({"run_key": run.run_key, "status": run.status, "error_code": run.error_code,
                "workflow_version_id": run.workflow_version_id, "model_execution_mode": run.model_execution_mode,
                "route": read_run_snapshot_document(run).get("runtime_context", {}).get("incremental_route"),
                "cost": {"calls": calls[0], "settled_micros": calls[1], "estimated_micros": calls[2], "source": "workflow_model_invocations", "invoice_reconciled": False},
                "shadow_results": [{"id": row.id, "step_key": row.step_key, "result_hash": row.result_hash,
                    "result": json.loads(row.result_json), "created_at": row.created_at, **comparison_for_shadow(db, row, db.get(IncrementalEntityAssignment, row.assignment_id))} for row in shadows],
                "publications": [{"id": row.id, "revision": row.revision, "delivery_status": row.delivery_status} for row in publications]})
        items.append({"id": event.id, "event_id": event.event_id, "status": event.status,
            "runs": run_items})
    return {"assignment": assignment_payload(db, assignment) if assignment else None, "events": items,
        "next_before_id": events[-1].id if len(events) == 50 else None}


def cost_summary(db, *, category_key, workflow_definition_id):
    since = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(hours=24)
    scoped = select(ProductionRun.id).where(ProductionRun.source_type == "content_ingress_event",
        ProductionRun.category_key == category_key, ProductionRun.workflow_definition_id == workflow_definition_id)
    # Aggregate inside the database: do not transfer every immutable workflow
    # snapshot to Python on each five-second dashboard refresh.
    document = (cast(ProductionRun.snapshot_json, JSON) if db.get_bind().dialect.name == "postgresql"
        else type_coerce(ProductionRun.snapshot_json, JSON))
    destination_expr = func.coalesce(document[("runtime_context", "incremental_route", "destination")].as_string(), "legacy")
    # Group the recent ledger before reading large frozen snapshots. GROUP BY
    # keeps MySQL from merging this back into one snapshot parse per invocation.
    calls_by_run = select(
        WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.status, WorkflowModelInvocation.error_code,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.endpoint_id,
        func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id).label("calls"),
        func.sum(WorkflowModelInvocation.actual_cost_micros).label("settled"),
        func.sum(WorkflowModelInvocation.estimated_cost_micros).label("estimate"),
        func.sum(WorkflowModelInvocation.input_tokens).label("input_tokens"),
        func.sum(WorkflowModelInvocation.output_tokens).label("output_tokens"),
        func.sum(WorkflowModelInvocation.cached_input_tokens).label("cached"),
    ).where(WorkflowModelInvocation.created_at >= since).group_by(
        WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id, WorkflowModelInvocation.status,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.error_code, WorkflowModelInvocation.endpoint_id).subquery()
    rows = db.execute(select(destination_expr, calls_by_run.c.status, calls_by_run.c.error_code,
        calls_by_run.c.endpoint_id,
        func.sum(calls_by_run.c.calls), func.sum(calls_by_run.c.settled),
        func.sum(calls_by_run.c.estimate), func.sum(calls_by_run.c.input_tokens),
        func.sum(calls_by_run.c.output_tokens), func.sum(calls_by_run.c.cached))
        .select_from(calls_by_run).join(ProductionRun,
            ProductionRun.id == calls_by_run.c.production_run_id)
        .where(ProductionRun.id.in_(scoped))
        .group_by(destination_expr, calls_by_run.c.status, calls_by_run.c.error_code,
            calls_by_run.c.endpoint_id))
    empty = lambda: {"calls": 0, "settled_micros": 0, "reserved_estimate_micros": 0, "unresolved_calls": 0,
        "input_tokens": 0, "output_tokens": 0, "cached_input_tokens": 0}
    totals = {key: empty() for key in ("shadow", "formal", "legacy")}
    by_endpoint: dict[str, dict] = {}
    for destination, status, error_code, endpoint_id, calls, settled, estimate, input_tokens, output_tokens, cached in rows:
        calls = int(calls)
        target = totals.setdefault(destination, empty())
        target["calls"] += calls
        target["settled_micros"] += settled or 0
        target["input_tokens"] += input_tokens or 0
        target["output_tokens"] += output_tokens or 0
        target["cached_input_tokens"] += cached or 0
        endpoint = by_endpoint.setdefault(endpoint_id if endpoint_id is not None else "direct",
            {"calls": 0, "settled_micros": 0, "input_tokens": 0, "cached_input_tokens": 0})
        endpoint["calls"] += calls
        endpoint["settled_micros"] += settled or 0
        endpoint["input_tokens"] += input_tokens or 0
        endpoint["cached_input_tokens"] += cached or 0
        if status == "reserved" or error_code == "model_invocation_outcome_unknown":
            target["unresolved_calls"] += calls
            target["reserved_estimate_micros"] += estimate or 0
    total_input = sum(item["input_tokens"] for item in totals.values())
    total_cached = sum(item["cached_input_tokens"] for item in totals.values())
    # Endpoint and destination totals share the same recent invocation scan.
    return {"window_hours": 24, "source": "workflow_model_invocations", "invoice_reconciled": False,
        "precision": "按调用结算证据汇总；供应商未报用量时仍可能为保守估计", "by_destination": totals,
        "by_endpoint": by_endpoint,
        "cache": {"input_tokens": total_input, "cached_input_tokens": total_cached,
            "hit_ratio": round(total_cached / total_input, 4) if total_input else None}}

===== FILE backend/app/incremental_readiness.py =====
"""Configuration-only readiness; never opens external connections on card refresh."""
from collections.abc import Mapping

from sqlalchemy.engine import make_url
from sqlalchemy.exc import ArgumentError

from .external_datasources import (
    ALLOWED_SCHEMES, ExternalDatasourceError, resolve_locator_table, resolve_locator_to_dsn,
)
from .incremental_input import incremental_autorun_policy
from .models import ConnectorContractVersion


def _positive_int(value):
    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int) or value < 1:
        return None
    return value


def automation_readiness_blocker(db, manifest, routing_policy, *, model_execution_mode=None):
    policy = incremental_autorun_policy({"workflow": {"manifest": manifest}})
    if (model_execution_mode or policy["model_execution_mode"]) != "real":
        return None
    mode = routing_policy.get("mode") if isinstance(routing_policy, Mapping) else getattr(routing_policy, "mode", None)
    for node in manifest.get("nodes", []):
        if node.get("kind") != "downstream_projection":
            continue
        config = node.get("config") or {}
        if mode == "shadow":
            if config.get("completion_mode") == "await_follow_up":
                return "影子链路的外部续跑尚无隔离回执适配；请先配置独立隔离链路"
            continue
        frozen = config.get("connector_snapshot") or {}
        # 按运行时冻结快照时的同一优先级读：``connector_contract_version_id`` 在前，
        # 存量快照里的 id 只是退路（workflow_connector_executor.freeze_connector_nodes）。
        # 只认快照会误判「只换了 id、没留快照」的版本——2026-09-16 线上 v299
        # (factory-prod-downstream-v3) 就是这样：它把下游换到合同 11 时只改了
        # connector_contract_version_id，卡片从此一直显示待就绪，而真实投递始终按
        # 合同 11 正常发。两个都在却对不上，那是真的有问题，仍然拦。
        pinned = _positive_int(config.get("connector_contract_version_id"))
        snapshot_id = _positive_int(frozen.get("connector_contract_id"))
        if pinned is not None and snapshot_id is not None and pinned != snapshot_id:
            return "写入下游冻结合同与当前登记不一致，请重新检查工作流版本"
        contract_id = pinned if pinned is not None else snapshot_id
        if contract_id is None:
            return "写入下游缺少冻结合同，请在工作流管理配置并发布版本"
        target = db.get(ConnectorContractVersion, contract_id)
        if target is None or target.status != "active":
            return "写入下游合同不存在或未启用"
        if (target.direction != "downstream"
            or target.contract_key != config.get("connector_key")
            # 快照字段只在留了快照时才比；没留快照的版本靠上面的 id 一致性兜住。
            or (frozen and (frozen.get("direction") != "downstream"
                            or target.contract_key != frozen.get("contract_key")
                            or (frozen.get("contract_hash")
                                and target.contract_hash != frozen["contract_hash"])))):
            return "写入下游冻结合同与当前登记不一致，请重新检查工作流版本"
        if target.adapter_key == "inspiration-factory-submit-v1":
            from .inspiration_factory_access import any_inspiration_access_enabled, inspiration_endpoint_enabled
            if not any_inspiration_access_enabled():
                return "灵感图正式链路尚未启动，等待运营核验生成内容"
            from .connector_delivery import ConnectorDeliveryError
            from .inspiration_factory_delivery import inspiration_factory_configuration
            try:
                endpoint, _, _, _ = inspiration_factory_configuration(target)
            except (ConnectorDeliveryError, ValueError, OSError):
                return "灵感图下游合同、等级映射或持久提交账本未就绪，请研发补齐配置"
            if not inspiration_endpoint_enabled(endpoint):
                return "灵感图当前仅开放测试接口，正式接口等待运营核验后启动"
            continue
        if target.adapter_key == "factory-submit-v1":
            from .connector_delivery import ConnectorDeliveryError
            from .factory_submit_delivery import resolve_locator_endpoint
            try:
                resolve_locator_endpoint(target.logical_locator)
            except ConnectorDeliveryError:
                return "下游提交端点未配置或不在允许的工厂域名内，请研发补齐部署侧映射"
            continue
        if target.adapter_key != "sql-envelope-v1":
            return "写入下游尚未配置可执行适配器"
        try:
            url = make_url(resolve_locator_to_dsn(target.logical_locator))
            resolve_locator_table(target.logical_locator)
            if url.drivername not in ALLOWED_SCHEMES:
                return "下游连接配置使用了不支持的数据库类型"
        except (ExternalDatasourceError, ArgumentError, ValueError):
            # Neither deployment DSNs nor bad locator values belong in UI errors.
            return "下游连接配置或物理表名未就绪，请研发补齐部署侧映射"
    return None

===== FILE backend/app/incremental_reassessment.py =====
"""Explicit replacement of a failed incremental run with the current mechanism.

Old model evidence, prices, input, and package remain immutable. This is a new
run and a new package for the SAME ingress fact, authorized by a server-written
audit. It is not a synthetic ingress event or an in-place snapshot rewrite.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from .workflow_snapshot_store import read_run_snapshot_document

import hashlib
import json
from sqlalchemy import select

from .audit import append_audit_event, canonical_json
from .failure_console import FailureConsoleError
from .models import (AuditEvent, ContentIngressEvent, MaterialPackage, MaterialPackageItem,
    ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, WorkflowModelInvocation, DownstreamPublishLedger)
from .failure_resolution_models import ManualFailureResolution

ACTION='incremental_reassessment_authorized'


def replacement(db, run_id):
    return db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.source_run_id==run_id,
        ProductionRun.source_type=='content_ingress_event').order_by(ProductionRun.id).limit(1))


def assert_no_replacement(db, run):
    child=replacement(db,run.id)
    if child is not None:
        raise FailureConsoleError('failure_already_reassessed',f'已由新运行 {child.id} 接手，请处理新运行',status_code=409)


def _eligible(db, run):
    if run.status!='failed' or run.source_type!='content_ingress_event' or run.model_execution_mode!='real':
        raise FailureConsoleError('reassessment_not_failed_incremental','仅终态失败的真实增量可以重评')
    assert_no_replacement(db,run)
    active_step=db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt.id).where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id==run.id,
        ProductionStepAttempt.status.in_(('running','leased'))).limit(1))
    active_call=db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation.id).where(WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id==run.id,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.status=='reserved').limit(1))
    delivered=db.scalar(select(DownstreamPublishLedger.id).where(DownstreamPublishLedger.origin_run_key==run.run_key).limit(1))
    manual=db.scalar(select(ManualFailureResolution.id).where(ManualFailureResolution.production_run_id==run.id,
        ManualFailureResolution.status=='published').limit(1))
    if active_step or active_call or delivered or manual:
        raise FailureConsoleError('reassessment_conflict','仍有在途请求或已发布结果，拒绝产生第二份结果')


def preview(db, run_id):
    from .incremental_mechanisms import resolve_incremental_mechanism_for_enqueue
    from .incremental_autorun import _real_execution_plan
    run=db.get(ProductionRun,run_id)
    if run is None:raise FailureConsoleError('failure_run_not_found','运行不存在',status_code=404)
    _eligible(db,run)
    frozen=resolve_incremental_mechanism_for_enqueue(db,run.category_key)
    if frozen.workflow_version_id==run.workflow_version_id:
        raise FailureConsoleError('reassessment_same_mechanism','当前机制与原运行相同，请使用原检查点恢复')
    runtime=read_run_snapshot_document(run)['runtime_context']
    old_input=runtime['input_manifest']
    if not isinstance(old_input.get('asset_id'),int) or 'case' in old_input:
        raise FailureConsoleError('reassessment_unsupported_input','当前恢复只支持单张图片素材')
    event=db.get(ContentIngressEvent,run.source_id)
    if event is None or event.status!='applied':
        raise FailureConsoleError('reassessment_missing_ingress','原接入事件不可用')
    mechanism={'schema_version':'category-incremental-mechanism-snapshot-v1',
        'category_key':run.category_key,'content_ingress_event_db_id':event.id,
        'content_ingress_event_id':event.event_id,'content_record_id':event.content_record_id,
        'binding_id':frozen.binding_id,'binding_lock_version':frozen.binding_lock_version,
        'workflow_version_id':frozen.workflow_version_id,'snapshot_hash':frozen.snapshot_hash,
        'workflow_snapshot':frozen.workflow_snapshot,'input_manifest':old_input,
        'input_manifest_hash':hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(old_input).encode()).hexdigest(),
        **({'incremental_route':runtime['incremental_route']} if 'incremental_route' in runtime else {})}
    budget, limits, route=_real_execution_plan(db,snapshot=mechanism,include_route=True)
    if route.get('mode')!='online':
        raise FailureConsoleError('reassessment_not_online','本次恢复仅允许当前在线接入点')
    basis={'source_run_id':run.id,'old_snapshot_hash':run.snapshot_hash,
        'new_workflow_version_id':frozen.workflow_version_id,'new_snapshot_hash':frozen.snapshot_hash,
        'input_hash':mechanism['input_manifest_hash'],'budget':budget,'route':route}
    quote_hash=hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(basis).encode()).hexdigest()
    return {**basis,'quote_hash':quote_hash,'mechanism':mechanism,'node_limits':limits}


def validate_authority(db, request, package):
    """Called by the ordinary real execution authorization, never trusts caller metadata."""
    if type(request.source_run_id) is not int:
        return False
    old=db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.id==request.source_run_id).with_for_update())
    if old is None or old.source_id!=request.source_id or old.category_key!=request.category_key:
        return False
    _eligible(db,old)
    event=db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.event_key==f'incremental-reassessment:{old.id}',
        AuditEvent.action==ACTION))
    if event is None:return False
    authority=json.loads(event.payload_json)
    return (authority['old_snapshot_hash']==old.snapshot_hash
        and authority['new_package_id']==package.id
        and authority['new_workflow_version_id']==request.workflow_version_id
        and authority['idempotency_key']==request.idempotency_key
        and authority['input_hash']==hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(dict(request.input_manifest)).encode()).hexdigest())


def create(db, *, run_id, quote_hash, reason, actor):
    from .workflow_runtime import ProductionRunRequest, create_production_run, _content_ingress_run_key
    old=db.get(ProductionRun,run_id)
    if old is None:raise FailureConsoleError('failure_run_not_found','运行不存在',status_code=404)
    # Consistent event -> old run ordering with normal incremental authorization.
    db.scalar(select(ContentIngressEvent).where(ContentIngressEvent.id==old.source_id).with_for_update())
    old=db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.id==run_id).with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    prior=replacement(db,old.id)
    if prior:
        authority=db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.event_key==f'incremental-reassessment:{old.id}'))
        saved=json.loads(authority.payload_json) if authority else {}
        if saved.get('quote_hash')!=quote_hash or saved.get('reason')!=reason.strip():
            raise FailureConsoleError('reassessment_idempotency_conflict','已存在不同的重评授权',status_code=409)
        return prior
    if not reason.strip() or not actor.strip():raise ValueError('actor and reason required')
    quoted=preview(db,run_id)
    if quote_hash!=quoted['quote_hash']:raise FailureConsoleError('reassessment_stale','机制或额度已变化，请重新预览')
    mechanism=quoted['mechanism'];input_manifest=mechanism['input_manifest']
    idempotency_key=_content_ingress_run_key(mechanism['content_ingress_event_id'],mechanism['snapshot_hash'])
    package=MaterialPackage(package_key=f'reassessment:{old.id}:{mechanism["snapshot_hash"][:32]}',
        name=f'失败重评 · 原运行 {old.id}',source='production_import',category_key=old.category_key,
        workflow_version_id=mechanism['workflow_version_id'],mechanism_snapshot_json=canonical_json(mechanism),
        status='incremental_pending',incremental_event_id=old.source_id,
        incremental_snapshot_hash=mechanism['snapshot_hash'],created_by=actor)
    db.add(package);db.flush()
    db.add(MaterialPackageItem(package_id=package.id,asset_id=input_manifest['asset_id'],
        original_name='failed-incremental-reassessment',position=1,duplicate=True));db.flush()
    append_audit_event(db,category='failure_console',action=ACTION,subject_type='production_run',subject_id=old.id,
        actor=actor,event_key=f'incremental-reassessment:{old.id}',payload={
            **{k:v for k,v in quoted.items() if k not in {'mechanism','node_limits'}},
            'new_package_id':package.id,'idempotency_key':idempotency_key,'reason':reason.strip(),
            'old_cost_liability_retained':True,'input_unchanged':True})
    db.flush()
    child=create_production_run(db,ProductionRunRequest(
        workflow_version_id=mechanism['workflow_version_id'],idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
        category_key=old.category_key,queue_class='production_batch',source_type='content_ingress_event',
        source_id=old.source_id,source_run_id=old.id,model_execution_mode='real',
        runtime_context={'run_mode':'incremental','material_package_id':package.id,
            **({'incremental_route':mechanism['incremental_route']} if 'incremental_route' in mechanism else {})},
        input_manifest=input_manifest,owner='operator-reassessment',reason=reason.strip()),actor=actor)
    package.status='incremental_sent'
    append_audit_event(db,category='failure_console',action='incremental_reassessment_created',
        subject_type='production_run',subject_id=old.id,actor=actor,
        event_key=f'incremental-reassessment-created:{old.id}',payload={'old_run_id':old.id,'new_run_id':child.id,
            'quote_hash':quote_hash,'new_workflow_version_id':child.workflow_version_id})
    db.flush()
    return child


def settle(db, *, run_id, actor):
    """Retire old dispatch only after the replacement has an accepted receipt."""
    from .models import RuntimeDispatchItem, RuntimeOutboxEvent
    old=db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.id==run_id).with_for_update())
    child=replacement(db,run_id)
    if old is None or child is None or child.status!='succeeded':
        raise FailureConsoleError('reassessment_not_complete','新运行尚未成功，不能结清旧死信')
    events=list(db.scalars(select(RuntimeOutboxEvent).join(DownstreamPublishLedger,
        DownstreamPublishLedger.outbox_event_id==RuntimeOutboxEvent.id).where(
        DownstreamPublishLedger.origin_run_key==child.run_key)))
    if len(events)!=1 or events[0].status!='delivered' or not json.loads(events[0].delivery_receipt_json):
        raise FailureConsoleError('reassessment_not_delivered','尚无唯一的下游接受回执')
    key=f'incremental-reassessment-settled:{old.id}'
    existing=db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.event_key==key))
    if existing:return json.loads(existing.payload_json)
    items=list(db.scalars(select(RuntimeDispatchItem).join(ProductionStepAttempt,
        ProductionStepAttempt.id==RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id).where(
        ProductionStepAttempt.run_id==old.id,RuntimeDispatchItem.status=='dead_lettered').with_for_update()))
    receipt={'old_run_id':old.id,'new_run_id':child.id,'outbox_event_id':events[0].id,
        'old_snapshot_hash':old.snapshot_hash,'retired_dispatch_ids':[i.id for i in items]}
    for item in items:item.status='canceled'
    append_audit_event(db,category='failure_console',action='incremental_reassessment_settled',
        subject_type='production_run',subject_id=old.id,actor=actor,event_key=key,payload=receipt)
    db.flush()
    return receipt


def main():
    import argparse
    from .database import SessionLocal
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('action',choices=['preview','create','settle'])
    parser.add_argument('--run-id',type=int,required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--quote-hash')
    parser.add_argument('--reason',default='')
    parser.add_argument('--actor',default='')
    args=parser.parse_args()
    with SessionLocal() as db:
        if args.action=='preview':
            result=preview(db,args.run_id)
            result={k:v for k,v in result.items() if k not in {'mechanism','node_limits'}}
            db.rollback()
        elif args.action=='create':
            child=create(db,run_id=args.run_id,quote_hash=args.quote_hash,reason=args.reason,actor=args.actor)
            result={'old_run_id':args.run_id,'new_run_id':child.id,'workflow_version_id':child.workflow_version_id,'status':child.status}
            db.commit()
        else:
            if not args.actor.strip():parser.error('--actor required for settle')
            result=settle(db,run_id=args.run_id,actor=args.actor);db.commit()
        print(json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False,default=str))

if __name__=='__main__':main()

===== FILE backend/app/incremental_routing.py =====
"""Durable business ownership for incremental automation, independent of model policy."""
from __future__ import annotations
from .workflow_snapshot_store import read_run_snapshot_text, read_run_snapshot_document

import hashlib
import json
from sqlalchemy import select, func, or_, and_
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import append_audit_event, canonical_json
from .incremental_mechanisms import IncrementalMechanismError
from .models import IncrementalRoutingPolicy, IncrementalEntityAssignment


def _error(code, message):
    return IncrementalMechanismError(code, message, status_code=409)


def current_policy(db, *, category_key, workflow_definition_id, current_read=False):
    statement = select(IncrementalRoutingPolicy).where(
        IncrementalRoutingPolicy.category_key == category_key,
        IncrementalRoutingPolicy.workflow_definition_id == workflow_definition_id,
    ).order_by(IncrementalRoutingPolicy.revision.desc()).limit(1)
    if current_read:
        statement = statement.with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    return db.scalar(statement)


def admission_policy(db, *, category_key, workflow_definition_id):
    scope = dict(category_key=category_key, workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id)
    _lock(db, scope)
    policy = current_policy(db, **scope, current_read=True)
    if policy is None and db.scalar(select(IncrementalRoutingPolicy.id).where(
        IncrementalRoutingPolicy.category_key == category_key).limit(1)) is not None:
        raise _error("routing_handover_required", "此类目已有路由归属，新绑定工作流必须配置策略并完成归属交接")
    return policy


def policy_payload(policy):
    if policy is None:
        return None
    return {key: getattr(policy, key) for key in (
        "id", "category_key", "workflow_definition_id", "revision", "mode",
        "ratio_percent", "admission_paused", "ownership_contract", "actor", "reason", "created_at",
    )}


def _lock(db, scope, *, shared=False):
    from .incremental_automation import _lock_definition
    _lock_definition(db, scope["workflow_definition_id"], shared=shared)


def configure_policy(db: Session, *, category_key, workflow_definition_id,
                     expected_revision, mode, ratio_percent, reason, actor,
                     ownership_contract="", _paused=None, _handover=False):
    scope = dict(category_key=category_key, workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id)
    if not reason.strip():
        raise _error("routing_reason_required", "必须填写策略变更原因")
    if type(ratio_percent) is not int or not 0 <= ratio_percent <= 100 or mode not in {"shadow", "split"}:
        raise _error("routing_policy_invalid", "分流比例必须是 0..100 整数，模式必须为影子或分流")
    if mode == "split" and not ownership_contract.strip():
        raise _error("routing_ownership_required", "分流必须登记双方遵守的归属合同或联调验收编号")
    _lock(db, scope)
    previous = current_policy(db, **scope, current_read=True)
    if expected_revision != (previous.revision if previous else 0):
        raise _error("routing_revision_conflict", "路由策略版本已变化，请刷新")
    if previous and mode != previous.mode and not _handover:
        raise _error("routing_handover_required", "运行模式变化必须经过暂停排空和归属交接")
    policy = IncrementalRoutingPolicy(**scope, revision=expected_revision + 1, mode=mode,
        ratio_percent=ratio_percent, admission_paused=(previous.admission_paused if previous else True) if _paused is None else _paused,
        ownership_contract=ownership_contract.strip(), actor=actor, reason=reason.strip())
    db.add(policy)
    db.flush()
    append_audit_event(db, category="incremental_routing", action="policy_changed",
        subject_type="workflow_definition", subject_id=workflow_definition_id, actor=actor,
        payload={"category_key": category_key, "revision": policy.revision, "mode": mode,
                 "ratio_percent": ratio_percent, "admission_paused": policy.admission_paused,
                 "previous_policy_id": previous.id if previous else None, "reason": reason})
    return policy_payload(policy)


def set_admission(db, *, category_key, workflow_definition_id, expected_revision, paused, reason, actor):
    scope = dict(category_key=category_key, workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id)
    _lock(db, scope)
    policy = current_policy(db, **scope, current_read=True)
    if policy is None:
        raise _error("routing_policy_missing", "请先保存路由策略")
    return configure_policy(db, **scope, expected_revision=expected_revision, mode=policy.mode,
        ratio_percent=policy.ratio_percent, ownership_contract=policy.ownership_contract,
        reason=reason, actor=actor, _paused=paused)


def assignment_payload(db, assignment):
    policy = db.get(IncrementalRoutingPolicy, assignment.policy_id)
    return {"assignment_id": assignment.id, "policy_id": policy.id,
        "policy_revision": policy.revision, "mode": policy.mode,
        "destination": assignment.destination, "ownership_epoch": assignment.ownership_epoch,
        "identity_hash": assignment.identity_hash}


def admit_entity(db, *, category_key, workflow_definition_id, source_system, entity_id):
    scope = dict(category_key=category_key, workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition_id)
    _lock(db, scope)
    policy = current_policy(db, **scope, current_read=True)
    if policy is None or policy.admission_paused:
        return None
    if not source_system or not entity_id:
        raise _error("routing_identity_missing", "路由归属缺少稳定来源或业务身份")
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json([source_system, category_key, entity_id]).encode()).hexdigest()
    existing = db.scalar(select(IncrementalEntityAssignment).where(IncrementalEntityAssignment.identity_hash == digest))
    if existing:
        if existing.workflow_definition_id != workflow_definition_id:
            raise _error("routing_handover_required", "业务实体已归属另一工作流，须显式交接")
        return assignment_payload(db, existing)
    bucket = int(digest[:16], 16) % 10000
    selected = bucket < policy.ratio_percent * 100
    assignment = IncrementalEntityAssignment(**scope, identity_hash=digest,
        source_system=source_system, entity_id=entity_id, policy_id=policy.id,
        destination=("shadow" if policy.mode == "shadow" else "formal") if selected else ("excluded" if policy.mode == "shadow" else "old"),
        ownership_epoch=1, bucket=bucket)
    db.add(assignment)
    db.flush()
    return assignment_payload(db, assignment)


def authoritative_route(db, *, source_type, source_id, category_key, workflow_definition_id, claimed_route):
    """Caller JSON is never authority; the immutable ingress package is."""
    from .models import MaterialPackage, ContentRecord
    from .workflow_runtime import WorkflowRuntimeError
    package = None
    if source_type == "content_ingress_event" and source_id is not None:
        package = db.scalar(select(MaterialPackage).where(MaterialPackage.incremental_event_id == source_id))
    frozen = json.loads(package.mechanism_snapshot_json).get("incremental_route") if package else None
    if frozen != claimed_route:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError("incremental_route_mismatch", "运行归属与冻结接入事实不一致", status_code=409)
    if frozen is None:
        return None
    assignment = db.get(IncrementalEntityAssignment, frozen.get("assignment_id"), populate_existing=True)
    if (assignment is None or assignment.category_key != category_key
        or assignment.workflow_definition_id != workflow_definition_id
        or assignment_payload(db, assignment) != frozen
        or assignment.destination not in {"shadow", "formal"}):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError("incremental_ownership_lost", "归属缺失或写入权已交接，禁止继续执行", status_code=409)
    if assignment.destination == "formal":
        package_snapshot = json.loads(package.mechanism_snapshot_json)
        frozen_input = package_snapshot["input_manifest"]
        record = db.scalar(select(ContentRecord).where(ContentRecord.id == frozen_input["content_record_id"])
            .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
        if record is None or record.status == "deleted" or record.source_version != frozen_input["source_version"]:
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError("incremental_source_superseded", "源版本已更新或删除，旧运行禁止覆盖正式结果", status_code=409)
    return frozen


def validate_run_route(db, run):
    # Validation only reads ownership. Handover/admission mutation keeps the
    # exclusive fence; unrelated run validations need not serialize each other.
    _lock(db, {"workflow_definition_id": run.workflow_definition_id}, shared=True)
    context = read_run_snapshot_document(run).get("runtime_context", {})
    return authoritative_route(db, source_type=run.source_type, source_id=run.source_id,
        category_key=run.category_key, workflow_definition_id=run.workflow_definition_id,
        claimed_route=context.get("incremental_route"))


def fence_outbox_payload(db, payload):
    from .models import ProductionRun
    from .workflow_runtime import WorkflowRuntimeError
    run_key = payload.get("origin_run_key")
    run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.run_key == run_key)) if run_key else None
    if run is None:
        if payload.get("incremental_route") is not None or payload.get("source_revision") is not None:
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError("incremental_route_mismatch", "下游归属缺少原始运行", status_code=409)
        return
    route = validate_run_route(db, run)
    if route is not None and (route["destination"] != "formal" or payload.get("incremental_route") != route):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError("incremental_destination_fenced", "影子结果或归属不一致的结果禁止写入正式下游", status_code=409)
    from .incremental_source_revision import authoritative_source_revision, source_revision_advances
    from .workflow_snapshot_store import load_snapshot_blob
    source_revision = authoritative_source_revision(db, run)
    if source_revision is not None and (payload.get("source_type") != run.source_type
        or not isinstance(payload.get("source_id"), int) or isinstance(payload.get("source_id"), bool)
        or payload.get("source_id") != run.source_id
        or payload.get("asset_identity") != source_revision["content_key"]):
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError("incremental_source_mismatch", "下游源版本身份与原始运行不一致", status_code=409)
    if "source_revision" in payload and payload["source_revision"] != source_revision:
        raise WorkflowRuntimeError("incremental_source_mismatch", "下游源版本证据与冻结接入事实不一致", status_code=409)
    if (route is not None or source_revision is not None) and payload.get("review_state") == "auto":
        from .runtime_outbox import latest_outbox_event
        latest = latest_outbox_event(db, target_contract_key=payload["target_contract_key"],
            business_idempotency_key=payload["business_idempotency_key"], current_read=True)
        if latest is not None and latest.review_state == "reviewed" and not source_revision_advances(load_snapshot_blob(latest.payload_blob), payload):
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError("incremental_review_precedence", "同一源版本已有人工纠偏，禁止自动结果覆盖", status_code=409)


def persist_shadow_result(db, run, *, route, step_key, output_manifest):
    from .models import IncrementalShadowResult
    snapshot = read_run_snapshot_document(run)
    context = snapshot["runtime_context"]
    frozen_input = context.get("input_manifest", {})
    result_json = canonical_json(dict(output_manifest))
    result_hash = hashlib.sha256(result_json.encode()).hexdigest()
    existing = db.scalar(select(IncrementalShadowResult).where(
        IncrementalShadowResult.run_key == run.run_key,
        IncrementalShadowResult.step_key == step_key,
        IncrementalShadowResult.result_hash == result_hash))
    if existing is None:
        existing = IncrementalShadowResult(assignment_id=route["assignment_id"], run_key=run.run_key,
            step_key=step_key, workflow_version_id=run.workflow_version_id,
            snapshot_hash=hashlib.sha256(read_run_snapshot_text(run).encode()).hexdigest(),
            event_id=str(context.get("content_ingress_event_id", "")),
            source_version=str(frozen_input.get("source_version", "")), policy_revision=route["policy_revision"],
            result_json=result_json, result_hash=result_hash,
            evidence_json=canonical_json({"model_execution_mode": run.model_execution_mode,
                "cost_source": "workflow_model_invocations", "comparison_status": "missing_old_result"}))
        db.add(existing)
        db.flush()
    return existing


def drain_state(db, *, category_key, workflow_definition_id):
    from .models import ProductionRun, MaterialPackage, WorkflowVersion, RuntimeOutboxEvent, WorkflowModelInvocation, ModelInvocationReview
    versions = select(WorkflowVersion.id).where(WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id == workflow_definition_id)
    runs = select(ProductionRun.id).where(ProductionRun.workflow_definition_id == workflow_definition_id,
        ProductionRun.category_key == category_key, ProductionRun.source_type == "content_ingress_event")
    pending = db.scalar(select(func.count(MaterialPackage.id)).where(MaterialPackage.workflow_version_id.in_(versions),
        MaterialPackage.category_key == category_key, MaterialPackage.status.in_(["incremental_pending", "incremental_blocked"]))) or 0
    in_flight = db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id)).where(ProductionRun.id.in_(runs),
        ProductionRun.status.not_in(["succeeded", "failed", "canceled"]))) or 0
    outbox = db.scalar(select(func.count(RuntimeOutboxEvent.id)).where(RuntimeOutboxEvent.mechanism_version_id.in_(versions),
        RuntimeOutboxEvent.status.not_in(["delivered", "canceled"]))) or 0
    # Narrow by status/error before joining historical runs. The deployment's
    # ix_invocations_error_run read index avoids scanning the invocation ledger.
    # UNION de-duplicates an invocation that is both reserved and unknown.
    unresolved = select(WorkflowModelInvocation.id, WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id).where(
        WorkflowModelInvocation.status == "reserved").union(select(
        WorkflowModelInvocation.id, WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id).where(
            WorkflowModelInvocation.error_code == "model_invocation_outcome_unknown",
            ~select(ModelInvocationReview.invocation_id).where(
                ModelInvocationReview.invocation_id == WorkflowModelInvocation.id).exists())).subquery()
    reserved = db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(unresolved).where(
        unresolved.c.production_run_id.in_(runs))) or 0
    reviewed = db.scalar(select(func.count(ModelInvocationReview.invocation_id)).join(
        WorkflowModelInvocation, WorkflowModelInvocation.id == ModelInvocationReview.invocation_id).where(
        WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id.in_(runs))) or 0
    watermark = db.scalar(select(func.max(MaterialPackage.incremental_event_id)).where(
        MaterialPackage.workflow_version_id.in_(versions), MaterialPackage.category_key == category_key))
    return {"pending": pending, "in_flight": in_flight, "outbox_unresolved": outbox,
        "provider_unresolved": reserved, "provider_reviewed_billing_pending": reviewed, "accepted_event_watermark": watermark,
        "drained": pending + in_flight + outbox + reserved == 0}
