                    or not entry["review_result"].strip()):
                raise ValueError
            strings.add(entry["review_result"])
        if (set(values) != strings or any(type(v) is not int for v in values.values())
                or len(set(values.values())) != len(values)):
            raise ValueError
        return mapping, values
    except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
        raise _error("mapping_invalid") from exc


class InspirationFactoryDownstreamAdapter:
    def __init__(self, url: str, *, level_mapping: Mapping, review_result_values: Mapping,
                 journal_path: str, send: Callable[[str, dict], bytes] = send_inspiration_request,
                 clock: Callable[[], float] = time.time) -> None:
        # Endpoint is resolved by resolve_locator_endpoint in the resolver.
        if url not in INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS:
            raise _error("endpoint_invalid")
        self._url = url
        self._level_mapping = dict(level_mapping)
        self._review_values = dict(review_result_values)
        self._send = send
        self._clock = clock
        self._path = _private_journal_path(journal_path)
        self._expected: dict[str, tuple[DeliveryKey, dict, dict, int | None]] = {}
        self._init_journal()
        self._journal_inode = self._path.stat().st_ino

    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
        _private_journal_path(str(self._path))
        if not self._path.is_file():
            raise _error("journal_file_missing")
        if (hasattr(self, "_journal_inode")
                and self._path.stat().st_ino != self._journal_inode):
            raise _error("journal_file_changed")
        conn = sqlite3.connect(str(self._path), timeout=5, isolation_level=None)
        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        conn.execute("PRAGMA synchronous=FULL")
        conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=DELETE")
        conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000")
        return conn

    @contextmanager
    def _journal(self):
        conn = self._connect()
        try:
            with conn:
                yield conn
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def _init_journal(self) -> None:
        created = False
        if not self._path.exists():
            try:
                fd = os.open(self._path, os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_WRONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o600)
                os.close(fd)
                created = True
            except FileExistsError:
                pass
        _private_journal_path(str(self._path))
        with self._journal() as conn:
            conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            if created:
                conn.execute("CREATE TABLE metadata (name TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value TEXT NOT NULL)")
                conn.execute("CREATE TABLE intents ("
                             "task_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, business_key TEXT NOT NULL, revision INTEGER NOT NULL, "
                             "review_state TEXT NOT NULL, mechanism_version_id INTEGER NOT NULL, "
                             "payload_hash TEXT NOT NULL, body_hash TEXT NOT NULL, state TEXT NOT NULL "
                             "CHECK(state IN ('sending','unknown','confirmed','rejected')))")
            else:
                tables = {row[0] for row in conn.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")}
                if not {"metadata", "intents"}.issubset(tables):
                    raise _error("journal_schema_invalid")
            endpoint = conn.execute("SELECT value FROM metadata WHERE name='endpoint'").fetchone()
            if endpoint is None:
                if not created:
                    raise _error("journal_schema_invalid")
                conn.execute("INSERT INTO metadata VALUES ('endpoint', ?)", (self._url,))
            elif endpoint[0] != self._url:
                raise _error("journal_endpoint_mismatch")
            conn.commit()

    def verify_target(self) -> DownstreamSafetyEvidence:
        _private_journal_path(str(self._path))
        if not self._path.is_file() or self._path.stat().st_ino != self._journal_inode:
            raise _error("journal_file_changed")
        return DownstreamSafetyEvidence(True, INSPIRATION_FACTORY_SCHEMA_FINGERPRINT,
                                        "inspiration-factory-json-journal:" + self._url)

    def _get_task(self, identity: Mapping) -> dict:
        if not inspiration_endpoint_enabled(self._url):
            raise _error("disabled")
        raw = self._send(self._url, request_envelope("getTask", {"task_id": identity["task_id"]},
                                                      int(self._clock())))
        return parse_task(_json_response(raw), identity)

    @staticmethod
    def _manual_failure(payload: Mapping, output: Mapping) -> bool:
        """Only the console's correlated manual-failure evidence permits a first review."""
        override = output.get("human_override")
        if not isinstance(override, Mapping) or override.get("schema_version") != "manual-failure-resolution-v1":
            return False
        result = payload.get("result")
        change = payload.get("change")
        manual = result.get("manual_resolution") if isinstance(result, Mapping) else None
        normalized = output.get("normalized_result")
        if (not isinstance(change, Mapping) or not isinstance(manual, Mapping)
                or not isinstance(normalized, Mapping)
                or change.get("trigger_kind") != "human_correction"
                or result.get("status") != "manual_resolution"
                or output.get("schema_version") != "inspiration-factory-result-v1"
                or output.get("prompt_a_status") != "manual" or output.get("prompt_b_status") != "manual"
                or normalized.get("source") != "manual_failure_resolution"
                or normalized.get("level") != output.get("final_level")
                or normalized.get("score") != output.get("final_score")
                or override.get("field") != "final_level"
                or override.get("level") != output.get("final_level")):
            raise _error("override_invalid")
        for field in ("actor", "reason", "correction_event_id"):
            value = override.get(field)
            if (not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip()
                    or manual.get(field) != value or change.get(field) != value):
                raise _error("override_invalid")
        return True

    @staticmethod
    def _human(payload: Mapping, output: Mapping) -> bool:
        frozen = output.get("human_override")
        change = payload.get("change")
        trigger = change.get("trigger_kind") if isinstance(change, Mapping) else None
        if frozen is not None and (not isinstance(frozen, Mapping)
                                   or (frozen.get("schema_version") != "incremental-level-override-v1"
                                       and not InspirationFactoryDownstreamAdapter._manual_failure(payload, output))):
            raise _error("override_invalid")
        if trigger not in {None, "", "auto_run", "human_correction"}:
            raise _error("override_invalid")
        if frozen is not None and trigger == "auto_run":
            raise _error("override_conflict")
        return frozen is not None or trigger == "human_correction"

    def _prepare(self, key: DeliveryKey, payload: object) -> tuple[dict, dict, int | None, str]:
        if (not isinstance(payload, Mapping) or type(key.revision) is not int or key.revision < 1
                or key.review_state not in {"auto", "reviewed"}
                or not isinstance(key.business_idempotency_key, str)
                or not key.business_idempotency_key):
            raise _error("payload_invalid")
        result = payload.get("result")
        output = result.get("output") if isinstance(result, Mapping) else None
        if not isinstance(output, Mapping):
            raise _error("output_invalid")
        human = self._human(payload, output)
        if human != (key.review_state == "reviewed"):
            raise _error("review_state_conflict")
        if output.get("schema_version") == "inspiration-factory-failure-v1":
            if human or key.revision != 1 or result.get("status") != "failed":
                raise _error("failure_revision_invalid")
            body = build_failure_submit_body(output)
            expected_review = None
        else:
            body = build_submit_body(output, level_mapping=self._level_mapping,
                                     human_override={"override_source": "human_correction"} if human else None)
            expected_review = self._review_values[body["review_result"]]
        identity = task_identity(output["inspiration_task"])
        return body, identity, expected_review, hashlib.sha256(_canonical(body)).hexdigest()

    def _stored(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection, task_id: int):
        return conn.execute("SELECT * FROM intents WHERE task_id=?", (task_id,)).fetchone()

    @staticmethod
    def _row_verdict(row, key: DeliveryKey, body_hash: str, *, manual_failure: bool = False) -> str | None:
        if row is None:
            return "first" if key.revision == 1 and (key.review_state == "auto"
                or (key.review_state == "reviewed" and manual_failure)) else "revision_invalid"
        if row["business_key"] != key.business_idempotency_key:
            return "business_key_conflict"
        if key.revision < row["revision"]:
            return "stale_revision"
        if key.revision == row["revision"]:
            if (row["payload_hash"] != key.payload_hash or row["body_hash"] != body_hash
                    or row["review_state"] != key.review_state
                    or row["mechanism_version_id"] != key.mechanism_version_id):
                return "identity_conflict"
            return "same"
        if row["state"] != "confirmed":
            if row["state"] == "rejected" and key.review_state == "reviewed" and manual_failure:
                # 上一修订被工厂明确拒绝、什么都没写：订正后的人工结论仍按首次落地处理
                # （任务仍是「已领取」在 _reserve 里核对）。没有意向记录时仍只认修订 1。
                return "first"
            return "prior_unknown"
        if key.review_state != "reviewed":
            return "review_guard"
        return "new_review"

    def _reserve(self, key: DeliveryKey, body_hash: str, identity: dict,
                 task: dict, equal: bool, settled: bool, *, manual_failure: bool = False,
                 plain: bool = False) -> str:
        with self._journal() as conn:
            conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            row = self._stored(conn, identity["task_id"])
            verdict = self._row_verdict(row, key, body_hash, manual_failure=manual_failure)
            if verdict not in {"first", "same", "new_review"}:
                conn.commit()
                return verdict
            if manual_failure and verdict != "same" and plain != (verdict == "first"):
                # 提交体形态是加锁前按投递日志选的（_landing_plain）；这期间意向变了就不发，
                # 留给人工重放，不拿首次落地的形态去覆盖已有结果，也不反过来。
                conn.commit()
                return "journal_intent_changed"
            if verdict == "first" and manual_failure and task["status"] != 1:
                conn.commit()
                return "status_invalid"
            if verdict == "same":
                if row["state"] == "rejected":
                    conn.commit()
                    return "rejected"
                if equal and row["state"] != "confirmed":
                    conn.execute("UPDATE intents SET state='confirmed' WHERE task_id=?",
                                 (identity["task_id"],))
                conn.commit()
                if equal:
                    return "confirmed"
                # 提交结果未知、或意图已落而没发完：工厂还没落定时只读等待，同一提交绝不重发。
                # 工厂已落定却不一致，才是确定的不一致（10-01 事件 78663：工厂落定滞后，重放即确认）。
                if row["state"] in ("sending", "unknown") and not settled:
                    return "unconfirmed"
                return "already_sent"
            if equal:
                state = "confirmed"
            else:
                if task["status"] != (1 if verdict == "first" else 4):
                    conn.commit()
                    return "status_invalid"
                state = "sending"
            conn.execute("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO intents VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
                         (identity["task_id"], key.business_idempotency_key, key.revision,
                          key.review_state, key.mechanism_version_id, key.payload_hash,
                          body_hash, state))
            conn.commit()
            return "confirmed" if equal else "send"

    def _mark(self, key: DeliveryKey, task_id: int, body_hash: str, state: str) -> None:
        with self._journal() as conn:
            conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            changed = conn.execute(
                "UPDATE intents SET state=? WHERE task_id=? AND business_key=? AND revision=? "
                "AND review_state=? AND mechanism_version_id=? AND payload_hash=? AND body_hash=? "
                "AND state!='rejected'",
                (state, task_id, key.business_idempotency_key, key.revision, key.review_state,
                 key.mechanism_version_id, key.payload_hash, body_hash),
            ).rowcount
            conn.commit()
            if changed != 1:
                raise _error("journal_intent_changed")

    @staticmethod
    def _matches(task: dict, body: dict, expected_review: int | None) -> bool:
        if body.get("result_status") == "failed":
            return matches_failure_readback(task, body)
        return matches_readback(task, body, expected_review_result=expected_review)

    @staticmethod
    def _settled(task: dict, key: DeliveryKey, *, first_landing: bool | None = None) -> bool:
        """本次提交已被工厂落定：首次落地提交前任务是 1，之后变为 4（成功）或 5（失败）才算。

        人工新修订提交前任务本来就是 4，状态不能区分"旧结果仍可见"与"本次确定不一致"，
        一律按未落定只读等待（r30 评审 S1）；同一提交仍绝不重发。首次落地通常是修订 1；
        订正后的人工失败结论修订号更大，但提交前任务同样是 1（r32）。
        """
        first = key.revision == 1 if first_landing is None else first_landing
        return first and task.get("status") in (4, 5)

    def _landing_plain(self, task_id: int, key: DeliveryKey, plain_hash: str) -> bool:
        """人工失败结论这次投递是否是任务的首次落地：是则按普通提交发，不带人工纠偏标记。

        工厂只允许对已提交、已完成或已失败的任务带 override_source（10-01 任务 171061：
        「只有已提交、已完成或已失败的任务允许人工纠偏」）。形态由投递日志决定，重试不变：
        同一修订沿用已记录的那一种；此前修订已确认，这次才是带标记的覆盖。
        """
        with self._journal() as conn:
            row = self._stored(conn, task_id)
        if row is None:
            return True
        if row["revision"] == key.revision:
            return row["body_hash"] == plain_hash
        return row["revision"] < key.revision and row["state"] == "rejected"

    def _unconfirmed_intent(self, task_id: int) -> bool:
        try:
            with self._journal() as conn:
                row = self._stored(conn, task_id)
        except Exception:
            return False
        return row is not None and row["state"] in ("sending", "unknown")

    def _fresh_equal(self, body: dict, identity: dict, expected_review: int | None) -> bool:
        task = self._get_task(identity)
        return self._matches(task, body, expected_review)

    def _one(self, key: DeliveryKey, payload: object) -> DeliveryWriteResult:
        try:
            body, identity, expected_review, body_hash = self._prepare(key, payload)
            manual_failure = self._manual_failure(payload, payload["result"]["output"])
            plain = False
            if manual_failure and "override_source" in body:
                candidate = {name: value for name, value in body.items() if name != "override_source"}
                candidate_hash = hashlib.sha256(_canonical(candidate)).hexdigest()
                if self._landing_plain(identity["task_id"], key, candidate_hash):
                    body, body_hash, plain = candidate, candidate_hash, True
            first_landing = key.revision == 1 or plain
            self._expected[key.business_idempotency_key] = (key, body, identity, expected_review)
            # Read before a new intent; an unknown prior intent remains read-only.
            try:
                task = self._get_task(identity)
            except (ConnectorDeliveryError, InspirationFactoryContractError):
                raise
            except Exception as exc:
                # Nothing has been written or sent yet, so a transport failure
                # or server-side error here is safe to retry; dead-lettering it
                # turned a few seconds of factory jitter into lost results.
                if isinstance(exc, HTTPError) and not (exc.code in (408, 429) or 500 <= exc.code < 600):
                    raise
                if not isinstance(exc, OSError):  # URLError/HTTPError/timeouts
                    raise
                # 已有未确认的提交意图时这是确认中的读回故障：同样隔 30 秒再读，
                # 不能按秒级退避把确认机会用完（r30 评审 R1）。首次发送前的抖动仍用默认退避。
                return DeliveryWriteResult(key, False, error_code="inspiration_factory_gettask_unavailable",
                                           retryable=True,
                                           retry_after_seconds=READBACK_CONFIRM_DELAY_SECONDS
                                           if self._unconfirmed_intent(identity["task_id"]) else None)
            equal = self._matches(task, body, expected_review)
            decision = self._reserve(key, body_hash, identity, task, equal,
                                     self._settled(task, key, first_landing=first_landing),
                                     manual_failure=manual_failure, plain=plain)
            if decision == "confirmed":
                return DeliveryWriteResult(key, True, f"inspiration:task:{identity['task_id']}")
            if decision == "unconfirmed":
                return DeliveryWriteResult(key, False, error_code="inspiration_factory_submit_unconfirmed",
                                           retryable=True, retry_after_seconds=READBACK_CONFIRM_DELAY_SECONDS)
            if decision != "send":
                return DeliveryWriteResult(key, False, error_code="inspiration_factory_" + decision,
                                           retryable=False)
            # The committed sending intent is the last operation before the POST.
            definitive_rejection = False
            refusal = ""
            with _submit_slots:
                if not inspiration_endpoint_enabled(self._url):
                    raise _error("disabled")
                try:
                    raw = self._send(self._url, request_envelope("submit", body, int(self._clock())))
                    response = _json_response(raw)
                    # 工厂业务拒绝时自己写的那句原因：留给审计，不再和超时一起吞成「未知」。
                    refusal = refusal_message(response)
                    if (isinstance(response, dict) and isinstance(response.get("data"), dict)
                            and response["data"].get("accepted") is False):
                        definitive_rejection = True
                    receipt = parse_submit(response, body["task_id"])
                    if receipt["status"] != (5 if body["result_status"] == "failed" else 4):
                        definitive_rejection = True
                except Exception:
                    # Network and malformed receipts are all unknown outcomes.
                    pass
            if definitive_rejection:
                self._mark(key, identity["task_id"], body_hash, "rejected")
                return DeliveryWriteResult(key, False, error_code="inspiration_factory_submit_rejected",
                                           retryable=False, diagnostic=refusal)
            settled = False
            untouched = False
            try:
                task = self._get_task(identity)
                if self._matches(task, body, expected_review):
                    self._mark(key, identity["task_id"], body_hash, "confirmed")
                    return DeliveryWriteResult(key, True, f"inspiration:task:{identity['task_id']}")
                settled = self._settled(task, key, first_landing=first_landing)
                untouched = untouched_task(task)
            except Exception:
                pass
            if refusal and untouched and manual_failure:
                # 工厂明确拒绝并给了说明，回读任务仍是「已领取」、什么都没写：确定没有落地。
                # 只对人工失败结论这样判——订正后的结论可以再作首次落地；自动结果沿用未知语义。
                self._mark(key, identity["task_id"], body_hash, "rejected")
                return DeliveryWriteResult(key, False, error_code="inspiration_factory_submit_rejected",
                                           retryable=False, diagnostic=refusal)
            self._mark(key, identity["task_id"], body_hash, "unknown")
            if settled:
                # 工厂已落定却与本次提交不一致：确定的不一致，不再等待。
                return DeliveryWriteResult(key, False, error_code="inspiration_factory_submit_unknown",
                                           retryable=False, diagnostic=refusal)
            # 工厂还没落定或读回失败：按投递只读重试确认，下一次 _reserve 走 same 路径，绝不重发。
            return DeliveryWriteResult(key, False, error_code="inspiration_factory_submit_unconfirmed",
                                       retryable=True, retry_after_seconds=READBACK_CONFIRM_DELAY_SECONDS,
                                       diagnostic=refusal)
        except (ConnectorDeliveryError, InspirationFactoryContractError) as exc:
            code = exc.code if isinstance(exc, ConnectorDeliveryError) else str(exc)
            return DeliveryWriteResult(key, False, error_code=code, retryable=False)
        except Exception:
            return DeliveryWriteResult(key, False, error_code="inspiration_factory_delivery_failed",
                                       retryable=False)

    def deliver(self, batch: DeliveryBatch) -> DeliveryAttempt:
        if not inspiration_endpoint_enabled(self._url):
            return DeliveryAttempt(tuple(DeliveryWriteResult(
                item.key, False, error_code="inspiration_factory_disabled", retryable=False,
            ) for item in batch.items))
        return DeliveryAttempt(tuple(self._one(item.key, item.payload) for item in batch.items))

    def read_back(self, keys: Sequence[DeliveryKey]) -> tuple[DeliveredRow, ...]:
        if not inspiration_endpoint_enabled(self._url):
            return ()
        rows: list[DeliveredRow] = []
        for key in keys:
            expected = self._expected.get(key.business_idempotency_key)
            if expected is None or expected[0] != key:
                continue
            _, body, identity, review = expected
            try:
                if self._fresh_equal(body, identity, review):
                    self._mark(key, identity["task_id"],
                               hashlib.sha256(_canonical(body)).hexdigest(), "confirmed")
                    rows.append(DeliveredRow(key, f"inspiration:task:{identity['task_id']}"))
            except Exception:
                continue
        return tuple(rows)


def inspiration_factory_configuration(target: ConnectorContractVersion) -> tuple:
    """Validate configuration without creating journals or performing HTTP."""
    if (target.direction != "downstream" or target.category_key != "inspiration_image"
            or target.adapter_key != INSPIRATION_FACTORY_ADAPTER_KEY):
        raise _error("adapter_unavailable")
    if target.schema_fingerprint != INSPIRATION_FACTORY_SCHEMA_FINGERPRINT:
        raise _error("schema_drift")
    mapping, values = _mapping_config(target.field_mapping_json)
    path = os.getenv(JOURNAL_ENV)
    if not path:
        raise _error("journal_path_missing")
    _private_journal_path(path)
    return (resolve_locator_endpoint(target.logical_locator, allowed=INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS),
            mapping, values, path)


def resolve_inspiration_factory_adapter(
    target: ConnectorContractVersion, *,
    send: Callable[[str, dict], bytes] = send_inspiration_request,
    clock: Callable[[], float] = time.time,
) -> InspirationFactoryDownstreamAdapter:
    endpoint, mapping, values, path = inspiration_factory_configuration(target)
    return InspirationFactoryDownstreamAdapter(
        endpoint, level_mapping=mapping,
        review_result_values=values, journal_path=path, send=send, clock=clock,
    )

===== FILE backend/app/inspiration_factory_failure.py =====
"""Opt-in, transaction-bound failure notification for owned inspiration input."""
from .workflow_snapshot_store import read_run_snapshot_document
from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import replace
import hashlib
import json

from sqlalchemy import select

from .audit import canonical_json, append_audit_event
from .inspiration_factory_contract import build_failure_submit_body
from .inspiration_factory_identity import validate_frozen_identity, SOURCE_SYSTEM
from .models import ConnectorContractVersion, RuntimeOutboxEvent

FAILURE_MODE = "inspiration-factory-failure-v1"


def enqueue_terminal_failure(db, run):
    """Called with the run locked, after its terminal state is established.

    Existing mechanisms have no opt-in and preserve their original behavior.
    Failure notifications cannot replace any previous business publication.
    No HTTP is performed here; delivery still requires the explicit factory gate.
    """
    if (run.status != "failed" or run.category_key != "inspiration_image"
            or run.source_type != "content_ingress_event"
            or run.model_execution_mode != "real"):
        return None
    snapshot = read_run_snapshot_document(run)
    if not isinstance(snapshot, Mapping):
        raise ValueError("inspiration_failure_snapshot_invalid")
    context = snapshot.get("runtime_context", {})
    workflow = snapshot.get("workflow", {})
    manifest = workflow.get("manifest", {}) if isinstance(workflow, Mapping) else {}
    if (not isinstance(context, Mapping) or context.get("run_mode") != "incremental"
            or context.get("model_execution_mode") != "real"):
        return None
    if not isinstance(manifest, Mapping) or not isinstance(manifest.get("nodes", []), list):
        raise ValueError("inspiration_failure_snapshot_invalid")
    nodes = []
    for node in manifest.get("nodes", []):
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping) or node.get("kind") != "downstream_projection":
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        mapping = config.get("mapping") if isinstance(config, Mapping) else None
        if isinstance(mapping, Mapping) and mapping.get("terminal_failure_mode") == FAILURE_MODE:
            nodes.append(node)
    if not nodes:
        return None

    def invalid(reason):
        raise ValueError("inspiration_failure_" + reason)

    if (len(nodes) != 1 or manifest.get("category_key") != "inspiration_image"
            or hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(snapshot).encode()).hexdigest() != run.snapshot_hash):
        invalid("snapshot_invalid")
    frozen = context.get("input_manifest", {})
    if not isinstance(frozen, Mapping) or frozen.get("source_system") != SOURCE_SYSTEM:
        invalid("source_invalid")
    identity = validate_frozen_identity(frozen, frozen.get("inspiration_task"))
    node = nodes[0]; config = node["config"]; step_key = node.get("node_instance_id")
    if (not isinstance(step_key, str) or not step_key
            or config["mapping"].get("schema_version") != "inspiration-factory-result-v1"
            or config.get("completion_mode", "continue") != "continue"
            or not isinstance(manifest.get("edges", []), list)
            or any(not isinstance(edge, Mapping) or edge.get("from") == step_key
                   for edge in manifest.get("edges", []))):
        invalid("projection_invalid")
    from .inspiration_factory_delivery import (
        INSPIRATION_FACTORY_ADAPTER_KEY, INSPIRATION_FACTORY_SCHEMA_FINGERPRINT,
    )
    connector = config.get("connector_snapshot", {})
    if not isinstance(connector, Mapping):
        invalid("target_invalid")
    target_id = connector.get("connector_contract_id")
    target = db.get(ConnectorContractVersion, target_id) if type(target_id) is int else None
    if (target is None or target.category_key != "inspiration_image"
            or target.adapter_key != INSPIRATION_FACTORY_ADAPTER_KEY
            or target.schema_fingerprint != INSPIRATION_FACTORY_SCHEMA_FINGERPRINT
            or connector.get("schema_fingerprint") != target.schema_fingerprint
            or target.direction != "downstream" or target.contract_key != config.get("connector_key")):
        invalid("target_invalid")
    output = {"schema_version": FAILURE_MODE, "inspiration_task": identity,
              "failure": {"terminal_status": "failed", "run_key": run.run_key,
                          "snapshot_hash": run.snapshot_hash}}
    build_failure_submit_body(output)
    result = {"schema_version": "workflow-node-result-v1", "node_instance_id": step_key,
              "node_kind": "downstream_projection", "status": "failed", "output": output}
    event_key = "inspiration-failure:" + hashlib.sha256(
        (run.run_key + ":" + step_key).encode()).hexdigest()
    existing = db.scalar(select(RuntimeOutboxEvent).where(RuntimeOutboxEvent.event_key == event_key))
    if existing is not None:
        from .workflow_snapshot_store import load_snapshot_blob
        prior = load_snapshot_blob(existing.payload_blob)
        if (prior.get("origin_run_key") != run.run_key or prior.get("result") != result
                or existing.target_contract_version_id != target.id):
            invalid("idempotency_conflict")
        return existing
    from .workflow_runtime import build_incremental_projection_outbox_request
    from .runtime_outbox import enqueue_runtime_outbox
    from .downstream_publish_ledger import record_downstream_publish
    # Reuse source-version, handover, shadow and human-precedence checks.
    request = build_incremental_projection_outbox_request(
        db, run, step_key=step_key, output_manifest=result)
    if request is None:
        return None
    if request.revision != 1:
        invalid("prior_publication")
    event = enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, replace(request, event_key=event_key, max_attempts=1))
    record_downstream_publish(db, event=event, payload=request.payload)
    append_audit_event(db, category="workflow_runtime", action="inspiration_failure_enqueued",
        subject_type="production_run", subject_id=run.id, actor="runtime",
        payload={"outbox_event_id": event.id, "snapshot_hash": run.snapshot_hash,
                 "external_io_performed": False},
        dedupe_by_content=True)
    return event


def record_terminal_failure(db, run):
    """A refused notification must not roll back the original terminal failure."""
    from .runtime_outbox import RuntimeOutboxError
    from .workflow_runtime import WorkflowRuntimeError
    try:
        with db.begin_nested():
            return enqueue_terminal_failure(db, run)
    except (ValueError, RuntimeOutboxError, WorkflowRuntimeError) as exc:
        # Keep provider text and upstream payloads out of the notification audit.
        code = getattr(exc, "code", None) or "inspiration_failure_evidence_invalid"
        append_audit_event(db, category="workflow_runtime", action="inspiration_failure_blocked",
            subject_type="production_run", subject_id=run.id, actor="runtime",
            payload={"code": code, "external_io_performed": False},
            dedupe_by_content=True)
        return None

===== FILE backend/app/inspiration_factory_identity.py =====
"""Bind Lgt task IDs to their environment and immutable content input."""
import hashlib
from collections.abc import Mapping

from .factory_test_claim import INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS, inspiration_upstream_prefixes
from .inspiration_factory_contract import task_identity

SOURCE_SYSTEM = 'inspiration-factory-material'
CATEGORY_KEY = 'inspiration_image'
#: incremental_materials.submit_manifest 的上限；超了连物料任务都建不出来。
CONTENT_ID_MAX = 160
CONTENT_VERSION_MAX = 120
SUBMISSION_ID_MAX = 160


def content_identity(task, *, url):
    task = task_identity(task)
    prefixes = inspiration_upstream_prefixes(url)
    content = (f"inspiration-{prefixes.identity}:{task['cloud_id']}"
               f":{task['project_id']}:task:{task['task_id']}")
    if len(content) > CONTENT_ID_MAX:
        # 工厂合同允许 project_id 最长 120 字符，拼上环境前缀和两个数字 ID 会超过 160（评审）。
        # 超长时把项目 ID 换成定长摘要：同一任务永远算出同一个身份，短的保持原样（已有记录不受影响）。
        digest = hashlib.sha256(str(task['project_id']).encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()[:32]
        content = (f"inspiration-{prefixes.identity}:{task['cloud_id']}"
                   f":sha256-{digest}:task:{task['task_id']}")
    version = 'inspiration-' + prefixes.version + str(task['task_id'])
    submission = 'inspiration-' + prefixes.submission + str(task['task_id'])
    return content, version, submission


def validate_frozen_identity(frozen: Mapping, task) -> dict:
    normalized = task_identity(task)
    if (frozen.get('source_system') != SOURCE_SYSTEM
            or frozen.get('category_key') != CATEGORY_KEY):
        raise ValueError('inspiration_source_identity_invalid')
    for url in INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS:
        content, version, _ = content_identity(normalized, url=url)
        if (frozen.get('source_content_id') == content
                and frozen.get('source_version') == version):
            return normalized
    raise ValueError('inspiration_source_identity_mismatch')


def identity_fits(content: str, version: str, submission: str) -> bool:
    """身份三件套都在物料任务的长度上限内（确认领取前要先过这一关）。"""
    return (0 < len(content) <= CONTENT_ID_MAX and 0 < len(version) <= CONTENT_VERSION_MAX
            and 0 < len(submission) <= SUBMISSION_ID_MAX)

===== FILE backend/app/inspiration_factory_intake.py =====
"""Disabled-by-default Lgt intake; durable ownership precedes real image jobs.

The factory claim only lists. A source=2 getTask and a new confirmed receipt
are both required. Unknown confirmation outcomes remain quarantined forever;
neither status=1 nor already_claimed is proof of our ownership.
"""
from contextlib import closing
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import fcntl
import io
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sqlite3
import time

from PIL import Image
from sqlalchemy import select

from .factory_pump import _private_fd
from .factory_test_claim import ClaimGateError, inspiration_upstream_prefixes
from .factory_material_stream import DailyAllowance, _settle_allowance
from .inspiration_factory_contract import request_envelope, parse_claim, parse_task, parse_confirm, task_identity
from .inspiration_factory_identity import SOURCE_SYSTEM, CATEGORY_KEY, content_identity, identity_fits
from .inspiration_factory_transport import send_inspiration_request
from .inspiration_factory_access import ENABLED_ENV, inspiration_endpoint_enabled
from .incremental_material_models import IncrementalMaterialJob
from . import incremental_materials

def _decode(raw):
    from .inspiration_factory_delivery import _json_response
    return _json_response(raw)


def _connection(path):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(path, timeout=5)
    conn.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL')
    return conn


def _enqueue(journal, *, sessions, enabled, upstream, limit, allowance=None, rejected=None, now=None):
    count = 0
    with closing(_connection(journal)) as db:
        rows = db.execute("SELECT task_id,payload,first_seen,confirmation FROM tasks WHERE state='confirmed' ORDER BY task_id LIMIT ?", (limit,)).fetchall()
        for ident, raw, observed, receipt in rows:
            if not enabled():
                break
            proof = parse_confirm(json.loads(receipt), [ident]) if receipt else {}
            if ident not in proof.get('confirmed_task_ids', []):
                raise ClaimGateError('inspiration_ownership_receipt_required')
            task = task_identity(json.loads(raw))
            if task['task_id'] != ident:
                raise ClaimGateError('inspiration_task_identity_conflict')
            content, version, submission = content_identity(task, url=upstream)
            payload = dict(content_id=content, content_version=version, category_key=CATEGORY_KEY,
                material={'url': task['image_url']}, inspiration_task=task,
                source_time_kind='platform_first_observed')
            occurred = datetime.fromisoformat(observed)
            if occurred.tzinfo is None:
                raise ClaimGateError('inspiration_observation_timezone_required')
            try:
                with sessions() as platform:
                    incremental_materials.submit_manifest(platform, source_system=SOURCE_SYSTEM,
                        submission_id=submission, occurred_at=occurred, payload=payload,
                        actor='inspiration-factory-material-intake')
                    platform.commit()
            except incremental_materials.MaterialError as exc:
                # 物料清单被确定性拒绝（重试也一样）：单独标记、释放预算占用后继续下一条。
                # 否则队列按任务号排序，每一轮都先撞上这一条，整个灵感图进料停摆（评审）。
                db.execute("UPDATE tasks SET state='material_rejected' WHERE task_id=? AND state='confirmed'", (ident,))
                db.commit()
                if allowance is not None:
                    allowance.finish(ident, now=now)
                if rejected is not None:
                    rejected.append({'task_id': ident, 'code': exc.code})
                continue
            # A crash here is replay-safe via the material job's unique identity.
            db.execute("UPDATE tasks SET state='material_enqueued' WHERE task_id=? AND state='confirmed'", (ident,))
            db.commit()
            count += 1
    return count


INSPIRATION_EXTRA_TOTALS = ('network_calls', 'foreign', 'identity_rejected', 'material_rejected')


def run_intake_loop(cycle, *, loop_seconds, interval_seconds, clock=None, sleep=None):
    """单次调用内连续跑多轮灵感图进料（与 3DSU 的 run_stream_loop 同一套收手条件）。

    09-30 并发复盘：灵感图进料每次调用只跑一轮（per_cycle 上限 10）就退出，timer 再空等 30 s，
    折合约 820 条/小时，而本地排队为 0、.205 每实例只跑 0～1 个请求——卡点在进料节奏。
    每轮仍是完整的 stream_cycle（平台开关、日额度、max_pending 检查都在里面），上游没料、
    到 pending 上限、stop/error 立刻收手。灵感图特有的计数也逐轮累加。
    """
    import time
    from .factory_material_stream import run_stream_loop
    extra = {key: 0 for key in INSPIRATION_EXTRA_TOTALS}

    def counted():
        out = cycle()
        for key in extra:
            if type(out.get(key)) is int:
                extra[key] += out[key]
        return out

    result = run_stream_loop(counted, loop_seconds=loop_seconds, interval_seconds=interval_seconds,
                             clock=clock or time.monotonic, sleep=sleep or time.sleep)
    return {**result, **extra}


def stream_cycle(journal, *, sessions, controls, upstream, daily_limit_micros,
                 per_task_micros, max_pending=10, per_cycle=1,
                 send=send_inspiration_request, now=None, clock=time.time):
    def enabled():
        if not inspiration_endpoint_enabled(upstream):
            return False
        flags = controls()
        return isinstance(flags, dict) and all(flags.get(k) is True for k in ('poll', 'confirm', 'material'))

    # Includes no directory creation, database access or network when disabled.
    if not enabled():
        return {'stop': 'operator_verification_pending', 'material_enqueued': 0, 'network_calls': 0}
    prefixes = inspiration_upstream_prefixes(upstream)
    from .inspiration_factory_delivery import _private_journal_path
    path = _private_journal_path(str(journal))
    if (type(max_pending) is not int or not 1 <= max_pending <= 256
            or type(per_cycle) is not int or not 1 <= per_cycle <= 10
            or type(per_task_micros) is not int or not 0 < per_task_micros <= daily_limit_micros):
        raise ClaimGateError('inspiration_intake_limit_invalid')
    now = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if now.tzinfo is None:
        raise ClaimGateError('inspiration_aware_time_required')
    lock = _private_fd(str(path)+'.lock')
    try:
        try:
            fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        except BlockingIOError:
            return {'stop': 'busy', 'material_enqueued': 0}
        budget = Path(str(path)+'.allowance.sqlite')
        if path.exists() and not budget.exists():
            raise ClaimGateError('inspiration_allowance_missing')
        allowance = DailyAllowance(budget, daily_limit_micros)
        os.close(_private_fd(path))
        with closing(_connection(path)) as db:
            db.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
            db.execute('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS config (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(id=1), endpoint TEXT NOT NULL)')
            db.execute('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tasks (task_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,payload TEXT NOT NULL,state TEXT NOT NULL,first_seen TEXT NOT NULL,confirmation TEXT)')
            tables = {r[0] for r in db.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")}
            if tables != {'config', 'tasks'}:
                raise ClaimGateError('inspiration_dedicated_journal_required')
            prior = db.execute('SELECT endpoint FROM config WHERE id=1').fetchone()
            if prior and prior[0] != upstream:
                raise ClaimGateError('inspiration_endpoint_changed')
            if prior is None:
                db.execute('INSERT INTO config VALUES (1,?)', (upstream,))
            db.execute("UPDATE tasks SET state='unknown' WHERE state='confirming'")
            db.commit()
        _settle_allowance(allowance, sessions, now, source_system=SOURCE_SYSTEM,
                          submission_prefix='inspiration-'+prefixes.submission)
        with closing(_connection(path)) as db:
            owned = {r[0] for r in db.execute("SELECT task_id FROM tasks WHERE state='confirmed'")}
        if not owned <= allowance.recorded():
            raise ClaimGateError('inspiration_reservation_required')
        rejected = []
        enqueued = _enqueue(path, sessions=sessions, enabled=enabled, upstream=upstream, limit=per_cycle,
                            allowance=allowance, rejected=rejected, now=now)
        result = dict(material_enqueued=enqueued, confirmed=0, network_calls=0, foreign=0, image_rejected=0,
                      identity_rejected=0, material_rejected=len(rejected))
        def call(action, body):
            if not enabled():
                raise ClaimGateError('inspiration_operator_verification_pending')
            result['network_calls'] += 1
            return _decode(send(upstream, request_envelope(action, body, int(clock()))))
        for _ in range(per_cycle-enqueued):
            if not enabled():
                result['stop']='platform_paused'; break
            if len(allowance.outstanding()) >= max_pending:
                result['stop']='pending_limit'; break
            candidates = parse_claim(call('claim', {'limit': 10}))
            if len(candidates)>10:
                raise ClaimGateError('inspiration_claim_limit_exceeded')
            with closing(_connection(path)) as db:
                known = {r[0] for r in db.execute('SELECT task_id FROM tasks')}
            selected = None
            for listed in candidates:
                ident = listed['task_id']
                if ident in known:
                    continue
                expected = {**listed, 'model_source': 2}
                raw = call('getTask', {'task_id': ident})
                if raw.get('data', {}).get('model_source') == 1:
                    with closing(_connection(path)) as db:
                        db.execute("INSERT INTO tasks VALUES (?,?,'foreign',?,NULL)", (ident,json.dumps(listed),now.isoformat()))
                        db.commit()
                    result['foreign'] += 1
                    continue
                task = parse_task(raw, expected)
                if task['status'] != 0:
                    continue
                content, version, submission = content_identity(task, url=upstream)
                if not identity_fits(content, version, submission):
                    # 建不出物料任务的身份绝不能先向工厂确认领取：领了处理不了，任务会一直挂在我们名下
                    with closing(_connection(path)) as db:
                        db.execute("INSERT INTO tasks VALUES (?,?,'identity_rejected',?,NULL)", (ident,json.dumps(task_identity(task)),now.isoformat()))
                        db.commit()
                    result['identity_rejected'] += 1
                    continue
                with sessions() as platform:
                    if platform.scalar(select(IncrementalMaterialJob.id).where(
                            IncrementalMaterialJob.source_system==SOURCE_SYSTEM,
                            IncrementalMaterialJob.submission_id==submission)):
                        continue
                try:
                    image = incremental_materials._download(task['image_url'], incremental_materials._policy(SOURCE_SYSTEM))
                    with Image.open(io.BytesIO(image)) as picture:
                        picture.verify()
                except (ValueError, OSError):
                    with closing(_connection(path)) as db:
                        db.execute("INSERT INTO tasks VALUES (?,?,'image_rejected',?,NULL)", (ident,json.dumps(task_identity(task)),now.isoformat()))
                        db.commit()
                    result['image_rejected'] += 1
                    continue
                selected = task_identity(task)
                break
            if selected is None:
                break
            if not enabled():
                result['stop']='platform_paused'; break
            ident = selected['task_id']
            # Download may take time. Revalidate both ownership availability
            # and source identity immediately before reserving/confirming.
            current = parse_task(call('getTask', {'task_id': ident}), selected)
            if current['status'] != 0:
                continue
            if not allowance.reserve(ident, per_task_micros, now):
                result['stop']='daily_allowance'; break
            # Persist BEFORE any confirm bytes. Even BaseException/process death
            # leaves a confirming row that a restart demotes to unknown.
            with closing(_connection(path)) as db:
                db.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
                db.execute("INSERT INTO tasks VALUES (?,?,'confirming',?,NULL)", (ident,json.dumps(selected),now.isoformat()))
                db.commit()
            try:
                raw = call('confirmClaim', {'task_ids': [ident]})
                receipt = parse_confirm(raw, [ident])
                state = ('confirmed' if ident in receipt['confirmed_task_ids'] else
                         'elsewhere' if ident in receipt['already_claimed_task_ids'] else 'rejected')
                with closing(_connection(path)) as db:
                    db.execute('UPDATE tasks SET state=?,confirmation=? WHERE task_id=?', (state,json.dumps(raw),ident))
                    db.commit()
            except Exception:
                with closing(_connection(path)) as db:
                    db.execute("UPDATE tasks SET state='unknown' WHERE task_id=?", (ident,)); db.commit()
                result['error']='inspiration_confirmation_unknown'; break
            if state != 'confirmed':
                allowance.finish(ident, now=now)
                continue
            result['confirmed'] += 1
            result['material_enqueued'] += _enqueue(path, sessions=sessions, enabled=enabled, upstream=upstream, limit=1,
                                                    allowance=allowance, rejected=rejected, now=now)
            result['material_rejected'] = len(rejected)
        return result
    finally:
        os.close(lock)

===== FILE backend/app/inspiration_factory_projection.py =====
"""Pure terminal projection of Qwen A facts and server-owned inspiration score."""
from collections.abc import Mapping
from copy import deepcopy


def project_a_fields(raw, *, source=None, draft=False):
    """Share the normal A-field projection with unvalidated console drafts."""
    from .inspiration_production_fields import published_fields, class_text_from_categories
    if source is None:
        source = raw.get('production_fields', raw)
    if not isinstance(source, Mapping):
        if not draft:
            raise ValueError('inspiration_projection_fields_invalid')
        source = {}
    source = {**dict(source), 'class_text': class_text_from_categories(raw)}
    return published_fields(source, draft=draft)


def project_inspiration(context, mapping):
    from .workflow_node_executors import (
        NodeExecutorError, _ExecutorOutput, _workflow_manifest, _frozen_run_input,
        connector_node_evidence, redact_workflow_secrets,
    )
    from .workflow_result_finalizer import reduce_native_workflow_result
    from .workflow_short_circuit import enabled_levels_from_manifest

    def refuse(code):
        raise NodeExecutorError(code, '灵感图投递需要完整的冻结身份、A 输出与服务端计分证据')

    manifest = _workflow_manifest(context)
    node_id = context.node.get('node_instance_id')
    nodes = {n.get('node_instance_id'): n for n in manifest.get('nodes', []) if isinstance(n, Mapping)}
    a_id, b_id = mapping.get('prompt_a_node_id'), mapping.get('prompt_b_node_id')
    if (manifest.get('category_key') != 'inspiration_image'
            or not isinstance(a_id, str) or not isinstance(b_id, str) or a_id == b_id
            or any(nodes.get(k, {}).get('kind') != 'prompt' for k in (a_id, b_id))
            or any(e.get('from') == node_id for e in manifest.get('edges', []) if isinstance(e, Mapping))):
        refuse('inspiration_projection_contract_invalid')
    from .inspiration_metadata_generation import resolve_metadata_node, merge_metadata
    try:
        metadata_id = resolve_metadata_node(manifest, mapping, node_id)
    except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError, AttributeError) as exc:
        raise NodeExecutorError('inspiration_metadata_wiring_invalid', '发布说明节点必须位于评分之后并使用完整字段合同') from exc
    upstream = context.input_manifest.get('upstream', {})
    skipped = context.input_manifest.get('skipped_upstream', [])
    if not isinstance(upstream, Mapping) or not isinstance(skipped, list):
        refuse('inspiration_projection_incomplete')
    for key in nodes.keys() - {node_id}:
        entry = upstream.get(key, {})
        if not isinstance(entry, Mapping) or (entry.get('status') not in {'succeeded', 'skipped'} and key not in skipped):
            refuse('inspiration_projection_incomplete')
    runtime = context.run_snapshot.get('runtime_context', {})
    try:
        normalized = reduce_native_workflow_result(manifest, upstream,
            model_execution_mode=runtime.get('model_execution_mode', 'simulated'))
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise NodeExecutorError('inspiration_projection_invalid', str(exc)) from exc
    if normalized.get('level') not in enabled_levels_from_manifest(manifest):
        refuse('inspiration_projection_invalid')
    # A's facts are required even when a frozen redline skips B. B's dimension
    # contract has no mandatory aesthetic_score scalar: the reducer owns score.
    a = upstream.get(a_id, {})
    if a.get('status') != 'succeeded' or not isinstance(a.get('output'), Mapping):
        refuse('inspiration_projection_a_missing')
    b = upstream.get(b_id, {})
    if b.get('status') == 'skipped' or b_id in skipped:
        decision = normalized.get('short_circuit_decision', {})
        if (normalized.get('decision_source') != 'frozen_short_circuit'
                or b_id not in decision.get('skipped_nodes', [])):
            refuse('inspiration_projection_unproven_skip')
    elif b.get('status') != 'succeeded' or not isinstance(b.get('output'), Mapping):
        refuse('inspiration_projection_b_missing')
    raw = redact_workflow_secrets(deepcopy(dict(a['output'])))
    source = raw.get('production_fields', raw)
    if not isinstance(source, Mapping):
        refuse('inspiration_projection_fields_invalid')
    if metadata_id is not None:
        metadata = upstream.get(metadata_id, {})
        if metadata.get('status') != 'succeeded':
            refuse('inspiration_metadata_missing')
        try:
            from .inspiration_metadata_bounds import postcheck_enabled
            source = merge_metadata(source, metadata.get('output'),
                output_contract=nodes[metadata_id]['config']['output_contract'],
                postcheck=postcheck_enabled(manifest))
        except ValueError as exc:
            refuse(str(exc))
    # 下游 class_text 以 A 段一级/二级分类为准，不用 C 段自由生成的分类文字
    try:
        fields = project_a_fields(raw, source=source)
    except ValueError as exc:
        refuse(str(exc))
    final_score = normalized.get('score')
    score_source = 'reducer'
    if final_score is None and normalized.get('decision_source') == 'frozen_short_circuit':
        # 冻结短路（如 class_two 直接定 L3、二维码直接 L5）只给等级不给分；工厂提交要求整数分，
        # 用冻结等级表里该等级的下限分代替，并在 output 里标明来源。
        try:
            final_score = short_circuit_level_floor(manifest, normalized['level'])
        except ValueError:
            refuse('inspiration_short_circuit_floor_ambiguous')
        if final_score is None:
            refuse('inspiration_short_circuit_floor_missing')
        score_source = 'short_circuit_level_floor'
    output = dict(schema_version='inspiration-factory-result-v1', a_fields=fields,
        final_score=final_score, final_level=normalized['level'], score_source=score_source,
        normalized_result=normalized, external_io_performed=False)
    frozen = _frozen_run_input(context)
    if frozen.get('source_system') == 'inspiration-factory-material':
        from .inspiration_factory_identity import validate_frozen_identity
        try:
            output['inspiration_task'] = validate_frozen_identity(frozen, frozen.get('inspiration_task'))
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise NodeExecutorError('inspiration_projection_identity_invalid', str(exc)) from exc
    return _ExecutorOutput(output=output, extra=connector_node_evidence(
        run_snapshot=context.run_snapshot, node=context.node, payload=output))


def short_circuit_level_floor(manifest, level):
    """冻结 score_level_matcher 里 ``level`` 的下限分；表里没有该等级返回 None。

    与评分器读同样的三套表（legacy_score_thresholds / legacy_level_thresholds / levels）。
    发布机制时三套表已被要求一致（mechanism_authoring_policy）；万一冻结快照里同一等级的下限分
    不一致，就抛 ValueError，不静默取第一套——否则工厂收到的分数会和评审口径对不上。
    """
    import json
    for node in manifest.get('nodes', []):
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping) or node.get('kind') != 'rule_module':
            continue
        config = node.get('config') if isinstance(node.get('config'), Mapping) else {}
        if config.get('module_type_key') != 'score_level_matcher':
            continue
        snapshot = config.get('component_snapshot') if isinstance(config.get('component_snapshot'), Mapping) else {}
        raw = snapshot.get('content_json')
        try:
            content = json.loads(raw) if isinstance(raw, str) else (snapshot.get('content') or {})
        except ValueError:
            return None
        floors = set()
        for key in ('legacy_score_thresholds', 'legacy_level_thresholds', 'levels'):
            rows = content.get(key) if isinstance(content, Mapping) else None
            for row in rows if isinstance(rows, list) else []:
                if isinstance(row, Mapping) and row.get('level') == level and type(row.get('min_score')) is int:
                    floors.add(max(0, min(100, row['min_score'])))
        if len(floors) > 1:
            raise ValueError('score_level_matcher tables disagree on ' + str(level))
        return next(iter(floors), None)
    return None

===== FILE backend/app/inspiration_factory_transport.py =====
"""Bounded JSON transport for the distinct LgtInternalLLM factory protocol."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from urllib.request import Request, build_opener, HTTPSHandler

from .factory_test_claim import (
    ClaimGateError, INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS, MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES, NoRedirect,
    verified_tls_context,
)
from .inspiration_factory_access import inspiration_endpoint_enabled


def send_inspiration_request(url: str, envelope: dict) -> bytes:
    # Resolve endpoints through the same deployment allowlist as 3DSU, while
    # keeping JSON and multipart protocols separate. Redirects remain denied.
    if url not in INSPIRATION_UPSTREAMS:
        raise ClaimGateError('inspiration_endpoint_not_allowed')
    if (not isinstance(envelope, dict)
            or set(envelope) != {'service', 'action', 'time', 'key', 'body'}
            or envelope.get('service') != 'LgtInternalLLM'
            or envelope.get('action') not in {'claim', 'confirmClaim', 'getTask', 'submit'}):
        raise ClaimGateError('inspiration_envelope_invalid')
    # Read-only diagnostics may list/get tasks while intake is held. Ownership
    # and result writes require the exact endpoint's operator switch as well.
    if (envelope['action'] in {'confirmClaim', 'submit'}
            and not inspiration_endpoint_enabled(url)):
        raise ClaimGateError('inspiration_operator_verification_pending')
    payload = json.dumps(envelope, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False,
                         separators=(',', ':')).encode('utf-8')
    if len(payload) > MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES:
        raise ClaimGateError('inspiration_request_limit')
    request = Request(url, data=payload, method='POST', headers={
        'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Accept': 'application/json',
    })
    opener = build_opener(NoRedirect(), HTTPSHandler(context=verified_tls_context()))
    with opener.open(request, timeout=30) as response:
        if response.status != 200 or response.geturl() != url:
            raise ClaimGateError('inspiration_http_rejected')
        raw = response.read(MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES + 1)
    if len(raw) > MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES:
        raise ClaimGateError('inspiration_response_limit')
    return raw

===== FILE backend/app/inspiration_metadata_bounds.py =====
"""Bound a NEW metadata contract; never mutate a published prompt or output.

141888 still truncated at 8192 tokens. Concise field-level constraints and a
complete-JSON instruction succeeded at 209 tokens. These limits are opt-in for
candidate preparation, not a runtime repair that clips model answers.
"""
from copy import deepcopy
import re

TEXT_LIMITS = {"class_text": 64, "style": 64, "cons": 240, "design": 320}
TAG_COUNT = 12
TAG_LENGTH = 32
OUTPUT_TOKENS = 4096
POSTCHECK_PROFILE = "bounded-metadata-postcheck-v1"


def postcheck_enabled(manifest):
    provenance = (manifest.get("resource_policy") or {}).get("source_provenance") or {}
    return (manifest.get("category_key") == "inspiration_image"
            and provenance.get("metadata_validation_profile") == POSTCHECK_PROFILE)
# Exact instruction validated by the original 141888 audited recovery.
GUIDANCE = '\n本次发布说明必须简洁、完整，不重复同一句话。class_text 和 style 各不超过64个字符；cons 不超过240个字符；design 不超过320个字符；tags 最多12项，每项不超过32个字符。不抄录图片里的长段文字。只描述可见事实，无法明确时沿用原有不确定表述；不得改变质量结论。输出完整 JSON 后立即结束。'


def metadata_text_conflicts(metadata):
    """Refuse a demonstrated generation loop; never clip or rewrite its text.

    Three consecutive copies of a long phrase were observed in 141888's cons.
    This is intentionally narrow and only used by the opt-in bounded contract.
    """
    conflicts = []
    for name in TEXT_LIMITS:
        value = metadata[name]
        if len(value) > TEXT_LIMITS[name]:
            conflicts.append("metadata_length:" + name)
        if re.search(r"(.{16,160}?)\1\1", value, flags=re.DOTALL):
            conflicts.append("metadata_repeated_text:" + name)
        if re.search(r'''["'“‘]([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9 -]{0,63})["'”’]\s*(?:误写为|应为|应写作|应改为|改为)\s*["'“‘]\1["'”’]''', value):
            conflicts.append("metadata_self_correction:" + name)
    if len(metadata["tags"]) > TAG_COUNT or any(len(t) > TAG_LENGTH for t in metadata["tags"]):
        conflicts.append("metadata_length:tags")
    return tuple(conflicts)


def validate_concise_text(metadata):
    conflicts = metadata_text_conflicts(metadata)
    if conflicts:
        raise ValueError("inspiration_" + conflicts[0].split(":")[0])


def bounded_contract(contract):
    from .prompt_output_contract import parse_output_contract

    parse_output_contract(contract)
    result = deepcopy(contract)
    schema = result["schema"]
    props = schema.get("properties", {})
    if (result.get("name") != "inspiration_production_metadata"
            or schema.get("type") != "object"
            or schema.get("additionalProperties") is not False
            or set(props) != {*TEXT_LIMITS, "tags"}
            or set(schema.get("required", [])) != set(props)):
        raise ValueError("metadata_bounds_source_contract_invalid")
    for name, limit in TEXT_LIMITS.items():
        field = props[name]
        if field.get("type") != "string" or field.get("minLength", 0) > limit:
            raise ValueError("metadata_bounds_field_invalid")
        field["maxLength"] = min(field.get("maxLength", limit), limit)
    tags = props["tags"]
    if (tags.get("type") != "array" or tags.get("items", {}).get("type") != "string"
            or tags.get("minItems", 0) > TAG_COUNT
            or tags["items"].get("minLength", 0) > TAG_LENGTH):
        raise ValueError("metadata_bounds_tags_invalid")
    tags["maxItems"] = min(tags.get("maxItems", TAG_COUNT), TAG_COUNT)
    tags["items"]["maxLength"] = min(tags["items"].get("maxLength", TAG_LENGTH), TAG_LENGTH)
    parse_output_contract(result)
    return result

===== FILE backend/app/inspiration_metadata_generation.py =====
"""Explicit post-scoring metadata node for new frozen inspiration workflows.

The ordinary prompt executor owns calls, quotas, costs and retries. This module
only validates the frozen wiring and joins five publishing fields to A's facts.
"""
from collections.abc import Mapping
from copy import deepcopy

from .inspiration_production_fields import METADATA_FIELDS

EXECUTION_ROLE = "production_metadata"
# PromptVersion.stage is VARCHAR(10) in the existing MySQL schema. The longer
# semantic role belongs in config; a separate C stage needs no database change.
PROMPT_STAGE = "C"
GUIDANCE = """
你负责灵感图的发布说明，不负责评分。只描述当前待评图片的可见内容。
仅输出 class_text、style、tags、cons、design 五个 JSON 字段：
class_text：主体的业务分类文字。
style：可见设计风格；证据不足时写“风格不明确”。
tags：与主体、空间、风格、材质直接相关且不重复的检索标签数组；无法识别时返回空数组。
cons：可见不足；无明显不足时写“未见明显不足”，不能因为用于推荐而隐去缺陷。
design：简洁描述构图、空间、材质、色彩或设计亮点，只写可见事实。
不要编造品牌、作者、地点或尺寸，不描述参照锚点，不猜测人工等级。
不输出或修改分数、等级、红线、赛道、媒介和评测证据。
""".strip()


def metadata_contract():
    text = {"type": "string", "minLength": 1}
    return {"mode": "json_schema", "name": "inspiration_production_metadata",
            "strict": True, "schema": {
                "type": "object", "additionalProperties": False,
                "properties": {key: ({"type": "array", "items": deepcopy(text)}
                                     if key == "tags" else deepcopy(text))
                               for key in METADATA_FIELDS},
                "required": list(METADATA_FIELDS)}}


def metadata_node_ids(manifest):
    """Exclude opt-in publishing prompts from A/B read models, not from costs."""
    if not isinstance(manifest, Mapping) or manifest.get("category_key") != "inspiration_image":
        return set()
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list):
        return set()
    return {node.get("node_instance_id") for node in nodes
            if isinstance(node, Mapping) and node.get("kind") == "prompt"
            and isinstance(node.get("node_instance_id"), str)
            and isinstance(node.get("config"), Mapping)
            and node["config"].get("execution_role") == EXECUTION_ROLE}


def resolve_metadata_node(manifest, mapping, projection_id):
    """Legacy A metadata is unchanged; an explicit C binding must be complete."""
    if "production_fields_node_id" not in mapping:
        return None

    def refuse():
        raise ValueError("inspiration_metadata_wiring_invalid")

    raw_nodes = manifest.get("nodes", [])
    if manifest.get("category_key") != "inspiration_image" or not isinstance(raw_nodes, list):
        refuse()
    nodes = {}
    for node in raw_nodes:
        key = node.get("node_instance_id") if isinstance(node, Mapping) else None
        if not isinstance(key, str) or not key or key in nodes:
            refuse()
        nodes[key] = node
    metadata_id = mapping.get("production_fields_node_id")
    a_id, b_id = mapping.get("prompt_a_node_id"), mapping.get("prompt_b_node_id")
    ids = (a_id, b_id, metadata_id, projection_id)
    if any(not isinstance(key, str) or key not in nodes for key in ids) or len(set(ids)) != 4:
        refuse()
    for key, asset_key in ((a_id, "prompt_a"), (b_id, "prompt_b"), (metadata_id, EXECUTION_ROLE)):
        if (nodes[key].get("kind") != "prompt"
                or nodes[key].get("config", {}).get("asset_key") != asset_key):
            refuse()
    if metadata_node_ids(manifest) != {metadata_id}:
        refuse()
    config = nodes[metadata_id].get("config", {})
    component = config.get("component_snapshot", {})
    content = component.get("content", {}) if isinstance(component, Mapping) else {}
    if (not isinstance(content, Mapping) or content.get("stage") != PROMPT_STAGE
            or config.get("short_circuit_rules")):
        refuse()
    from .prompt_output_contract import parse_output_contract
    contract = parse_output_contract(config.get("output_contract"))
    expected = metadata_contract()
    # Existing frozen workflows retain their original contract. New workflows
    # may opt into this exact bounded variant; do not relax arbitrary schemas.
    from .inspiration_metadata_bounds import bounded_contract
    allowed_schemas = (expected["schema"], bounded_contract(expected)["schema"])
    if (contract.mode != expected["mode"] or contract.name != expected["name"]
            or contract.strict is not True or contract.schema not in allowed_schemas):
        refuse()
    projection = nodes[projection_id]
    if (projection.get("kind") != "downstream_projection"
            or projection.get("config", {}).get("mapping") != mapping
            or mapping.get("schema_version") != "inspiration-factory-result-v1"):
        refuse()
    parents = {key: set() for key in nodes}
    children = {key: set() for key in nodes}
    for edge in manifest.get("edges", []):
        source, target = (edge.get("from"), edge.get("to")) if isinstance(edge, Mapping) else (None, None)
        if not isinstance(source, str) or not isinstance(target, str) or source not in nodes or target not in nodes:
            refuse()
        parents[target].add(source)
        children[source].add(target)
    # Reject cycles rather than accepting a path that also feeds C back to scoring.
    remaining = {key: set(value) for key, value in parents.items()}
    ready = [key for key, value in remaining.items() if not value]
    visited = set()
    while ready:
        key = ready.pop()
        if key in visited:
            continue
        visited.add(key)
        for child in children[key]:
            remaining[child].discard(key)
            if not remaining[child]:
                ready.append(child)
    if len(visited) != len(nodes) or children[projection_id] or children[metadata_id] != {projection_id}:
        refuse()
    ancestors = set()
    pending = list(parents[metadata_id])
    while pending:
        key = pending.pop()
        if key not in ancestors:
            ancestors.add(key)
            pending.extend(parents[key])
    rules = {key for key, node in nodes.items() if node.get("kind") == "rule_module"}
    if not rules or not (rules | {a_id, b_id}) <= ancestors:
        refuse()
    return metadata_id


def production_fields_ready(manifest):
    """Admission accepts existing extended A or a validated separate C contract."""
    try:
        if manifest.get("category_key") != "inspiration_image":
            return False
        nodes = manifest["nodes"]
        projections = [n for n in nodes if n.get("kind") == "downstream_projection"]
        if len(projections) != 1:
            return False
        projection = projections[0]
        mapping = projection.get("config", {}).get("mapping", {})
        a = next((n for n in nodes if n.get("kind") == "prompt"
                  and n.get("config", {}).get("asset_key") == "prompt_a"), None)
        if "production_fields_node_id" in mapping:
            required = a.get("config", {}).get("output_contract", {}).get("schema", {}).get("required", []) if a else []
            return ({"title", "seotitle", "reason", "trait", "image_defects", "track_classification"} <= set(required)
                    and resolve_metadata_node(manifest, mapping, projection["node_instance_id"]) is not None)
        return a is not None and set(METADATA_FIELDS) <= set(
            a.get("config", {}).get("output_contract", {}).get("schema", {}).get("required", []))
    except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError, AttributeError):
        return False


def merge_metadata(precheck, metadata, *, output_contract=None, postcheck=False):
    if not isinstance(precheck, Mapping) or not isinstance(metadata, Mapping) or set(metadata) != set(METADATA_FIELDS):
        raise ValueError("inspiration_metadata_fields_invalid")
    for key, value in metadata.items():
        values = value if key == "tags" and isinstance(value, list) else [value]
        if ((key == "tags" and not isinstance(value, list))
                or any(not isinstance(item, str) or not item.strip() for item in values)):
            raise ValueError("inspiration_metadata_fields_invalid")
    if output_contract is not None and output_contract != metadata_contract():
        from .inspiration_metadata_bounds import bounded_contract, validate_concise_text
        if output_contract != bounded_contract(metadata_contract()):
            raise ValueError("inspiration_metadata_wiring_invalid")
        validate_concise_text(metadata)
    elif postcheck:
        from .inspiration_metadata_bounds import validate_concise_text
        validate_concise_text(metadata)
    return {**deepcopy(dict(precheck)), **deepcopy(dict(metadata))}

===== FILE backend/app/inspiration_original_retention.py =====
"""灵感图增量原图的本地保留期：评测结束 N 天后删除 .85 上的原图文件，素材记录与评测历史全部保留。

为什么要删：灵感图原图平均约 0.7 MB，按 .205 每天约 2.5 万张算每天约 18 GB；原图放在机械盘（.85 的 /backup，
与备份共用 1.8 TB）上也得有上限。
为什么只删文件、不改 Asset.status：把素材标成 deleted 会让复评、重新进料按绑定不一致拒收；
所以只删字节，并写一条 event_key 为 ``asset:{id}:original-expired`` 的审计事件作为已过期的唯一标记。

删掉之后仍可用：等级纠偏与改判重发（含纠到 L5 触发下游下架）、结果与历史查询。
删掉之后不可用：失败重试 / 断点恢复、需要重跑模型节点的字段纠偏、复评，页面上看不到原图
（缩略图组件已有「原图已不在存储上」的占位）。

只处理「增量进料下载的灵感图原图」（category_key=inspiration_image、本地存储、文件名 material-…，可在原图子目录下）。
以下情况一律不删：
- 还没有完成进料或还没有生产运行（找不到 ContentRecord / 运行）；
- 任一相关生产运行（含以它为源的子运行）未结束，或最近变动在保留期内；
- 保留期内还有模型调用用过这张图；
- 被基准集、样本集、维度标定、设计案例或非增量素材包引用（这些是要长期复用的样本）；
- 有排队或运行中的旧评测任务，或有未结束的进料任务绑定了它；
- 文件的 sha256 与素材记录不符（不删来历不明的字节，只报告）。

删除前的生命周期锁（Astra 全仓复审 F2）：批量预筛读完引用之后，另一会话可能刚把素材加入基准集，按旧判断
unlink 会删掉长期样本的原图。所以真正删除前逐张加锁、在锁内按同一套条件新鲜复核，再删文件、写审计、提交：
- MySQL：``SELECT … FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`` 锁住素材行（锁不到就跳过，下轮再来）。基准集、样本集、维度标定、
  设计案例、素材包条目、评测任务、模型媒体用量这些引用表都有指向 ``assets.id`` 的外键，InnoDB 插入子行做外键
  检查时要对父行加共享锁：清理器持锁期间新增引用必须等它提交；先提交的引用在锁内复核时一定看得见。
- SQLite：``BEGIN IMMEDIATE`` 取得写锁，其它连接的写入同样要等。
预演（apply=False）不加锁、不写任何东西。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import String, cast, exists, func, literal, or_, select
from sqlalchemy.exc import OperationalError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .api_datetime_encoding import platform_isoformat

CATEGORY = "inspiration_image"
DEFAULT_RETENTION_DAYS = 3
ACTION = "asset_original_expired"
TERMINAL_RUN_STATUSES = frozenset({"succeeded", "canceled", "failed"})
ACTIVE_MATERIAL_JOB_STATUSES = ("queued", "processing", "retry_wait")
ACTIVE_EVALUATION_JOB_STATUSES = ("queued", "processing", "paused")
# 增量进料自动建的素材包；其余状态（手工导入的包、待投递/被拦的增量包）都要保住原图
SETTLED_PACKAGE_STATUSES = ("incremental_sent", "incremental_legacy", "deleted")


def event_key(asset_id: int) -> str:
    return f"asset:{asset_id}:original-expired"


def _naive_utc(value: datetime | None) -> datetime | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    if value.tzinfo is not None:
        value = value.astimezone(timezone.utc).replace(tzinfo=None)
    return value


def _candidates(db: Session, *, cutoff: datetime, after_id: int, chunk: int):
    from .models import Asset, AuditEvent

    expired_key = literal("asset:") + cast(Asset.id, String) + literal(":original-expired")
    return db.scalars(
        select(Asset)
        .where(
            Asset.category_key == CATEGORY,
            Asset.storage_backend == "local",
            # 进料原图在上传目录根（旧）或原图子目录（机械盘挂载点，如 originals/material-…）
            or_(Asset.stored_name.like("material-%"), Asset.stored_name.like("%/material-%")),
            Asset.created_at < cutoff,
            Asset.id > after_id,
            ~exists().where(AuditEvent.event_key == expired_key),
        )
        .order_by(Asset.id)
        .limit(chunk)
    ).all()


def _bound_by_active_material_jobs(db: Session) -> set[int]:
    from .incremental_material_models import IncrementalMaterialJob

    bound: set[int] = set()
    for raw in db.scalars(
        select(IncrementalMaterialJob.resolved_json).where(
            IncrementalMaterialJob.category_key == CATEGORY,
            IncrementalMaterialJob.status.in_(ACTIVE_MATERIAL_JOB_STATUSES),
        )
    ):
        try:
            resolved = json.loads(raw or "{}")
        except ValueError:
            continue
        for item in resolved.values() if isinstance(resolved, dict) else ():
            if isinstance(item, dict) and isinstance(item.get("asset_id"), int):
                bound.add(item["asset_id"])
    return bound


def _pinned(db: Session, ids: list[int]) -> dict[int, str]:
    """长期样本与仍在排队的旧任务：原图必须保住。返回 asset_id → 原因。"""
    from .models import (
        BaselineSetItem, DesignCaseSample, DimensionCalibrationItem, EvaluationJob,
        MaterialPackage, MaterialPackageItem, SampleSetItem,
    )

    reasons: dict[int, str] = {}
    for model, reason in (
        (BaselineSetItem, "baseline_set"),
        (SampleSetItem, "sample_set"),
        (DimensionCalibrationItem, "dimension_calibration"),
        (DesignCaseSample, "design_case"),
    ):
        for asset_id in db.scalars(select(model.asset_id).where(model.asset_id.in_(ids)).distinct()):
            reasons.setdefault(asset_id, reason)
    for asset_id in db.scalars(
        select(MaterialPackageItem.asset_id)
        .join(MaterialPackage, MaterialPackage.id == MaterialPackageItem.package_id)
        .where(MaterialPackageItem.asset_id.in_(ids), MaterialPackage.status.not_in(SETTLED_PACKAGE_STATUSES))
        .distinct()
    ):
        reasons.setdefault(asset_id, "material_package")
    for asset_id in db.scalars(
        select(EvaluationJob.asset_id)
        .where(EvaluationJob.asset_id.in_(ids), EvaluationJob.status.in_(ACTIVE_EVALUATION_JOB_STATUSES))
        .distinct()
    ):
        reasons.setdefault(asset_id, "active_evaluation_job")
    return reasons


def _runs_by_asset(db: Session, ids: list[int]) -> dict[int, list[tuple[str, datetime | None]]]:
    """素材 → 它的所有生产运行（进料事件直接触发的，以及以这些运行为源的子运行）的 (状态, 最近变动时间)。"""
    from .models import ContentIngressEvent, ContentRecord, IncrementalAdmissionBacklog, ProductionRun

    event_asset = dict(
        db.execute(
            select(ContentIngressEvent.id, ContentRecord.asset_id)
            .join(ContentRecord, ContentRecord.id == ContentIngressEvent.content_record_id)
            .where(ContentRecord.asset_id.in_(ids))
        ).all()
    )
    runs: dict[int, list[tuple[str, datetime | None]]] = {}
    if not event_asset:
        return runs
    # 进料事件还在准入积压里等待重放：运行还没建出来，原图要留着
    for event_id in db.scalars(
        select(IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.event_id).where(
            IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.event_id.in_(list(event_asset)),
            IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.status == "pending",
        )
    ):
        runs.setdefault(event_asset[event_id], []).append(("admission_pending", None))
    frontier = {
        run_id: event_asset[source_id]
        for run_id, source_id in db.execute(
            select(ProductionRun.id, ProductionRun.source_id).where(
                ProductionRun.source_type == "content_ingress_event",
                ProductionRun.source_id.in_(list(event_asset)),
            )
        ).all()
    }
    seen: dict[int, int] = {}
    while frontier:
        seen.update(frontier)
        rows = db.execute(
            select(ProductionRun.id, ProductionRun.status, ProductionRun.updated_at,
                   ProductionRun.finished_at, ProductionRun.created_at)
            .where(ProductionRun.id.in_(list(frontier)))
        ).all()
        for run_id, status, updated_at, finished_at, created_at in rows:
            latest = max((t for t in map(_naive_utc, (updated_at, finished_at, created_at)) if t), default=None)
            runs.setdefault(frontier[run_id], []).append((status, latest))
        children = db.execute(
            select(ProductionRun.id, ProductionRun.source_run_id).where(
                ProductionRun.source_run_id.in_(list(frontier))
            )
        ).all()
        frontier = {child: seen[parent] for child, parent in children if child not in seen}
    return runs


def _recent_model_use(db: Session, ids: list[int], cutoff: datetime) -> set[int]:
    from .models import WorkflowModelMediaUsage

    return set(
        db.scalars(
            select(WorkflowModelMediaUsage.asset_id)
            .where(WorkflowModelMediaUsage.asset_id.in_(ids))
            .group_by(WorkflowModelMediaUsage.asset_id)
            .having(func.max(WorkflowModelMediaUsage.created_at) >= cutoff)
        )
    )


def _file_sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as stream:
        while chunk := stream.read(1024 * 1024):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _in_scope(asset, *, cutoff: datetime) -> bool:
    """与 _candidates 同一口径（锁内复核时重看一次，素材行可能在预筛之后被改过）。"""
    stored = str(asset.stored_name or "")
    created = _naive_utc(asset.created_at)
    return (
        asset.category_key == CATEGORY
        and asset.storage_backend == "local"
        and (stored.startswith("material-") or "/material-" in stored)
        and created is not None
        and created < cutoff
    )


def _skip_reason(
    asset_id: int,
    *,
    pinned: dict[int, str],
    bound: set[int],
    runs: dict[int, list[tuple[str, datetime | None]]],
    recent: set[int],
    cutoff: datetime,
) -> str | None:
    """保留条件（批量预筛与锁内复核共用）；仍可删除时返回 None。"""
    asset_runs = runs.get(asset_id, [])
    if asset_id in pinned:
        return pinned[asset_id]
    if asset_id in bound:
        return "active_material_job"
    if not asset_runs:
        return "no_production_run"
    if any(status not in TERMINAL_RUN_STATUSES for status, _ in asset_runs):
        return "run_not_finished"
    if any(latest is None or latest >= cutoff for _, latest in asset_runs):
        return "run_recent"
    if asset_id in recent:
        return "model_used_recently"
    return None


def _lock_for_expiry(db: Session, asset_id: int):
    """取得删除这张原图所需的生命周期锁，返回锁内新鲜读到的素材行；锁不到返回 None。"""
    from .database_capabilities import DatabaseCapabilities
    from .models import Asset

    DatabaseCapabilities.for_dialect(db.get_bind().dialect.name).reserve_claim_transaction(db)
    return db.scalar(
        select(Asset)
        .where(Asset.id == asset_id)
        .with_for_update(skip_locked=True)
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )


def _recheck_locked(db: Session, asset, *, cutoff: datetime) -> str | None:
    """锁内复核：所有保留条件都用此刻的新鲜数据按单张重算一遍。"""
    from .models import AuditEvent
