    if db.scalar(select(AuditEvent.id).where(AuditEvent.event_key == event_key(asset.id)).limit(1)) is not None:
        return "already_expired"
    if not _in_scope(asset, cutoff=cutoff):
        return "out_of_scope"
    return _skip_reason(
        asset.id,
        pinned=_pinned(db, [asset.id]),
        bound=_bound_by_active_material_jobs(db),
        runs=_runs_by_asset(db, [asset.id]),
        recent=_recent_model_use(db, [asset.id], cutoff),
        cutoff=cutoff,
    )


def expire_originals(
    db: Session,
    *,
    upload_dir: Path,
    retention_days: int = DEFAULT_RETENTION_DAYS,
    limit: int = 5000,
    apply: bool = False,
    actor: str = "system:inspiration-original-retention",
    now: datetime | None = None,
    chunk: int = 500,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """扫描过了保留期的灵感图原图；apply=True 时删文件并逐张写审计事件（每张单独提交）。"""
    from .audit import append_audit_event

    if not 1 <= int(retention_days) <= 365:
        raise ValueError("retention_days must be between 1 and 365")
    now = _naive_utc(now or datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    cutoff = now - timedelta(days=int(retention_days))
    root = Path(upload_dir).resolve()
    receipt: dict[str, Any] = {
        "applied": apply, "retention_days": int(retention_days), "cutoff": platform_isoformat(cutoff),
        "scanned": 0, "eligible": 0, "expired": 0, "already_missing": 0, "freed_bytes": 0,
        "skipped": {}, "errors": [],
    }

    def skip(reason: str) -> None:
        receipt["skipped"][reason] = receipt["skipped"].get(reason, 0) + 1

    bound = _bound_by_active_material_jobs(db)
    after_id = 0
    while receipt["eligible"] < limit:
        batch = _candidates(db, cutoff=cutoff, after_id=after_id, chunk=chunk)
        if not batch:
            break
        after_id = batch[-1].id
        ids = [asset.id for asset in batch]
        pinned = _pinned(db, ids)
        runs = _runs_by_asset(db, ids)
        recent = _recent_model_use(db, ids, cutoff)
        for asset in batch:
            if receipt["eligible"] >= limit:
                break
            receipt["scanned"] += 1
            reason = _skip_reason(asset.id, pinned=pinned, bound=bound, runs=runs, recent=recent, cutoff=cutoff)
            if reason is not None:
                skip(reason); continue
            path = root / str(asset.stored_name)
            try:
                path.parent.resolve().relative_to(root)
            except ValueError:
                skip("unsafe_path"); continue
            if path.is_symlink() or path.parent.is_symlink() or ".." in Path(str(asset.stored_name)).parts:
                skip("unsafe_path"); continue
            if not apply:
                missing = not path.exists()
                size = 0
                if not missing:
                    if not path.is_file():
                        skip("not_regular_file"); continue
                    if _file_sha256(path) != asset.sha256:
                        skip("sha256_mismatch"); continue
                    size = path.stat().st_size
                receipt["eligible"] += 1
                receipt["freed_bytes"] += size
                continue
            asset_id = asset.id
            try:
                locked = _lock_for_expiry(db, asset_id)
            except OperationalError:
                # 只有取锁这一步的数据库错误（锁等待、SQLite 忙）算锁竞争；之后的失败如实记为错误（复审 N2）。
                db.rollback()
                skip("asset_locked"); continue
            if locked is None:
                db.rollback()
                skip("asset_locked"); continue
            stage = "recheck"
            try:
                reason = _recheck_locked(db, locked, cutoff=cutoff)
                if reason is not None:
                    db.rollback()
                    skip(reason); continue
                stage = "file_check"
                missing = not path.exists()
                size = 0
                if not missing:
                    if not path.is_file():
                        db.rollback()
                        skip("not_regular_file"); continue
                    if _file_sha256(path) != locked.sha256:
                        db.rollback()
                        skip("sha256_mismatch"); continue
                    size = path.stat().st_size
                receipt["eligible"] += 1
                # 仍在锁内：先删文件后记事件。记事件失败时下一轮会看到文件已不在，按 already_missing 补记；
                # 反过来先记事件则可能留下「已过期」却仍占盘、且再也不会被扫到的文件。
                stage = "unlink"
                if not missing:
                    path.unlink()
                stage = "audit"
                append_audit_event(
                    db, category="materials", action=ACTION, subject_type="asset", subject_id=asset_id,
                    actor=actor, event_key=event_key(asset_id),
                    payload={"stored_name": locked.stored_name, "sha256": locked.sha256, "size_bytes": size,
                             "file_missing_before": missing, "retention_days": int(retention_days),
                             "cutoff": platform_isoformat(cutoff), "record_retained": True,
                             "rechecked_under_lock": True},
                )
                stage = "commit"
                db.commit()
            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — 单张失败不拖垮整轮，回执里如实报告失败阶段
                db.rollback()
                receipt["errors"].append({"asset_id": asset_id, "error": type(exc).__name__, "stage": stage})
                continue
            if missing:
                receipt["already_missing"] += 1
            else:
                receipt["expired"] += 1
                receipt["freed_bytes"] += size
    if not apply:
        db.rollback()
    return receipt
